`.env.example`:

```example
# Copy to .env (cp .env.example .env). Values shown are the defaults.
# KAFKA_VERSION is the kafka-clients version Spring Boot resolves (./mvnw dependency:tree | grep kafka-clients)
KAFKA_VERSION=4.2.2
KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP=localhost:9092
TOPIC_RF=1
SIM_SEED=42
SIM_PLAYERS=1000
SIM_DURATION=5m
# exactly_once_v2 (default) or at_least_once, for the before-and-after integrity demo
PROCESSING_GUARANTEE=exactly_once_v2
STATE_DIR=/tmp/kafka-streams
SERVER_PORT=8082

# Arena (Project 1) integration. Off by default; Telemetry runs fully standalone without it.
# Containers reach Arena on the host at http://host.docker.internal:8080 (use localhost when running with java -jar).
ARENA_ENABLED=false
ARENA_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:8080
# Password of Arena's telemetry-sim service account (Arena's own env var of the same name). Required when ARENA_ENABLED=true.
TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD=
SIM_PLAYER_PASSWORD=sim-player-pass

```

`Dockerfile`:

```
# One file for all three apps: docker build --build-arg MODULE=telemetry-detector .
FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jdk AS build
WORKDIR /src
COPY . .
ARG MODULE
# The cache mount keeps downloaded dependencies between builds, so only the first build is slow.
RUN --mount=type=cache,target=/root/.m2 ./mvnw -q -B -pl ${MODULE} -am package -DskipTests

FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jre
ARG MODULE
WORKDIR /app
RUN useradd --system --uid 1001 app && mkdir -p /tmp/kafka-streams /app/reports /data \
    && chown -R app /tmp/kafka-streams /app/reports /data
COPY --from=build /src/${MODULE}/target/${MODULE}-*.jar app.jar
USER app
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75", "-jar", "app.jar"]

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Fairplay Telemetry

[![ci](https://github.com/ArghadeepMisra/gamer-simulation/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/ArghadeepMisra/gamer-simulation/actions/workflows/ci.yml)

Real-time cheat detection on Kafka Streams. A simulator streams about 25,000 gameplay events per second for 1,000 fake players (5% of them cheating) into Kafka. A three-instance Kafka Streams detector flags aimbots and speedhacks within seconds, and a verifier scores the flags against ground truth and proves that no event was lost or duplicated, even after one detector is killed mid-run.

Telemetry is Project 2 of **Fairplay**: Arena (Project 1) is the game platform, Telemetry produces the evidence, and the Judge (Project 3) reads the flags on `suspicious-players` and decides what to do about them. Every number in [Results](#results) was measured on the machine described there, including the ones that miss their targets.

## What it does

- **Detects cheating from raw events.** Two rules run over 5-second hopping windows that advance every second: an aimbot rule (fast reactions plus a high headshot rate) and a speedhack rule (too many movement samples over the speed limit).
- **Publishes flags to a topic.** Each flag on `suspicious-players` carries a score between 0.5 and 1.0 and the evidence behind it. A per-player, per-rule cooldown keeps one cheater from flooding the topic.
- **Proves exactly-once processing.** The topology counts every event's sequence number per player and publishes the totals (count, duplicates, gaps) to `integrity-stats`. The verifier compares them with the simulator's manifest of what it sent.
- **Survives bad input.** Malformed bytes, invalid events and events whose key does not match their player go to a dead-letter topic instead of crashing the stream.
- **Forwards finished matches to Arena (optional).** A Kafka listener posts each `match-summaries` record to Arena's `POST /api/v1/matches`, retrying temporary failures with exponential backoff and dead-lettering permanent ones. It is off by default.

## Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    SIM[Simulator] -->|match-events| DET
    SIM -->|player-profiles| DET
    SIM -->|match-summaries| BR[Arena bridge]
    BR -->|POST /api/v1/matches| ARENA[(Arena, Project 1)]
    BR -.->|rejected| SDLT[match-summaries.DLT]
    SIM -->|simulation-manifest, cheater-ground-truth| VER[Verifier]
    subgraph DET[Detector: Kafka Streams, 3 replicas]
        P[Parse and validate] --> I[Integrity tracker]
        P --> A[Aimbot window]
        P --> S[Speedhack window]
        A --> J[Join with profiles]
        S --> J
        J --> C[Cooldown]
    end
    P -.->|bad events| DLT[match-events.DLT]
    C -->|suspicious-players| OUT[Judge, Project 3]
    I -->|integrity-stats| VER
    OUT --> VER
```

The detector reads `match-events` as raw bytes and parses them inside the topology, so a corrupt record is routed to the dead-letter topic rather than killing the stream thread. The window branches re-parse the raw stream with `mapValues` instead of reusing the parse step's output. `KStream#process` marks its output as possibly key-changing, and Kafka Streams would then add a repartition topic per window and push every event through the broker a second time. A topology test and a broker test both guard against that.

Flags from both rules are merged and repartitioned by player, left-joined to the `player-profiles` table to add the username and skill tier, then de-duplicated by the cooldown before they are written out.

### Topics

Keys are player ids (strings) and values are UTF-8 JSON. Player-keyed topics have 6 partitions, so the join is co-partitioned.

| Topic | Partitions | Cleanup | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `match-events` | 6 | delete, 24 h | Shots, positions and kills from the simulator |
| `match-events.DLT` | 6 | delete, 7 d | Events the detector could not parse or validate |
| `player-profiles` | 6 | compact | Username and skill tier per player |
| `suspicious-players` | 6 | delete, 7 d | Detector output: one flag per rule hit (the contract with the Judge) |
| `integrity-stats` | 6 | compact | Per-player count, duplicates and gaps |
| `match-summaries` | 3 | delete, 7 d | Finished match results, shaped like Arena's request body |
| `match-summaries.DLT` | 3 | delete, 7 d | Summaries Arena rejected or that could not be parsed |
| `cheater-ground-truth` | 1 | compact | Who really cheats; read by the verifier only, never by the detector |
| `simulation-manifest` | 1 | compact | What the simulator sent, for the integrity check |

Consumers of `suspicious-players` and `integrity-stats` must read with `isolation.level=read_committed`, because the detector writes inside Kafka transactions.

### Detection rules

Boundaries are inclusive. The values below are the defaults in `telemetry-detector/src/main/resources/application.yml` and can all be overridden.

| Rule | Fires when, in one 5 s window |
| --- | --- |
| Aimbot | at least 12 hits, average reaction time at most 140 ms, and headshot rate at least 0.62 |
| Speedhack | at least 40 moving samples, and at least 15% of them over the speed limit of 7.5 |

A flag's score runs from 0.5 (just over the line) to 1.0 (far over it). The formulas are in `DetectionRules`.

## Quick start

You need Java 21 and Docker. The Maven wrapper downloads Maven itself. On Windows, use `.\mvnw.cmd` in PowerShell or `./mvnw` in Git Bash.

```bash
cp .env.example .env
docker compose up -d --build                             # broker, Kafka UI, 3 detectors, Prometheus, Grafana
docker compose --profile sim up -d telemetry-simulator   # starts the 5-minute run
docker compose logs -f telemetry-simulator               # wait for SIMULATION COMPLETE
docker compose --profile tools run --rm telemetry-verifier --mode=detection --min-recall=0.98 --max-fpr=0.01
docker compose --profile tools run --rm telemetry-verifier --mode=integrity --settle=60s
```

Reports are written to `reports/`. Player ids repeat for a given seed, so run `docker compose --profile sim --profile tools down -v` before every measured run.

- Kafka UI: <http://localhost:8090>
- Grafana (dashboard "Fairplay Telemetry", login admin/admin): <http://localhost:3000>
- Prometheus: <http://localhost:9090>

`scripts/measured-run.sh` does a whole measured run (fresh stack, simulation, a chaos kill about 90 s in, both verifier modes). `scripts/chaos-kill.sh` kills one detector replica, prints how long the survivors took to settle, and shows that all 6 `match-events` partitions are owned again.

To change the processing guarantee for the comparison run, set `PROCESSING_GUARANTEE=at_least_once` in `.env` before starting the stack. `docker compose down -v` does not remove services behind a profile, so always name them: `docker compose --profile sim --profile tools down -v`.

### Build and test

```bash
./mvnw verify      # all unit and integration tests, plus the 85% line-coverage gate per module (needs Docker)
./mvnw test        # unit tests only, no Docker needed
./mvnw -pl telemetry-detector -am test -Dtest=AimbotTopologyTest -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false
```

### Configuration

`.env.example` lists every setting with its default. The ones you are most likely to change:

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP` | `localhost:9092` | Broker address (containers use `kafka:19092`) |
| `SIM_SEED` | `42` | Seed for the population and each player's behavior |
| `SIM_PLAYERS` | `1000` | Simulated players (must be a multiple of match size times shard threads) |
| `SIM_DURATION` | `5m` | How long a run lasts (must exceed the 60 s latest cheat start) |
| `PROCESSING_GUARANTEE` | `exactly_once_v2` | `at_least_once` for the integrity comparison |
| `ARENA_ENABLED` | `false` | Register the players in Arena and forward finished matches to it |
| `TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD` | none | Password of Arena's `telemetry-sim` service account; startup fails if it is blank while Arena is enabled |

Invalid settings fail at startup with a message naming the property.

### Using Arena

With Arena (Project 1) running, set `ARENA_ENABLED=true`, `ARENA_BASE_URL` and `TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD`. The simulator registers its 1,000 players through Arena's API (on virtual threads, remembering the ids in `sim-players.json`) before the run starts, and uses Arena's ids for everything it sends. The detector's bridge then posts each finished match. Replays are safe because Arena treats the match's `externalId` as idempotent.

Once the Judge bans cheaters, Arena answers `422 PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE` for matches that include them, and those summaries land in `match-summaries.DLT`. That is expected behavior, not a bug.

## Design decisions

- **Parse inside the topology, not in a deserializer.** A deserialization exception handler can only skip or fail. Parsing in a processor lets one record be routed to `match-events.DLT` with its offset, Base64 payload and the reason, while the stream carries on.
- **5-second hopping windows, advancing every second.** A 5 s window is long enough to hold 12 or more shots and 40 or more movement samples, and a 1 s advance means a cheater is flagged within about a second of crossing the threshold. Wider windows would make detection slower, narrower ones noisier.
- **Per-player sequence numbers as the exactly-once proof.** Every event carries a per-player `seq` (1, 2, 3, ...) and the simulator records its final value. If the detector's count for a player equals that final value with zero duplicates and zero gaps, nothing was lost and nothing was processed twice. This is a stronger check than comparing totals.
- **Standby replicas and a 6-second session timeout.** `num.standby.replicas=1` keeps a warm copy of every state store on another instance, and `session.timeout.ms=6000` (the broker minimum) lets the group notice a killed instance in about 6 s instead of the default 45 s.
- **A cooldown store.** A hopping window keeps matching for as long as a cheater cheats, so the same player would be flagged every second. A store keyed by `playerId|rule` drops repeats for 10 minutes, and an aimbot flag never hides a speedhack flag.
- **Ground truth is kept away from the detector.** The simulator writes who really cheats to its own topic, which only the verifier reads. A topology test fails if the detector's topology ever lists that topic as a source, so the evaluation stays honest.

## Results

Measured on one machine, with one run per configuration. No result was tuned after the fact.

**Machine:** AMD Ryzen 5 3600 (6 cores, 12 threads), 16 GB RAM, Windows 11, Docker Desktop on WSL2 (VM with 12 CPUs and 7.7 GB). The broker, UI, Prometheus and Grafana ran with no memory cap; each detector replica and the simulator were capped at 1 GB. Everything ran on that one machine, including the load generator.

**Run:** 1,000 players, 5 minutes, seed 42, 3 detector replicas, one replica killed about 90 s in with `docker kill`.

### Benchmark table

| Claim | Target | Measured (`exactly_once_v2`) | Met? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Events per second | 25,000 | About 24,900/s produced, steady for the whole 5 minutes (24,556 to 25,204 in every 10 s window); about 25,000/s processed | yes, for the rate. **Lag did not stay flat** (see below) |
| `match-events` partitions, keyed by player id | 6 | 6 | yes |
| Median detection latency, obvious cheaters | under 5 s | `AIMBOT` 3.9 s, `SPEEDHACK` 0.9 s | yes |
| Cheaters caught (recall) | at least 98% | 100% (50 of 50) | yes |
| False-positive rate | under 1% | 0.11% (1 of 950 honest and pro players) | yes |
| Lost or duplicated events after killing 1 of 3 detectors | 0 and 0 | 0 and 0 (7,466,329 expected, 7,466,329 counted, all 1,000 players pass) | yes |
| Failover | under 10 s | 14.4 s (16.7 s and 14.5 s in the two at-least-once runs) | **no** |
| Test coverage | at least 85% per module | 100% common, 99.2% simulator, 100% detector, 100% verifier | yes |

### Detection

| Cheat type | Cheaters | Detected | p50 | p95 | max |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AIMBOT` | 20 | 20 | 3.9 s | 7.6 s | 10.9 s |
| `AIMBOT_SUBTLE` | 10 | 10 | 6.6 s | 21.4 s | 21.4 s |
| `SPEEDHACK` | 12 | 12 | 0.9 s | 2.5 s | 2.5 s |
| `SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE` | 8 | 8 | 2.0 s | 5.7 s | 5.7 s |
| **All** | 50 | 50 | 3.0 s | 17.6 s | 21.4 s |

Latency is the time from the moment a cheater's cheat starts to the first flag. All 50 flags used the correct rule, and no cheater was flagged before cheating. The one false positive is `sim_0815`, a PRO player (an honest but genuinely excellent one) flagged by the aimbot rule with a score of 0.538 (12 hits, headshot rate 0.667, average reaction 139 ms). That is exactly the kind of borderline case the Judge is meant to clear, and 1 of 20 pros is 5% while 0 of 930 ordinary players were flagged.

### Exactly-once, and the at-least-once comparison

Same run, same seed, same kill, only `PROCESSING_GUARANTEE` changed.

| | `exactly_once_v2` | `at_least_once` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Run id | `r-49fe8f` | `r-df576c` (and `r-8bfb5e`, see below) |
| Events expected (sum of `finalSeq`) | 7,466,329 | 7,465,795 (7,467,529) |
| Events counted | 7,466,329 | 7,465,795 (7,467,529) |
| Duplicates | 0 | 0 (0) |
| Gaps | 0 | 0 (0) |
| Players passing | 1,000 of 1,000 | 1,000 of 1,000 (1,000 of 1,000) |
| Failover | 14.4 s | 16.7 s (14.5 s) |
| Integrity exit code | 0 | 0 (0) |

**This comparison did not show what I expected.** The plan was that the at-least-once run would count duplicates after the kill, because a state-store write can reach the changelog before the matching offset commit, so a new owner would replay events the store had already seen. It showed none. I checked that the detectors really ran `at_least_once` (the logged Streams configuration says so). Because the spec says to try harder when that happens, I ran it a second time with the state-store cache switched off (`statestore.cache.max.bytes=0`, the figures in parentheses), which is the condition under which that window should be widest. It also showed 0 duplicates and 0 gaps.

So what these runs establish is narrower than the original plan: the exactly-once run lost and duplicated nothing across a mid-run kill, and the checker would have caught it otherwise (`ExactlyOnceIT` re-sends one event and expects exactly one duplicate). They do **not** show exactly-once beating at-least-once. I did not find out why at-least-once stayed clean. A likely reason is that a duplicate needs the instance to die inside the short gap between a changelog write and the offset commit, and `docker kill` in one run did not land there, but I have not verified that. One side effect did show up: with the cache off, the detector wrote every update to its changelog and fell behind, and the median detection latency rose from about 3 s to about 30 s.

### Throughput and the failover

- The simulator logged 24,556 to 25,204 events/s in every 10-second window for the whole run (7,466,329 acknowledged, 0 failed sends, 7,503 malformed records injected).
- Grafana and Prometheus over minutes 2 to 4 of the exactly-once run: events produced averaged 24,886/s and events processed 25,647/s. That window contains the failover, so the processed rate rises above the produced rate while the survivors catch up.
- **Lag did not stay flat, and the failover missed its target.** With one third of the consumers gone the survivors fell behind by about 14 s of input, and consumer lag (per-partition maximum) peaked near 380,000 events. Two instances have only about 5,000 events/s of headroom over the 25,000/s input here, so draining that backlog took roughly 2 minutes. Before the kill, the per-partition maximum lag sat between 15,000 and 20,000, above the roughly 12,500 the spec expects. The consumer group's lag was 0 once the run ended and the detectors caught up.
- The 14.4 s failover has a clear timeline from the survivors' logs: the group noticed the dead member about 5 s after the kill (the session timeout), then Kafka Streams ran three rebalances back to back (task migration is done in steps, and each step triggers a follow-up rebalance) before both survivors were `RUNNING` again. The 6 s session timeout is therefore not the only cost: expect roughly 5 to 6 s of detection plus 8 to 9 s of rebalancing in this setup. The consumer group showed all 6 `match-events` partitions owned by the two survivors afterwards.

## Tech stack

Java 21, Spring Boot 4.1.2, Apache Kafka and Kafka Streams 4.2.2 (KRaft, no ZooKeeper), Jackson 3, Micrometer with Prometheus output, Maven multi-module build, JUnit 5, Testcontainers, JaCoCo, Docker Compose, Prometheus and Grafana.

## Modules

| Module | What is in it |
| --- | --- |
| `telemetry-common` | Event and result records, the JSON serde, topic definitions, and a test-jar of shared helpers (fixed clock, event builders, Kafka test container, a stub Arena server) |
| `telemetry-simulator` | Seeded player population and cheater assignment, behavior profiles, event generation, match accounting, malformed-record injection, the shard workers and run loop, the Kafka publisher, the manifest, and Arena player registration |
| `telemetry-detector` | The Kafka Streams topology (plain Java, no Spring), the Spring Boot app around it (metrics, health indicator, runtime config), and the optional Arena bridge |
| `telemetry-verifier` | A one-shot command-line app: scores detection against ground truth, checks integrity totals against the manifest, writes Markdown reports and exits with a status code |

The simulator is seeded, so the same seed produces the same players, the same cheaters and the same behavior. Match boundaries follow the wall clock, so a whole run is not bit-for-bit repeatable.

## Testing

Tests come in three layers:

1. **Unit tests** for the pure logic: detection rules, window statistics, the event validator, property validation, the seeded simulator parts, the verifier's math.
2. **`TopologyTestDriver` tests** that run the whole detector topology in memory with a controllable clock. There is no Spring and no broker, so window, join and cooldown behavior can be tested at exact timestamps.
3. **Integration tests** (`*IT`) that start `apache/kafka-native` through Testcontainers: the simulator end to end, the detector end to end, the exactly-once check with a negative control, the verifier's topic reader, and the Arena bridge's retry and dead-letter behavior against a stub Arena.

Last full `./mvnw verify` (2026-11-19): **269 unit tests and 17 integration tests, all passing.** Line coverage is 100% in `telemetry-common`, 99.2% in `telemetry-simulator`, 100% in `telemetry-detector` and 100% in `telemetry-verifier` (the build fails below 85% per module). Coverage only says a line ran: for example every verifier unit test passed while the verifier could not start inside Spring, which is why `VerifierContextTest` now starts the whole application context.

## What I would do next

- **Avro with Schema Registry** for `MatchEvent`, with a demonstrated backward-compatible change.
- **Kafka 4.2's native Kafka Streams dead-letter queue support**, in place of routing bad records by hand.
- **Per-tier thresholds**, so a PRO player is judged against other pros and the one false positive above disappears.
- **A second broker with replication factor 3**, so a broker failure can be tested the way a detector failure is.
- **Faster failover.** The three back-to-back rebalances are the largest cost after the session timeout. Tuning warm-up and probing settings, or trying the new Kafka Streams rebalance protocol, are the first things to try.

```

`compose.yaml`:

```yaml
# Full stack: broker, Kafka UI, 3 detector replicas, Prometheus and Grafana.
# The simulator and the verifier sit behind profiles so each run is started deliberately:
#   docker compose --profile sim up -d telemetry-simulator
#   docker compose --profile tools run --rm telemetry-verifier --mode=detection
x-app: &app
  env_file: .env
  extra_hosts: ["host.docker.internal:host-gateway"]
  depends_on:
    kafka: { condition: service_healthy }

services:
  kafka:
    image: apache/kafka:${KAFKA_VERSION}
    ports: ["9092:9092"]
    environment:
      CLUSTER_ID: 4L6g3nShT-eMCtK--X86AQ
      KAFKA_NODE_ID: 1
      KAFKA_PROCESS_ROLES: broker,controller
      KAFKA_LISTENERS: INTERNAL://:19092,EXTERNAL://:9092,CONTROLLER://:9093
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: INTERNAL://kafka:19092,EXTERNAL://localhost:9092
      KAFKA_LISTENER_SECURITY_PROTOCOL_MAP: INTERNAL:PLAINTEXT,EXTERNAL:PLAINTEXT,CONTROLLER:PLAINTEXT
      KAFKA_INTER_BROKER_LISTENER_NAME: INTERNAL
      KAFKA_CONTROLLER_LISTENER_NAMES: CONTROLLER
      KAFKA_CONTROLLER_QUORUM_VOTERS: 1@kafka:9093
      KAFKA_OFFSETS_TOPIC_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1
      KAFKA_TRANSACTION_STATE_LOG_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1
      KAFKA_TRANSACTION_STATE_LOG_MIN_ISR: 1
      KAFKA_GROUP_INITIAL_REBALANCE_DELAY_MS: 0
      KAFKA_AUTO_CREATE_TOPICS_ENABLE: "false"
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "/opt/kafka/bin/kafka-broker-api-versions.sh --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 > /dev/null 2>&1"]
      interval: 5s
      retries: 20

  kafka-ui:
    image: ghcr.io/kafbat/kafka-ui:latest
    ports: ["8090:8080"]
    environment:
      KAFKA_CLUSTERS_0_NAME: fairplay
      KAFKA_CLUSTERS_0_BOOTSTRAPSERVERS: kafka:19092
    depends_on:
      kafka: { condition: service_healthy }

  telemetry-detector:
    <<: *app
    build: { context: ., args: { MODULE: telemetry-detector } }
    environment:
      KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP: kafka:19092
      SERVER_PORT: "8082"
    deploy:
      replicas: 3
    # DECISION: a memory cap per container keeps the stack inside Docker Desktop's VM on a 16 GB laptop. Without
    # one, each JVM sizes its heap from the whole VM (-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75) and three detectors starve the host.
    mem_limit: 1g

  telemetry-simulator:
    <<: *app
    profiles: ["sim"]
    build: { context: ., args: { MODULE: telemetry-simulator } }
    ports: ["8081:8081"]
    environment:
      KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP: kafka:19092
      # .env carries SERVER_PORT=8082 for the detectors, and an environment variable beats server.port in the YAML.
      SERVER_PORT: "8081"
      SIM_MAPPING_FILE: /data/sim-players.json
    volumes: ["./data:/data"]
    mem_limit: 1g

  telemetry-verifier:
    <<: *app
    profiles: ["tools"]
    build: { context: ., args: { MODULE: telemetry-verifier } }
    environment:
      KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP: kafka:19092
      REPORTS_DIR: /app/reports
    volumes: ["./reports:/app/reports"]

  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:latest
    ports: ["9090:9090"]
    volumes: ["./ops/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml:ro"]

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:latest
    ports: ["3000:3000"]
    volumes:
      - ./ops/grafana/provisioning:/etc/grafana/provisioning:ro
      - ./ops/grafana/dashboards:/var/lib/grafana/dashboards:ro

`mvnw`:

```
#!/bin/sh
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
# or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
# distributed with this work for additional information
# regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
# to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
# "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
# with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
# software distributed under the License is distributed on an
# "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
# KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
# specific language governing permissions and limitations
# under the License.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Apache Maven Wrapper startup batch script, version 3.3.4
#
# Optional ENV vars
# -----------------
#   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir, required when download maven via java source
#   MVNW_REPOURL - repo url base for downloading maven distribution
#   MVNW_USERNAME/MVNW_PASSWORD - user and password for downloading maven
#   MVNW_VERBOSE - true: enable verbose log; debug: trace the mvnw script; others: silence the output
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

set -euf
[ "${MVNW_VERBOSE-}" != debug ] || set -x

# OS specific support.
native_path() { printf %s\\n "$1"; }
case "$(uname)" in
CYGWIN* | MINGW*)
  [ -z "${JAVA_HOME-}" ] || JAVA_HOME="$(cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME")"
  native_path() { cygpath --path --windows "$1"; }
  ;;
esac

# set JAVACMD and JAVACCMD
set_java_home() {
  # For Cygwin and MinGW, ensure paths are in Unix format before anything is touched
  if [ -n "${JAVA_HOME-}" ]; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ]; then
      # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
      JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
      JAVACCMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/javac"
    else
      JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
      JAVACCMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/javac"

      if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] || [ ! -x "$JAVACCMD" ]; then
        echo "The JAVA_HOME environment variable is not defined correctly, so mvnw cannot run." >&2
        echo "JAVA_HOME is set to \"$JAVA_HOME\", but \"\$JAVA_HOME/bin/java\" or \"\$JAVA_HOME/bin/javac\" does not exist." >&2
        return 1
      fi
    fi
  else
    JAVACMD="$(
      'set' +e
      'unset' -f command 2>/dev/null
      'command' -v java
    )" || :
    JAVACCMD="$(
      'set' +e
      'unset' -f command 2>/dev/null
      'command' -v javac
    )" || :

    if [ ! -x "${JAVACMD-}" ] || [ ! -x "${JAVACCMD-}" ]; then
      echo "The java/javac command does not exist in PATH nor is JAVA_HOME set, so mvnw cannot run." >&2
      return 1
    fi
  fi
}

# hash string like Java String::hashCode
hash_string() {
  str="${1:-}" h=0
  while [ -n "$str" ]; do
    char="${str%"${str#?}"}"
    h=$(((h * 31 + $(LC_CTYPE=C printf %d "'$char")) % 4294967296))
    str="${str#?}"
  done
  printf %x\\n $h
}

verbose() { :; }
[ "${MVNW_VERBOSE-}" != true ] || verbose() { printf %s\\n "${1-}"; }

die() {
  printf %s\\n "$1" >&2
  exit 1
}

trim() {
  # MWRAPPER-139:
  #   Trims trailing and leading whitespace, carriage returns, tabs, and linefeeds.
  #   Needed for removing poorly interpreted newline sequences when running in more
  #   exotic environments such as mingw bash on Windows.
  printf "%s" "${1}" | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

scriptDir="$(dirname "$0")"
scriptName="$(basename "$0")"

# parse distributionUrl and optional distributionSha256Sum, requires .mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties
while IFS="=" read -r key value; do
  case "${key-}" in
  distributionUrl) distributionUrl=$(trim "${value-}") ;;
  distributionSha256Sum) distributionSha256Sum=$(trim "${value-}") ;;
  esac
done <"$scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties"
[ -n "${distributionUrl-}" ] || die "cannot read distributionUrl property in $scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties"

case "${distributionUrl##*/}" in
maven-mvnd-*bin.*)
  MVN_CMD=mvnd.sh _MVNW_REPO_PATTERN=/maven/mvnd/
  case "${PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE-}${PROCESSOR_ARCHITEW6432-}:$(uname -a)" in
  *AMD64:CYGWIN* | *AMD64:MINGW*) distributionPlatform=windows-amd64 ;;
  :Darwin*x86_64) distributionPlatform=darwin-amd64 ;;
  :Darwin*arm64) distributionPlatform=darwin-aarch64 ;;
  :Linux*x86_64*) distributionPlatform=linux-amd64 ;;
  *)
    echo "Cannot detect native platform for mvnd on $(uname)-$(uname -m), use pure java version" >&2
    distributionPlatform=linux-amd64
    ;;
  esac
  distributionUrl="${distributionUrl%-bin.*}-$distributionPlatform.zip"
  ;;
maven-mvnd-*) MVN_CMD=mvnd.sh _MVNW_REPO_PATTERN=/maven/mvnd/ ;;
*) MVN_CMD="mvn${scriptName#mvnw}" _MVNW_REPO_PATTERN=/org/apache/maven/ ;;
esac

# apply MVNW_REPOURL and calculate MAVEN_HOME
# maven home pattern: ~/.m2/wrapper/dists/{apache-maven-<version>,maven-mvnd-<version>-<platform>}/<hash>
[ -z "${MVNW_REPOURL-}" ] || distributionUrl="$MVNW_REPOURL$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN${distributionUrl#*"$_MVNW_REPO_PATTERN"}"
distributionUrlName="${distributionUrl##*/}"
distributionUrlNameMain="${distributionUrlName%.*}"
distributionUrlNameMain="${distributionUrlNameMain%-bin}"
MAVEN_USER_HOME="${MAVEN_USER_HOME:-${HOME}/.m2}"
MAVEN_HOME="${MAVEN_USER_HOME}/wrapper/dists/${distributionUrlNameMain-}/$(hash_string "$distributionUrl")"

exec_maven() {
  unset MVNW_VERBOSE MVNW_USERNAME MVNW_PASSWORD MVNW_REPOURL || :
  exec "$MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD" "$@" || die "cannot exec $MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD"
}

if [ -d "$MAVEN_HOME" ]; then
  verbose "found existing MAVEN_HOME at $MAVEN_HOME"
  exec_maven "$@"
fi

case "${distributionUrl-}" in
*?-bin.zip | *?maven-mvnd-?*-?*.zip) ;;
*) die "distributionUrl is not valid, must match *-bin.zip or maven-mvnd-*.zip, but found '${distributionUrl-}'" ;;
esac

# prepare tmp dir
if TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR="$(mktemp -d)" && [ -d "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" ]; then
  clean() { rm -rf -- "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR"; }
  trap clean HUP INT TERM EXIT
else
  die "cannot create temp dir"
fi

mkdir -p -- "${MAVEN_HOME%/*}"

# Download and Install Apache Maven
verbose "Couldn't find MAVEN_HOME, downloading and installing it ..."
verbose "Downloading from: $distributionUrl"
verbose "Downloading to: $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName"

# select .zip or .tar.gz
if ! command -v unzip >/dev/null; then
  distributionUrl="${distributionUrl%.zip}.tar.gz"
  distributionUrlName="${distributionUrl##*/}"
fi

# verbose opt
__MVNW_QUIET_WGET=--quiet __MVNW_QUIET_CURL=--silent __MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP=-q __MVNW_QUIET_TAR=''
[ "${MVNW_VERBOSE-}" != true ] || __MVNW_QUIET_WGET='' __MVNW_QUIET_CURL='' __MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP='' __MVNW_QUIET_TAR=v

# normalize http auth
case "${MVNW_PASSWORD:+has-password}" in
'') MVNW_USERNAME='' MVNW_PASSWORD='' ;;
has-password) [ -n "${MVNW_USERNAME-}" ] || MVNW_USERNAME='' MVNW_PASSWORD='' ;;
esac

if [ -z "${MVNW_USERNAME-}" ] && command -v wget >/dev/null; then
  verbose "Found wget ... using wget"
  wget ${__MVNW_QUIET_WGET:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_WGET"} "$distributionUrl" -O "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" || die "wget: Failed to fetch $distributionUrl"
elif [ -z "${MVNW_USERNAME-}" ] && command -v curl >/dev/null; then
  verbose "Found curl ... using curl"
  curl ${__MVNW_QUIET_CURL:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_CURL"} -f -L -o "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" "$distributionUrl" || die "curl: Failed to fetch $distributionUrl"
elif set_java_home; then
  verbose "Falling back to use Java to download"
  javaSource="$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/Downloader.java"
  targetZip="$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName"
  cat >"$javaSource" <<-END
	public class Downloader extends java.net.Authenticator
	{
	  protected java.net.PasswordAuthentication getPasswordAuthentication()
	  {
	    return new java.net.PasswordAuthentication( System.getenv( "MVNW_USERNAME" ), System.getenv( "MVNW_PASSWORD" ).toCharArray() );
	  }
	  public static void main( String[] args ) throws Exception
	  {
	    setDefault( new Downloader() );
	    java.nio.file.Files.copy( java.net.URI.create( args[0] ).toURL().openStream(), java.nio.file.Paths.get( args[1] ).toAbsolutePath().normalize() );
	  }
	}
	END
  # For Cygwin/MinGW, switch paths to Windows format before running javac and java
  verbose " - Compiling Downloader.java ..."
  "$(native_path "$JAVACCMD")" "$(native_path "$javaSource")" || die "Failed to compile Downloader.java"
  verbose " - Running Downloader.java ..."
  "$(native_path "$JAVACMD")" -cp "$(native_path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR")" Downloader "$distributionUrl" "$(native_path "$targetZip")"
fi

# If specified, validate the SHA-256 sum of the Maven distribution zip file
if [ -n "${distributionSha256Sum-}" ]; then
  distributionSha256Result=false
  if [ "$MVN_CMD" = mvnd.sh ]; then
    echo "Checksum validation is not supported for maven-mvnd." >&2
    echo "Please disable validation by removing 'distributionSha256Sum' from your maven-wrapper.properties." >&2
    exit 1
  elif command -v sha256sum >/dev/null; then
    if echo "$distributionSha256Sum  $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" | sha256sum -c - >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      distributionSha256Result=true
    fi
  elif command -v shasum >/dev/null; then
    if echo "$distributionSha256Sum  $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" | shasum -a 256 -c >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      distributionSha256Result=true
    fi
  else
    echo "Checksum validation was requested but neither 'sha256sum' or 'shasum' are available." >&2
    echo "Please install either command, or disable validation by removing 'distributionSha256Sum' from your maven-wrapper.properties." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  if [ $distributionSha256Result = false ]; then
    echo "Error: Failed to validate Maven distribution SHA-256, your Maven distribution might be compromised." >&2
    echo "If you updated your Maven version, you need to update the specified distributionSha256Sum property." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
fi

# unzip and move
if command -v unzip >/dev/null; then
  unzip ${__MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_UNZIP"} "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" -d "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" || die "failed to unzip"
else
  tar xzf${__MVNW_QUIET_TAR:+"$__MVNW_QUIET_TAR"} "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" -C "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" || die "failed to untar"
fi

# Find the actual extracted directory name (handles snapshots where filename != directory name)
actualDistributionDir=""

# First try the expected directory name (for regular distributions)
if [ -d "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlNameMain" ]; then
  if [ -f "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlNameMain/bin/$MVN_CMD" ]; then
    actualDistributionDir="$distributionUrlNameMain"
  fi
fi

# If not found, search for any directory with the Maven executable (for snapshots)
if [ -z "$actualDistributionDir" ]; then
  # enable globbing to iterate over items
  set +f
  for dir in "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR"/*; do
    if [ -d "$dir" ]; then
      if [ -f "$dir/bin/$MVN_CMD" ]; then
        actualDistributionDir="$(basename "$dir")"
        break
      fi
    fi
  done
  set -f
fi

if [ -z "$actualDistributionDir" ]; then
  verbose "Contents of $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR:"
  verbose "$(ls -la "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR")"
  die "Could not find Maven distribution directory in extracted archive"
fi

verbose "Found extracted Maven distribution directory: $actualDistributionDir"
printf %s\\n "$distributionUrl" >"$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$actualDistributionDir/mvnw.url"
mv -- "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$actualDistributionDir" "$MAVEN_HOME" || [ -d "$MAVEN_HOME" ] || die "fail to move MAVEN_HOME"

clean || :
exec_maven "$@"

```

`mvnw.cmd`:

```cmd
<# : batch portion
@REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
@REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
@REM distributed with this work for additional information
@REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
@REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
@REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
@REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
@REM
@REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
@REM
@REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
@REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
@REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
@REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
@REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
@REM under the License.
@REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

@REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
@REM Apache Maven Wrapper startup batch script, version 3.3.4
@REM
@REM Optional ENV vars
@REM   MVNW_REPOURL - repo url base for downloading maven distribution
@REM   MVNW_USERNAME/MVNW_PASSWORD - user and password for downloading maven
@REM   MVNW_VERBOSE - true: enable verbose log; others: silence the output
@REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

@IF "%__MVNW_ARG0_NAME__%"=="" (SET __MVNW_ARG0_NAME__=%~nx0)
@SET __MVNW_CMD__=
@SET __MVNW_ERROR__=
@SET __MVNW_PSMODULEP_SAVE=%PSModulePath%
@SET PSModulePath=
@FOR /F "usebackq tokens=1* delims==" %%A IN (`powershell -noprofile "& {$scriptDir='%~dp0'; $script='%__MVNW_ARG0_NAME__%'; icm -ScriptBlock ([Scriptblock]::Create((Get-Content -Raw '%~f0'))) -NoNewScope}"`) DO @(
  IF "%%A"=="MVN_CMD" (set __MVNW_CMD__=%%B) ELSE IF "%%B"=="" (echo %%A) ELSE (echo %%A=%%B)
)
@SET PSModulePath=%__MVNW_PSMODULEP_SAVE%
@SET __MVNW_PSMODULEP_SAVE=
@SET __MVNW_ARG0_NAME__=
@SET MVNW_USERNAME=
@SET MVNW_PASSWORD=
@IF NOT "%__MVNW_CMD__%"=="" ("%__MVNW_CMD__%" %*)
@echo Cannot start maven from wrapper >&2 && exit /b 1
@GOTO :EOF
: end batch / begin powershell #>

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
if ($env:MVNW_VERBOSE -eq "true") {
  $VerbosePreference = "Continue"
}

# calculate distributionUrl, requires .mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties
$distributionUrl = (Get-Content -Raw "$scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties" | ConvertFrom-StringData).distributionUrl
if (!$distributionUrl) {
  Write-Error "cannot read distributionUrl property in $scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties"
}

switch -wildcard -casesensitive ( $($distributionUrl -replace '^.*/','') ) {
  "maven-mvnd-*" {
    $USE_MVND = $true
    $distributionUrl = $distributionUrl -replace '-bin\.[^.]*$',"-windows-amd64.zip"
    $MVN_CMD = "mvnd.cmd"
    break
  }
  default {
    $USE_MVND = $false
    $MVN_CMD = $script -replace '^mvnw','mvn'
    break
  }
}

# apply MVNW_REPOURL and calculate MAVEN_HOME
# maven home pattern: ~/.m2/wrapper/dists/{apache-maven-<version>,maven-mvnd-<version>-<platform>}/<hash>
if ($env:MVNW_REPOURL) {
  $MVNW_REPO_PATTERN = if ($USE_MVND -eq $False) { "/org/apache/maven/" } else { "/maven/mvnd/" }
  $distributionUrl = "$env:MVNW_REPOURL$MVNW_REPO_PATTERN$($distributionUrl -replace "^.*$MVNW_REPO_PATTERN",'')"
}
$distributionUrlName = $distributionUrl -replace '^.*/',''
$distributionUrlNameMain = $distributionUrlName -replace '\.[^.]*$','' -replace '-bin$',''

$MAVEN_M2_PATH = "$HOME/.m2"
if ($env:MAVEN_USER_HOME) {
  $MAVEN_M2_PATH = "$env:MAVEN_USER_HOME"
}

if (-not (Test-Path -Path $MAVEN_M2_PATH)) {
    New-Item -Path $MAVEN_M2_PATH -ItemType Directory | Out-Null
}

$MAVEN_WRAPPER_DISTS = $null
if ((Get-Item $MAVEN_M2_PATH).Target[0] -eq $null) {
  $MAVEN_WRAPPER_DISTS = "$MAVEN_M2_PATH/wrapper/dists"
} else {
  $MAVEN_WRAPPER_DISTS = (Get-Item $MAVEN_M2_PATH).Target[0] + "/wrapper/dists"
}

$MAVEN_HOME_PARENT = "$MAVEN_WRAPPER_DISTS/$distributionUrlNameMain"
$MAVEN_HOME_NAME = ([System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create().ComputeHash([byte[]][char[]]$distributionUrl) | ForEach-Object {$_.ToString("x2")}) -join ''
$MAVEN_HOME = "$MAVEN_HOME_PARENT/$MAVEN_HOME_NAME"

if (Test-Path -Path "$MAVEN_HOME" -PathType Container) {
  Write-Verbose "found existing MAVEN_HOME at $MAVEN_HOME"
  Write-Output "MVN_CMD=$MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD"
  exit $?
}

if (! $distributionUrlNameMain -or ($distributionUrlName -eq $distributionUrlNameMain)) {
  Write-Error "distributionUrl is not valid, must end with *-bin.zip, but found $distributionUrl"
}

# prepare tmp dir
$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR_HOLDER = New-TemporaryFile
$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR = New-Item -Itemtype Directory -Path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR_HOLDER.dir"
$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR_HOLDER.Delete() | Out-Null
trap {
  if ($TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR.Exists) {
    try { Remove-Item $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR -Recurse -Force | Out-Null }
    catch { Write-Warning "Cannot remove $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" }
  }
}

New-Item -Itemtype Directory -Path "$MAVEN_HOME_PARENT" -Force | Out-Null

# Download and Install Apache Maven
Write-Verbose "Couldn't find MAVEN_HOME, downloading and installing it ..."
Write-Verbose "Downloading from: $distributionUrl"
Write-Verbose "Downloading to: $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName"

$webclient = New-Object System.Net.WebClient
if ($env:MVNW_USERNAME -and $env:MVNW_PASSWORD) {
  $webclient.Credentials = New-Object System.Net.NetworkCredential($env:MVNW_USERNAME, $env:MVNW_PASSWORD)
}
[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12
$webclient.DownloadFile($distributionUrl, "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName") | Out-Null

# If specified, validate the SHA-256 sum of the Maven distribution zip file
$distributionSha256Sum = (Get-Content -Raw "$scriptDir/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties" | ConvertFrom-StringData).distributionSha256Sum
if ($distributionSha256Sum) {
  if ($USE_MVND) {
    Write-Error "Checksum validation is not supported for maven-mvnd. `nPlease disable validation by removing 'distributionSha256Sum' from your maven-wrapper.properties."
  }
  Import-Module $PSHOME\Modules\Microsoft.PowerShell.Utility -Function Get-FileHash
  if ((Get-FileHash "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLower() -ne $distributionSha256Sum) {
    Write-Error "Error: Failed to validate Maven distribution SHA-256, your Maven distribution might be compromised. If you updated your Maven version, you need to update the specified distributionSha256Sum property."
  }
}

# unzip and move
Expand-Archive "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$distributionUrlName" -DestinationPath "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" | Out-Null

# Find the actual extracted directory name (handles snapshots where filename != directory name)
$actualDistributionDir = ""

# First try the expected directory name (for regular distributions)
$expectedPath = Join-Path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" "$distributionUrlNameMain"
$expectedMvnPath = Join-Path "$expectedPath" "bin/$MVN_CMD"
if ((Test-Path -Path $expectedPath -PathType Container) -and (Test-Path -Path $expectedMvnPath -PathType Leaf)) {
  $actualDistributionDir = $distributionUrlNameMain
}

# If not found, search for any directory with the Maven executable (for snapshots)
if (!$actualDistributionDir) {
  Get-ChildItem -Path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" -Directory | ForEach-Object {
    $testPath = Join-Path $_.FullName "bin/$MVN_CMD"
    if (Test-Path -Path $testPath -PathType Leaf) {
      $actualDistributionDir = $_.Name
    }
  }
}

if (!$actualDistributionDir) {
  Write-Error "Could not find Maven distribution directory in extracted archive"
}

Write-Verbose "Found extracted Maven distribution directory: $actualDistributionDir"
Rename-Item -Path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$actualDistributionDir" -NewName $MAVEN_HOME_NAME | Out-Null
try {
  Move-Item -Path "$TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR/$MAVEN_HOME_NAME" -Destination $MAVEN_HOME_PARENT | Out-Null
} catch {
  if (! (Test-Path -Path "$MAVEN_HOME" -PathType Container)) {
    Write-Error "fail to move MAVEN_HOME"
  }
} finally {
  try { Remove-Item $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR -Recurse -Force | Out-Null }
  catch { Write-Warning "Cannot remove $TMP_DOWNLOAD_DIR" }
}

Write-Output "MVN_CMD=$MAVEN_HOME/bin/$MVN_CMD"

```

scrape_configs:
  - job_name: simulator
    metrics_path: /actuator/prometheus
    scrape_interval: 5s
    static_configs:
      - targets: ["telemetry-simulator:8081"]
  # Compose runs 3 detector replicas without host ports, so Prometheus finds them by DNS: the service name
  # resolves to one A record per replica.
  - job_name: detector
    metrics_path: /actuator/prometheus
    scrape_interval: 5s
    dns_sd_configs:
      - names: ["telemetry-detector"]
        type: A
        port: 8082

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 https://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <parent>
        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-parent</artifactId>
        <version>4.1.2</version>
        <relativePath/>
    </parent>

    <groupId>dev.fairplay</groupId>
    <artifactId>telemetry</artifactId>
    <version>0.0.1-SNAPSHOT</version>
    <packaging>pom</packaging>
    <name>telemetry</name>
    <description>Fairplay Telemetry: real-time cheat detection on Kafka Streams</description>

    <modules>
        <module>telemetry-common</module>
        <module>telemetry-simulator</module>
        <module>telemetry-detector</module>
        <module>telemetry-verifier</module>
    </modules>

    <properties>
        <java.version>21</java.version>
        <!-- The parent has no code; each child sets jacoco.skip=false. -->
        <jacoco.skip>true</jacoco.skip>
        <!-- Minimum line coverage per module, enforced in verify. -->
        <jacoco.minimum>0.85</jacoco.minimum>
    </properties>

    <!-- Test dependencies shared by every module. -->
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
            <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-test</artifactId>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
            <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-kafka-test</artifactId>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>

            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>

            <artifactId>testcontainers-kafka</artifactId>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.awaitility</groupId>
            <artifactId>awaitility</artifactId>
            <scope>test</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>

    <build>
        <plugins>
            <!-- Boot's parent only manages failsafe; listing it here runs *IT classes in `verify`. -->
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                <artifactId>maven-failsafe-plugin</artifactId>
            </plugin>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.jacoco</groupId>
                <artifactId>jacoco-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>0.8.13</version>
                <configuration>
                    <excludes>
                        <exclude>**/*Application.class</exclude>
                    </excludes>
                </configuration>
                <executions>
                    <execution>
                        <id>prepare</id>
                        <goals><goal>prepare-agent</goal></goals>
                    </execution>
                    <execution>
                        <id>report</id>
                        <phase>verify</phase>
                        <goals><goal>report</goal></goals>
                    </execution>
                    <execution>
                        <id>check</id>
                        <phase>verify</phase>
                        <goals><goal>check</goal></goals>
                        <configuration>
                            <rules>
                                <rule>
                                    <element>BUNDLE</element>
                                    <limits>
                                        <limit>
                                            <counter>LINE</counter>
                                            <value>COVEREDRATIO</value>
                                            <minimum>${jacoco.minimum}</minimum>
                                        </limit>
                                    </limits>
                                </rule>
                            </rules>
                        </configuration>
                    </execution>
                </executions>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</project>

`scripts/chaos-kill.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Run this about 90 s into a run. It kills one detector replica, waits until both survivors log Kafka Streams'
# own "State transition from REBALANCING to RUNNING" line, and prints the elapsed time.
# Leave the killed replica down until the run ends: the point is to prove that 2 survivors lose nothing.
# Bring it back afterwards with: docker compose up -d
set -euo pipefail
# Git Bash on Windows rewrites paths such as /opt/kafka/... into C:/Program Files/Git/opt/...; this stops that.
export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1

victim=$(docker compose ps -q telemetry-detector | head -n 1)
if [ -z "$victim" ]; then
  echo "No running telemetry-detector replica found" >&2
  exit 1
fi
# The trailing Z makes docker read the time as UTC, not as the machine's local time.
since=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)
start_ms=$(date +%s%3N)
echo "Killing ${victim:0:12} at $(date -u +%H:%M:%S.%3N)"
docker kill "$victim" > /dev/null

while true; do
  running=$(docker compose logs --since "$since" telemetry-detector 2>/dev/null \
            | grep -c "State transition from REBALANCING to RUNNING" || true)
  if [ "$running" -ge 2 ]; then break; fi
  if [ $(( $(date +%s%3N) - start_ms )) -gt 60000 ]; then echo "Timed out after 60 s"; exit 1; fi
  sleep 0.2
done

echo "Failover complete in $(( $(date +%s%3N) - start_ms )) ms"
# The group must show all 6 match-events partitions with a consumer assigned.
docker compose exec -T kafka /opt/kafka/bin/kafka-consumer-groups.sh \
  --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 --describe --group telemetry-detector

```

`scripts/measured-run.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# One full measured run, start to finish (spec section 14, "Running one measured run"):
# fresh stack, 5-minute simulation, one detector killed about 90 s in, then both verifier modes.
#
#   scripts/measured-run.sh [label]
#
# The label (default: the processing guarantee) names the output files in reports/. Set PROCESSING_GUARANTEE in .env
# first: exactly_once_v2 (default) or at_least_once for the before-and-after demo. Needs Docker and a .env file.
set -euo pipefail
export MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1   # Git Bash on Windows would otherwise rewrite /opt/kafka/... paths

cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
guarantee=$(grep -E '^PROCESSING_GUARANTEE=' .env | cut -d= -f2 || true)
label="${1:-${guarantee:-exactly_once_v2}}"
mkdir -p reports

# The profiles are named so the simulator and verifier containers of an earlier run are removed too.
docker compose --profile sim --profile tools down -v
docker compose up -d --build

echo "Waiting for the 3 detectors to join the group"
until [ "$(docker compose logs telemetry-detector 2>/dev/null | grep -c 'State transition from REBALANCING to RUNNING')" -ge 3 ]; do sleep 3; done

docker compose --profile sim up -d --build telemetry-simulator
sim=$(docker compose ps -q telemetry-simulator)

echo "Simulation running; killing one detector after about 90 s"
until [ "$(docker logs "$sim" 2>&1 | grep -c 'events/s')" -ge 8 ]; do sleep 3; done
bash scripts/chaos-kill.sh | tee "reports/chaos-$label.txt"

until docker logs "$sim" 2>&1 | grep -q 'SIMULATION COMPLETE'; do sleep 5; done
docker logs "$sim" 2>&1 | grep -o '"message":"SIMULATION COMPLETE[^"]*"' | tee -a "reports/chaos-$label.txt"

echo "Waiting for the detectors to drain their lag"
for _ in $(seq 1 60); do
  lag=$(docker compose exec -T kafka /opt/kafka/bin/kafka-consumer-groups.sh --bootstrap-server localhost:9092 \
        --describe --group telemetry-detector 2>/dev/null | awk '$2=="match-events"{s+=$6} END{print s+0}')
  [ "$lag" -le 0 ] && break
  sleep 5
done

set +e
docker compose --profile tools run --rm telemetry-verifier --mode=detection --min-recall=0.98 --max-fpr=0.01
echo "detection exit code: $?" | tee -a "reports/chaos-$label.txt"
docker compose --profile tools run --rm telemetry-verifier --mode=integrity --settle=60s
echo "integrity exit code: $?" | tee -a "reports/chaos-$label.txt"

```

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/Topics.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common;

/** Topic names are shared with downstream consumers, Arena and the verifier, so renaming one breaks them. */
public final class Topics {

    public static final String MATCH_EVENTS = "match-events";
    public static final String MATCH_EVENTS_DLT = "match-events.DLT";
    public static final String PLAYER_PROFILES = "player-profiles";
    public static final String SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS = "suspicious-players";
    public static final String INTEGRITY_STATS = "integrity-stats";
    public static final String MATCH_SUMMARIES = "match-summaries";
    public static final String MATCH_SUMMARIES_DLT = "match-summaries.DLT";
    public static final String CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH = "cheater-ground-truth";
    public static final String SIMULATION_MANIFEST = "simulation-manifest";

    /** Key under which {@code RunInfo} is stored in {@link #SIMULATION_MANIFEST}. */
    public static final String RUN_KEY = "__run__";

    /** Partitions of every topic keyed by player id, so joined streams are co-partitioned. */
    public static final int PLAYER_PARTITIONS = 6;

    private Topics() {
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/TopicsConfig.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common;

import org.apache.kafka.clients.admin.NewTopic;
import org.apache.kafka.common.config.TopicConfig;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.TopicBuilder;

/** One {@link NewTopic} per topic this project uses. Spring's KafkaAdmin creates the missing ones at startup. */
@Configuration
@EnableConfigurationProperties(TopicsProperties.class)
public class TopicsConfig {

    static final String ONE_DAY_MS = "86400000";
    static final String SEVEN_DAYS_MS = "604800000";

    private final int replicationFactor;

    public TopicsConfig(TopicsProperties properties) {
        this.replicationFactor = properties.replicationFactor();
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic matchEventsTopic() {
        return deleteAfter(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, Topics.PLAYER_PARTITIONS, ONE_DAY_MS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic matchEventsDltTopic() {
        return deleteAfter(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, Topics.PLAYER_PARTITIONS, SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic playerProfilesTopic() {
        return compacted(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, Topics.PLAYER_PARTITIONS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic suspiciousPlayersTopic() {
        return deleteAfter(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, Topics.PLAYER_PARTITIONS, SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic integrityStatsTopic() {
        return compacted(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, Topics.PLAYER_PARTITIONS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic matchSummariesTopic() {
        return deleteAfter(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, 3, SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic matchSummariesDltTopic() {
        return deleteAfter(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES_DLT, 3, SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic cheaterGroundTruthTopic() {
        return compacted(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH, 1);
    }

    @Bean
    NewTopic simulationManifestTopic() {
        return compacted(Topics.SIMULATION_MANIFEST, 1);
    }

    private NewTopic deleteAfter(String name, int partitions, String retentionMs) {
        return TopicBuilder.name(name)
                .partitions(partitions)
                .replicas(replicationFactor)
                .config(TopicConfig.CLEANUP_POLICY_CONFIG, TopicConfig.CLEANUP_POLICY_DELETE)
                .config(TopicConfig.RETENTION_MS_CONFIG, retentionMs)
                .build();
    }

    private NewTopic compacted(String name, int partitions) {
        return TopicBuilder.name(name)
                .partitions(partitions)
                .replicas(replicationFactor)
                .compact()
                .build();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/TopicsProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.topics")
public record TopicsProperties(@DefaultValue("1") int replicationFactor) {

    public TopicsProperties {
        // Checked here instead of with @Positive so telemetry-common needs no validation starter.
        if (replicationFactor < 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.topics.replication-factor must be positive");
        }
    }
}

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/json/JsonSerde.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json;

import java.util.Map;

import org.apache.kafka.common.errors.SerializationException;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.Deserializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.Serde;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.Serializer;
import tools.jackson.databind.DeserializationFeature;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

/** Jackson 3 JSON serde for one record class. Used instead of Spring Kafka's Jackson 2 JsonSerde. */
public final class JsonSerde<T> implements Serde<T>, Serializer<T>, Deserializer<T> {

    public static final JsonMapper MAPPER = JsonMapper.builder()
            .disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)
            .build();

    private final Class<T> type;

    public JsonSerde(Class<T> type) {
        this.type = type;
    }

    @Override
    public byte[] serialize(String topic, T value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(value);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new SerializationException("Cannot serialize " + type.getSimpleName(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public T deserialize(String topic, byte[] bytes) {
        if (bytes == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return MAPPER.readValue(bytes, type);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new SerializationException("Cannot deserialize " + type.getSimpleName(), e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Serializer<T> serializer() {
        return this;
    }

    @Override
    public Deserializer<T> deserializer() {
        return this;
    }

    // Serde, Serializer and Deserializer each define default configure/close, so one must be chosen.
    @Override
    public void configure(Map<String, ?> configs, boolean isKey) {
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/json/JsonSerdes.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;

/** Factories for the serdes of the shared contract types. */
public final class JsonSerdes {

    private JsonSerdes() {
    }

    /**
     * Detector-only types (AimStats, SpeedStats, FlagCandidate) live in the detector module, which
     * cannot be referenced from here, so the detector builds its serdes with this generic factory.
     */
    public static <T> JsonSerde<T> of(Class<T> type) {
        return new JsonSerde<>(type);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<MatchEvent> matchEvent() {
        return of(MatchEvent.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<PlayerProfile> profile() {
        return of(PlayerProfile.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<SuspiciousPlayer> suspicious() {
        return of(SuspiciousPlayer.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<IntegrityStats> integrity() {
        return of(IntegrityStats.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<DeadLetter> deadLetter() {
        return of(DeadLetter.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<GroundTruth> groundTruth() {
        return of(GroundTruth.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<ManifestEntry> manifestEntry() {
        return of(ManifestEntry.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<RunInfo> runInfo() {
        return of(RunInfo.class);
    }

    public static JsonSerde<MatchSummary> matchSummary() {
        return of(MatchSummary.class);
    }
}

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;

@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record DeadLetter(
        String sourceTopic,
        int partition,
        long offset,
        String key,
        String payloadBase64,
        String error,
        long receivedAt) {

    public static final int MAX_ERROR_LENGTH = 500;

    public DeadLetter {
        if (error != null && error.length() > MAX_ERROR_LENGTH) {
            error = error.substring(0, MAX_ERROR_LENGTH);
        }
    }
}

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/model/Evidence.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;

/**
 * Why a flag fired. AIMBOT fills shots, hits, headshotHits, headshotRate and avgReactionMs.
 * SPEEDHACK fills movingSamples, overspeedSamples, overspeedRatio and maxSpeed. Unused fields are omitted.
 */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record Evidence(
        Integer shots,
        Integer hits,
        Integer headshotHits,
        Double headshotRate,
        Double avgReactionMs,
        Integer movingSamples,
        Integer overspeedSamples,
        Double overspeedRatio,
        Double maxSpeed) {

    public static Evidence aimbot(int shots, int hits, int headshotHits, double headshotRate, double avgReactionMs) {
        return new Evidence(shots, hits, headshotHits, headshotRate, avgReactionMs, null, null, null, null);
    }

    public static Evidence speedhack(int movingSamples, int overspeedSamples, double overspeedRatio, double maxSpeed) {
        return new Evidence(null, null, null, null, null, movingSamples, overspeedSamples, overspeedRatio, maxSpeed);
    }
}

`telemetry-common/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/model/MatchEvent.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model;

import java.util.UUID;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;

/**
 * One gameplay event. Fields not used by an event's type are null and omitted from the JSON.
 * Times are epoch milliseconds.
 */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record MatchEvent(
        String eventId,
        String runId,
        String playerId,
        String matchId,
        long seq,
        EventType type,
        long ts,
        Boolean hit,
        Boolean headshot,
        Integer reactionMs,
        Double dx,
        Double dy,
        Integer dtMs,
        String victimId) {

    /** False when a field required for the type is missing, seq &lt; 1, ts &lt;= 0 or playerId is not a UUID. */
    @JsonIgnore
    public boolean isValid() {
        if (type == null || seq < 1 || ts <= 0 || !isUuid(playerId)) {
            return false;
        }
        if (isBlank(eventId) || isBlank(runId) || isBlank(matchId)) {
            return false;
        }
        return switch (type) {
            case SHOT -> hit != null && headshot != null && reactionMs != null;
            // dtMs must be positive too, because speed = distance / dt would divide by zero.
            case POSITION -> dx != null && dy != null && dtMs != null && dtMs > 0;
            case KILL -> !isBlank(victimId) && headshot != null;
        };
    }

    private static boolean isBlank(String s) {
        return s == null || s.isBlank();
    }

    /** Strict check: {@link UUID#fromString} alone accepts shortened forms such as "1-1-1-1-1". */
    static boolean isUuid(String s) {
        if (s == null) {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            return UUID.fromString(s).toString().equalsIgnoreCase(s);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/TopicsConfigTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import org.apache.kafka.clients.admin.NewTopic;
import org.apache.kafka.common.config.TopicConfig;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.runner.ApplicationContextRunner;

class TopicsConfigTest {

    private final ApplicationContextRunner runner = new ApplicationContextRunner().withUserConfiguration(TopicsConfig.class);

    private static Map<String, NewTopic> topicsOf(org.springframework.context.ApplicationContext ctx) {
        return ctx.getBeansOfType(NewTopic.class).values().stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(NewTopic::name, t -> t));
    }

    @Test
    void declaresTheNineTopicsWithPartitionsAndCleanupPolicies() {
        runner.run(ctx -> {
            Map<String, NewTopic> topics = topicsOf(ctx);

            assertThat(topics).hasSize(9);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, 6, "delete", TopicsConfig.ONE_DAY_MS);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, 6, "delete", TopicsConfig.SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, 6, "compact", null);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, 6, "delete", TopicsConfig.SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, 6, "compact", null);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, 3, "delete", TopicsConfig.SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES_DLT, 3, "delete", TopicsConfig.SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH, 1, "compact", null);
            assertTopic(topics, Topics.SIMULATION_MANIFEST, 1, "compact", null);
        });
    }

    @Test
    void replicationFactorComesFromProperties() {
        runner.withPropertyValues("telemetry.topics.replication-factor=3").run(ctx ->
                assertThat(topicsOf(ctx).values()).allSatisfy(t -> assertThat(t.replicationFactor()).isEqualTo((short) 3)));
    }

    @Test
    void replicationFactorDefaultsToOne() {
        runner.run(ctx ->
                assertThat(topicsOf(ctx).values()).allSatisfy(t -> assertThat(t.replicationFactor()).isEqualTo((short) 1)));
    }

    @Test
    void nonPositiveReplicationFactorIsRejected() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new TopicsProperties(0)).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
        runner.withPropertyValues("telemetry.topics.replication-factor=0")
                .run(ctx -> assertThat(ctx).hasFailed());
    }

    private static void assertTopic(Map<String, NewTopic> topics, String name, int partitions, String cleanup,
                                    String retentionMs) {
        NewTopic topic = topics.get(name);
        assertThat(topic).as(name).isNotNull();
        assertThat(topic.numPartitions()).as(name + " partitions").isEqualTo(partitions);
        assertThat(topic.configs()).as(name + " cleanup").containsEntry(TopicConfig.CLEANUP_POLICY_CONFIG, cleanup);
        if (retentionMs != null) {
            assertThat(topic.configs()).as(name + " retention").containsEntry(TopicConfig.RETENTION_MS_CONFIG, retentionMs);
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/json/JsonSerdeTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ParticipantSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.apache.kafka.common.errors.SerializationException;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.Serde;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class JsonSerdeTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);

    private static <T> void assertRoundTrip(Serde<T> serde, T value) {
        byte[] bytes = serde.serializer().serialize("t", value);
        assertThat(serde.deserializer().deserialize("t", bytes)).isEqualTo(value);
    }

    @Test
    void roundTripsEveryEventType() {
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.matchEvent(), TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, true, false, 231));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.matchEvent(), TestEvents.position(P, 2, 1050, 0.25, -0.5));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.matchEvent(), TestEvents.kill(P, 3, 1100, TestEvents.player(2), true));
    }

    @Test
    void roundTripsEveryContractRecord() {
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.profile(), new PlayerProfile(P, "sim_0001", "IN", SkillTier.GOLD, 1763372000000L));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.groundTruth(),
                new GroundTruth("r-9f2c41", P, Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, 1763372151000L));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.groundTruth(), new GroundTruth("r-9f2c41", P, Segment.HONEST, CheatType.NONE, null));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.manifestEntry(), new ManifestEntry("r-9f2c41", P, 7342, 7342, 0));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.runInfo(), new RunInfo("r-9f2c41", 1763372100000L, 1763372400000L, 1000,
                7518234, 0, 7519));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.integrity(), new IntegrityStats(P, 7342, 7342, 0, 0, 1763372405000L));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.deadLetter(), new DeadLetter("match-events", 4, 918273, P, "eyJicm9rZW4i", "bad", 5L));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.matchSummary(), new MatchSummary("sim-r-9f2c41-000017", "FFA", "Rust Yard",
                "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z", "2026-11-17T10:02:00Z",
                List.of(new ParticipantSummary(P, 1, 14, 3, 6, 540, 170, 228))));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.suspicious(), new SuspiciousPlayer("7c1e", P, "sim_0042", SkillTier.GOLD,
                Rule.AIMBOT, 0.874, 1763372150000L, 1763372155000L, 1763372155412L,
                Evidence.aimbot(23, 17, 14, 0.8235, 61.4), "1.0.0"));
        assertRoundTrip(JsonSerdes.suspicious(), new SuspiciousPlayer("7c1f", P, null, null,
                Rule.SPEEDHACK, 0.6, 1L, 2L, 3L, Evidence.speedhack(50, 20, 0.4, 11.0), "1.0.0"));
    }

    @Test
    void omitsNullFieldsAndKeepsContractFieldNames() {
        String json = new String(JsonSerdes.suspicious().serializer().serialize("t",
                new SuspiciousPlayer("f", P, null, null, Rule.SPEEDHACK, 0.5, 1, 2, 3,
                        Evidence.speedhack(40, 6, 0.15, 8.0), "1.0.0")), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThat(json)
                .contains("\"flagId\"", "\"rule\":\"SPEEDHACK\"", "\"movingSamples\":40", "\"detectorVersion\"")
                .doesNotContain("username", "skillTier", "headshotRate");
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotSerializeIsValidAsAProperty() {
        String json = new String(TestEvents.bytes(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, true, true, 60)), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThat(json).doesNotContain("valid").doesNotContain("dx").doesNotContain("victimId");
    }

    @Test
    void nullValueSerializesToNullAndNullBytesDeserializeToNull() {
        assertThat(JsonSerdes.matchEvent().serializer().serialize("t", null)).isNull();
        assertThat(JsonSerdes.matchEvent().deserializer().deserialize("t", null)).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void garbageBytesThrowSerializationException() {
        var deserializer = JsonSerdes.matchEvent().deserializer();

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> deserializer.deserialize("t", "{\"broken\":".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)))
                .isInstanceOf(SerializationException.class)
                .hasCauseInstanceOf(RuntimeException.class);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> deserializer.deserialize("t", new byte[] {1, 2, 3, 4}))
                .isInstanceOf(SerializationException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void unknownFieldsAreIgnored() {
        byte[] json = ("{\"playerId\":\"" + P + "\",\"username\":\"u\",\"skillTier\":\"PRO\",\"registeredAt\":1,"
                + "\"somethingNew\":42}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        PlayerProfile profile = JsonSerdes.profile().deserializer().deserialize("t", json);

        assertThat(profile.username()).isEqualTo("u");
        assertThat(profile.skillTier()).isEqualTo(SkillTier.PRO);
    }

    @Test
    void unserializableValueThrowsSerializationException() {
        var serde = JsonSerdes.of(Unserializable.class);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> serde.serializer().serialize("t", new Unserializable()))
                .isInstanceOf(SerializationException.class);
    }

    /** Jackson wraps the exception thrown by this getter in a JacksonException. */
    public static class Unserializable {
        public String getBoom() {
            throw new IllegalStateException("boom");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void serdeIsItsOwnSerializerAndDeserializerAndConfigureIsANoOp() {
        var serde = JsonSerdes.matchEvent();

        assertThat(serde.serializer()).isSameAs(serde);
        assertThat(serde.deserializer()).isSameAs(serde);
        serde.configure(java.util.Map.of(), false);
        serde.close();
    }
}
