`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/model/MatchEventTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class MatchEventTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final String V = TestEvents.player(2);

    @Test
    void wellFormedEventsOfEveryTypeAreValid() {
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, true, true, 60).isValid()).isTrue();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, false, false, 60).isValid()).isTrue();
        assertThat(TestEvents.position(P, 1, 1000, 0, 0).isValid()).isTrue();
        assertThat(TestEvents.kill(P, 1, 1000, V, false).isValid()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void shotNeedsHitHeadshotAndReaction() {
        MatchEvent ok = TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, true, false, 60);

        assertThat(withShot(ok, null, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(withShot(ok, true, null, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(withShot(ok, true, false, null).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void positionNeedsDxDyAndPositiveDt() {
        MatchEvent ok = TestEvents.position(P, 1, 1000, 0.1, 0.2);

        assertThat(withPosition(ok, null, 0.2, 50).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(withPosition(ok, 0.1, null, 50).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(withPosition(ok, 0.1, 0.2, null).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(withPosition(ok, 0.1, 0.2, 0).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void killNeedsVictimAndHeadshot() {
        MatchEvent ok = TestEvents.kill(P, 1, 1000, V, true);

        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), ok.runId(), P, ok.matchId(), 1, EventType.KILL, 1000,
                null, true, null, null, null, null, null).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), ok.runId(), P, ok.matchId(), 1, EventType.KILL, 1000,
                null, true, null, null, null, null, " ").isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), ok.runId(), P, ok.matchId(), 1, EventType.KILL, 1000,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, V).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void seqBelowOneAndNonPositiveTimestampAreInvalid() {
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, 0, 1000, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, -3, 1000, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 0, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, -5, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void playerIdMustBeAUuid() {
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot("not-a-uuid", 1, 1000, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot("1-1-1-1-1", 1, 1000, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(TestEvents.shot(null, 1, 1000, true, false, 60).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void missingTypeAndMissingIdsAreInvalid() {
        MatchEvent ok = TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, true, false, 60);

        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), ok.runId(), P, ok.matchId(), 1, null, 1000,
                true, false, 60, null, null, null, null).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(new MatchEvent(null, ok.runId(), P, ok.matchId(), 1, EventType.SHOT, 1000,
                true, false, 60, null, null, null, null).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), "", P, ok.matchId(), 1, EventType.SHOT, 1000,
                true, false, 60, null, null, null, null).isValid()).isFalse();
        assertThat(new MatchEvent(ok.eventId(), ok.runId(), P, null, 1, EventType.SHOT, 1000,
                true, false, 60, null, null, null, null).isValid()).isFalse();
    }

    private static MatchEvent withShot(MatchEvent e, Boolean hit, Boolean headshot, Integer reaction) {
        return new MatchEvent(e.eventId(), e.runId(), e.playerId(), e.matchId(), e.seq(), e.type(), e.ts(),
                hit, headshot, reaction, null, null, null, null);
    }

    private static MatchEvent withPosition(MatchEvent e, Double dx, Double dy, Integer dtMs) {
        return new MatchEvent(e.eventId(), e.runId(), e.playerId(), e.matchId(), e.seq(), e.type(), e.ts(),
                null, null, null, dx, dy, dtMs, null);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/model/ModelHelpersTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ModelHelpersTest {

    @Test
    void deadLetterCapsTheErrorAt500Characters() {
        DeadLetter letter = new DeadLetter("match-events", 0, 1, "k", "e30=", "x".repeat(900), 1);

        assertThat(letter.error()).hasSize(500);
    }

    @Test
    void deadLetterKeepsShortAndNullErrors() {
        assertThat(new DeadLetter("t", 0, 1, "k", "e30=", "short", 1).error()).isEqualTo("short");
        assertThat(new DeadLetter("t", 0, 1, "k", "e30=", null, 1).error()).isNull();
        assertThat(new DeadLetter("t", 0, 1, "k", "e30=", "y".repeat(500), 1).error()).hasSize(500);
    }

    @Test
    void integrityStatsStartFromZeros() {
        IntegrityStats empty = IntegrityStats.empty("p");

        assertThat(empty).isEqualTo(new IntegrityStats("p", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void evidenceFactoriesFillOnlyTheirOwnFields() {
        Evidence aim = Evidence.aimbot(23, 17, 14, 0.8235, 61.4);
        Evidence speed = Evidence.speedhack(50, 20, 0.4, 11.0);

        assertThat(aim.shots()).isEqualTo(23);
        assertThat(aim.movingSamples()).isNull();
        assertThat(speed.maxSpeed()).isEqualTo(11.0);
        assertThat(speed.shots()).isNull();
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support;
```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/support/MutableClock.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;

/** A {@link Clock} whose instant tests can set and advance. */
public final class MutableClock extends Clock {

    private volatile Instant now;

    public MutableClock(Instant start) {
        this.now = start;
    }

    public void set(Instant instant) {
        this.now = instant;
    }

    public void advance(Duration duration) {
        this.now = now.plus(duration);
    }

    @Override
    public Instant instant() {
        return now;
    }

    @Override
    public ZoneId getZone() {
        return ZoneOffset.UTC;
    }

    @Override
    public Clock withZone(ZoneId zone) {
        return this;
    }
}
```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/support/StubArenaServer.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.function.Function;

import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;

/**
 * A stand-in for Arena (Project 1), built on the JDK's {@link HttpServer} on a free port so it needs no extra
 * dependency. Each route ({@code METHOD path}) answers from a scripted queue of responses, then from its default
 * handler, and every request is recorded.
 *
 * <p>{@code POST /api/v1/auth/login} answers 200 with a fresh token ({@code token-1}, {@code token-2}, ...) unless
 * scripted otherwise, so tests can count logins and see a re-login.
 */
public final class StubArenaServer implements AutoCloseable {

    /** One recorded call. {@code query} is null when the URL had none. */
    public record Request(String method, String path, String query, String authorization, String body) {
    }

    public record Response(int status, String body) {
        public static Response json(int status, String body) {
            return new Response(status, body);
        }

        public static Response problem(int status, String code, String detail) {
            return new Response(status, "{\"status\":" + status + ",\"code\":\"" + code + "\",\"detail\":\"" + detail + "\"}");
        }
    }

    private final HttpServer server;
    private final Map<String, Deque<Response>> scripted = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, Function<Request, Response>> handlers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final List<Request> requests = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final AtomicInteger tokens = new AtomicInteger();

    public StubArenaServer() {
        try {
            server = HttpServer.create(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 0), 0);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot start the stub Arena server", e);
        }
        on("POST", "/api/v1/auth/login", request ->
                Response.json(200, "{\"tokenType\":\"Bearer\",\"accessToken\":\"token-" + tokens.incrementAndGet() + "\"}"));
        server.createContext("/", this::handle);
        server.start();
    }

    public String baseUrl() {
        return "http://127.0.0.1:" + server.getAddress().getPort();
    }

    /** Sets what a route answers when its scripted queue is empty. */
    public StubArenaServer on(String method, String path, Function<Request, Response> handler) {
        handlers.put(method + " " + path, handler);
        return this;
    }

    public StubArenaServer on(String method, String path, Response response) {
        return on(method, path, request -> response);
    }

    /** Queues responses for a route; each request takes the next one before the default handler is used. */
    public StubArenaServer enqueue(String method, String path, Response... responses) {
        Deque<Response> queue = scripted.computeIfAbsent(method + " " + path, k -> new ArrayDeque<>());
        synchronized (queue) {
            queue.addAll(List.of(responses));
        }
        return this;
    }

    public List<Request> requests() {
        return List.copyOf(requests);
    }

    public List<Request> requests(String method, String path) {
        return requests.stream().filter(r -> r.method().equals(method) && r.path().equals(path)).toList();
    }

    /** Forgets recorded requests and scripted responses. Default handlers stay, and tokens keep counting up. */
    public void reset() {
        requests.clear();
        scripted.clear();
    }

    private void handle(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        String body = new String(exchange.getRequestBody().readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        Request request = new Request(exchange.getRequestMethod(), exchange.getRequestURI().getPath(),
                exchange.getRequestURI().getRawQuery(), exchange.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("Authorization"), body);
        requests.add(request);

        String route = request.method() + " " + request.path();
        Response response = null;
        Deque<Response> queue = scripted.get(route);
        if (queue != null) {
            synchronized (queue) {
                response = queue.poll();
            }
        }
        if (response == null) {
            Function<Request, Response> handler = handlers.get(route);
            response = handler == null ? Response.problem(404, "NOT_FOUND", "no stub for " + route) : handler.apply(request);
        }

        byte[] bytes = response.body().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().add("Content-Type", "application/json");
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(response.status(), bytes.length == 0 ? -1 : bytes.length);
        if (bytes.length > 0) {
            exchange.getResponseBody().write(bytes);
        }
        exchange.close();
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        server.stop(0);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-common/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/common/support/TestEvents.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;

/** Builders for valid {@link MatchEvent}s. Ids are deterministic so tests are repeatable. */
public final class TestEvents {

    public static final String RUN_ID = "r-test01";
    public static final String MATCH_ID = "sim-r-test01-000001";

    private TestEvents() {
    }

    /** A stable player id (a UUID string) for test player {@code n}. */
    public static String player(int n) {
        return UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes(("player-" + n).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString();
    }

    public static MatchEvent shot(String player, long seq, long ts, boolean hit, boolean headshot, int reactionMs) {
        return new MatchEvent(eventId(player, seq), RUN_ID, player, MATCH_ID, seq, EventType.SHOT, ts,
                hit, headshot, reactionMs, null, null, null, null);
    }

    public static MatchEvent position(String player, long seq, long ts, double dx, double dy) {
        return new MatchEvent(eventId(player, seq), RUN_ID, player, MATCH_ID, seq, EventType.POSITION, ts,
                null, null, null, dx, dy, 50, null);
    }

    public static MatchEvent kill(String player, long seq, long ts, String victim, boolean headshot) {
        return new MatchEvent(eventId(player, seq), RUN_ID, player, MATCH_ID, seq, EventType.KILL, ts,
                null, headshot, null, null, null, null, victim);
    }

    public static byte[] bytes(MatchEvent event) {
        return JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(event);
    }

    private static String eventId(String player, long seq) {
        return UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes((player + ":" + seq).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString();
    }
}
```

```xml
            <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-validation</artifactId>
        </dependency>

            <groupId>io.micrometer</groupId>
            <artifactId>micrometer-registry-prometheus</artifactId>
            <scope>runtime</scope>
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/DetectionProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import java.time.Duration;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;

/**
 * Detection tunables bound from {@code telemetry.detection.*}. Startup fails when a rule is broken.
 *
 * <p>Rules are checked in compact constructors, not with Bean Validation annotations, because
 * Hibernate Validator has no {@code @Positive} for {@link Duration} and the window rules compare two fields.
 */
@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.detection")
public record DetectionProperties(
        String detectorVersion,
        Duration cooldown,
        Duration integrityEmitInterval,
        Aimbot aimbot,
        Speedhack speedhack) {

    public DetectionProperties {
        if (detectorVersion == null || detectorVersion.isBlank()) {
            throw invalid("detector-version must not be blank");
        }
        positive(cooldown, "cooldown");
        positive(integrityEmitInterval, "integrity-emit-interval");
        if (aimbot == null || speedhack == null) {
            throw invalid("aimbot and speedhack settings are required");
        }
    }

    /** Aimbot rule: a hopping window over SHOT events. */
    public record Aimbot(
            Duration windowSize,
            Duration advance,
            Duration grace,
            int minHits,
            double maxAvgReactionMs,
            double minHeadshotRate) {

        public Aimbot {
            window(windowSize, advance, grace, "aimbot");
            positive(minHits, "aimbot.min-hits");
            positive(maxAvgReactionMs, "aimbot.max-avg-reaction-ms");
            ratio(minHeadshotRate, "aimbot.min-headshot-rate");
        }
    }

    /** Speedhack rule: a hopping window over POSITION events. */
    public record Speedhack(
            Duration windowSize,
            Duration advance,
            Duration grace,
            int minMovingSamples,
            double movingSpeedFloor,
            double speedLimit,
            double minOverspeedRatio) {

        public Speedhack {
            window(windowSize, advance, grace, "speedhack");
            positive(minMovingSamples, "speedhack.min-moving-samples");
            positive(movingSpeedFloor, "speedhack.moving-speed-floor");
            positive(speedLimit, "speedhack.speed-limit");
            ratio(minOverspeedRatio, "speedhack.min-overspeed-ratio");
        }
    }

    private static void window(Duration size, Duration advance, Duration grace, String rule) {
        positive(size, rule + ".window-size");
        positive(advance, rule + ".advance");
        positive(grace, rule + ".grace");
        // A hopping window cannot advance by more than its size, or Kafka Streams rejects the topology.
        if (advance.compareTo(size) > 0) {
            throw invalid(rule + ".advance must not be greater than " + rule + ".window-size");
        }
    }

    private static void positive(Duration value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isZero() || value.isNegative()) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be positive");
        }
    }

    private static void positive(double value, String name) {
        if (!(value > 0)) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be positive");
        }
    }

    private static void ratio(double value, String name) {
        if (!(value >= 0.0 && value <= 1.0)) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be between 0.0 and 1.0");
        }
    }

    private static IllegalArgumentException invalid(String message) {
        return new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.detection." + message);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import org.apache.kafka.streams.KafkaStreams;
import org.springframework.boot.health.contributor.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.health.contributor.HealthIndicator;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.StreamsBuilderFactoryBean;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * UP when the streams client is RUNNING, OUT_OF_SERVICE while it is CREATED or REBALANCING, DOWN otherwise. Before
 * the client has been created at all there is nothing to ask, which also counts as out of service.
 */
@Component
class KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator implements HealthIndicator {

    private final StreamsBuilderFactoryBean factoryBean;

    KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator(StreamsBuilderFactoryBean factoryBean) {
        this.factoryBean = factoryBean;
    }

    @Override
    public Health health() {
        KafkaStreams streams = factoryBean.getKafkaStreams();
        if (streams == null) {
            return Health.outOfService().withDetail("state", "NOT_CREATED").build();
        }
        KafkaStreams.State state = streams.state();
        Health.Builder builder = switch (state) {
            case RUNNING -> Health.up();
            case CREATED, REBALANCING -> Health.outOfService();
            default -> Health.down();
        };
        return builder.withDetail("state", state.name()).build();
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/MicrometerTopologyMetrics.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology.TopologyMetrics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Counter;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * The topology's counters. A retried transaction can count an event twice, so these are for the
 * dashboard only. Exact numbers come from the verifier.
 */
@Component
class MicrometerTopologyMetrics implements TopologyMetrics {

    private final Counter parsed;
    private final Counter deadLettered;
    private final Map<Rule, Counter> flags = new EnumMap<>(Rule.class);

    MicrometerTopologyMetrics(MeterRegistry registry) {
        this.parsed = registry.counter("telemetry.detect.events.parsed");
        this.deadLettered = registry.counter("telemetry.detect.events.dead_lettered");
        for (Rule rule : Rule.values()) {
            flags.put(rule, registry.counter("telemetry.detect.flags", "rule", rule.name()));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void parsed() {
        parsed.increment();
    }

    @Override
    public void deadLettered() {
        deadLettered.increment();
    }

    @Override
    public void flagged(Rule rule) {
        flags.get(rule).increment();
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/StreamsRuntimeConfig.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import org.apache.kafka.streams.errors.StreamsUncaughtExceptionHandler.StreamThreadExceptionResponse;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.StreamsBuilderFactoryBeanConfigurer;

/**
 * Runtime behavior of the streams client: a thread that dies is replaced instead of taking the instance down, and
 * every state change is logged (the timestamps are a second source for the failover time, next to chaos-kill.sh).
 */
@Configuration
class StreamsRuntimeConfig {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(StreamsRuntimeConfig.class);

    @Bean
    StreamsBuilderFactoryBeanConfigurer streamsRuntimeConfigurer() {
        return factoryBean -> {
            factoryBean.setStreamsUncaughtExceptionHandler(exception -> {
                log.error("Stream thread died, replacing it", exception);
                return StreamThreadExceptionResponse.REPLACE_THREAD;
            });
            factoryBean.setStateListener((newState, oldState) ->
                    log.info("Kafka Streams state transition from {} to {}", oldState, newState));
        };
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import java.time.Clock;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology.TelemetryTopology;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology.TopologyMetrics;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.StreamsBuilder;

/**
 * The only place Spring touches the topology: it hands the auto-configured {@link StreamsBuilder} to
 * {@link TelemetryTopology}. The streams client starts after all beans exist, so the topology is complete by then.
 */
@Component
class StreamsWiring {

    StreamsWiring(StreamsBuilder builder, DetectionProperties props, Clock clock, TopologyMetrics metrics) {
        new TelemetryTopology(props, clock, metrics).addTo(builder);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/ArenaBridgeListener.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.springframework.kafka.annotation.KafkaListener;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Forwards each finished match from {@code match-summaries} to Arena. Delivery is at-least-once, and Arena's
 * {@code externalId} idempotency (a replay returns 200 and changes nothing) makes the end result exactly-once.
 *
 * <p>The value is read as a {@code String} and parsed here, so one {@code KafkaTemplate<String, String>} can publish
 * dead letters for both bad JSON and Arena rejections. The container exists always but only starts when
 * {@code telemetry.arena.enabled=true}.
 */
@Component
class ArenaBridgeListener {

    private final ArenaMatchClient client;

    ArenaBridgeListener(ArenaMatchClient client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    // idIsGroup = false: by default the listener id would also become the consumer group and override the
    // group-id in the YAML (telemetry-arena-bridge).
    @KafkaListener(id = "arena-bridge", idIsGroup = false, topics = Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES,
            autoStartup = "${telemetry.arena.enabled:false}")
    void onSummary(ConsumerRecord<String, String> record) {
        MatchSummary summary;
        try {
            summary = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(record.value(), MatchSummary.class);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new NonRetryableArenaException("MALFORMED_SUMMARY", String.valueOf(e.getMessage()));
        }
        client.recordMatch(summary);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/ArenaMatchClient.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import java.time.Duration;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Counter;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.client.SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.client.ResourceAccessException;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;

/**
 * Posts one finished match to Arena's {@code POST /api/v1/matches}. It logs in as the service account lazily and
 * caches the access token.
 *
 * <pre>
 * 201                                  created, counted as outcome=created
 * 200                                  already stored (a replay), counted as outcome=replayed
 * 401                                  log in again and retry once; a second 401 is non-retryable
 * 429, 5xx, no connection, timeout     RetryableArenaException
 * 400, 404, 409, 422 (and other 4xx)   NonRetryableArenaException carrying Arena's code and detail
 * </pre>
 */
@Component
public class ArenaMatchClient {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ArenaMatchClient.class);

    private record Reply(int status, String body) {
    }

    private final ArenaProperties props;
    private final RestClient http;
    private final Counter created;
    private final Counter replayed;
    private volatile String token;

    public ArenaMatchClient(ArenaProperties props, MeterRegistry registry) {
        this.props = props;
        SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory factory = new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
        factory.setConnectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2));
        factory.setReadTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5));
        this.http = RestClient.builder().baseUrl(props.baseUrl()).requestFactory(factory).build();
        this.created = Counter.builder("telemetry.bridge.matches.posted").tag("outcome", "created").register(registry);
        this.replayed = Counter.builder("telemetry.bridge.matches.posted").tag("outcome", "replayed").register(registry);
    }

    public void recordMatch(MatchSummary summary) {
        String json = JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsString(summary);
        Reply reply = post(json);
        if (reply.status() == 401) {
            // The token expired or was never valid: log in again and retry once inside this same call.
            token = null;
            reply = post(json);
        }
        switch (reply.status()) {
            case 201 -> {
                created.increment();
                log.info("Arena stored match {}", summary.externalId());
            }
            case 200 -> {
                replayed.increment();
                log.info("Arena already had match {} (replay)", summary.externalId());
            }
            case 401 -> throw new NonRetryableArenaException("UNAUTHORIZED", "Arena rejected the service token twice");
            case 429 -> throw new RetryableArenaException("Arena answered 429 (rate limited)");
            default -> {
                if (reply.status() >= 500) {
                    throw new RetryableArenaException("Arena answered " + reply.status());
                }
                throw problem(reply);
            }
        }
    }

    private Reply post(String json) {
        try {
            String bearer = token != null ? token : login();
            return http.post().uri("/api/v1/matches").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                    .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + bearer).body(json)
                    .exchange((request, response) -> new Reply(response.getStatusCode().value(), body(response.bodyTo(String.class))));
        } catch (ResourceAccessException e) {
            throw new RetryableArenaException("Arena is unreachable: " + e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }

    private synchronized String login() {
        Reply reply = http.post().uri("/api/v1/auth/login").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .body(JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsString(
                        java.util.Map.of("username", props.serviceUsername(), "password", props.servicePassword())))
                .exchange((request, response) -> new Reply(response.getStatusCode().value(), body(response.bodyTo(String.class))));
        if (reply.status() == 200) {
            String accessToken = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readTree(reply.body()).path("accessToken").asString();
            if (accessToken == null || accessToken.isBlank()) {
                throw new NonRetryableArenaException("LOGIN_FAILED", "Arena login returned no accessToken");
            }
            token = accessToken;
            return accessToken;
        }
        if (reply.status() == 429 || reply.status() >= 500) {
            throw new RetryableArenaException("Arena login answered " + reply.status());
        }
        throw new NonRetryableArenaException("LOGIN_FAILED", "Arena login answered " + reply.status());
    }

    /** Arena's problem JSON has a {@code code} and a {@code detail}. Anything else is reported as the raw status. */
    private static NonRetryableArenaException problem(Reply reply) {
        String code = "HTTP_" + reply.status();
        String detail = reply.body().length() > 300 ? reply.body().substring(0, 300) : reply.body();
        try {
            JsonNode node = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readTree(reply.body());
            if (node.hasNonNull("code")) {
                code = node.path("code").asString();
            }
            if (node.hasNonNull("detail")) {
                detail = node.path("detail").asString();
            }
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            // Not JSON: keep the raw body as the detail.
        }
        return new NonRetryableArenaException(code, detail);
    }

    private static String body(String body) {
        return body == null ? "" : body;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/ArenaProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import java.time.Duration;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

/**
 * Settings of the Arena bridge, bound from {@code telemetry.arena.*}. The service password comes from
 * {@code TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD} and is never hard-coded; when Arena is enabled a blank one fails startup.
 *
 * <p>DECISION: the spec uses {@code ArenaProperties} without defining it. These are the keys of the detector YAML in
 * spec section 6. The simulator has its own record of the same name for its registration keys.
 */
@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.arena")
public record ArenaProperties(
        @DefaultValue("false") boolean enabled,
        @DefaultValue("http://localhost:8080") String baseUrl,
        @DefaultValue("telemetry-sim") String serviceUsername,
        @DefaultValue("") String servicePassword,
        @DefaultValue("3") int maxRetries,
        @DefaultValue("1s") Duration initialBackoff,
        @DefaultValue("2.0") double backoffMultiplier) {

    public ArenaProperties {
        if (enabled && (servicePassword == null || servicePassword.isBlank())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "telemetry.arena.service-password must not be blank when Arena is enabled: set TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD");
        }
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.base-url must not be blank");
        }
        if (maxRetries < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.max-retries must not be negative");
        }
        if (initialBackoff == null || initialBackoff.isZero() || initialBackoff.isNegative()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.initial-backoff must be positive");
        }
        if (!(backoffMultiplier >= 1.0)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.backoff-multiplier must be at least 1.0");
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/BridgeErrorHandlingConfig.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Counter;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import org.apache.kafka.common.TopicPartition;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.KafkaTemplate;
import org.springframework.kafka.listener.ConsumerRecordRecoverer;
import org.springframework.kafka.listener.DeadLetterPublishingRecoverer;
import org.springframework.kafka.listener.DefaultErrorHandler;
import org.springframework.kafka.support.ExponentialBackOffWithMaxRetries;

/**
 * Retries temporary Arena failures with exponential backoff and dead-letters permanent ones. Boot applies a single
 * {@code CommonErrorHandler} bean to its default listener container factory automatically. With the defaults a
 * failing record is tried 4 times (the first attempt plus 3 retries after 1 s, 2 s and 4 s), then dead-lettered.
 * The DLT has the same 3 partitions as the source, because the recoverer writes to the same partition number.
 */
@Configuration
class BridgeErrorHandlingConfig {

    @Bean
    DefaultErrorHandler bridgeErrorHandler(KafkaTemplate<String, String> template, ArenaProperties arena,
                                           MeterRegistry registry) {
        DeadLetterPublishingRecoverer dlt = new DeadLetterPublishingRecoverer(template,
                (rec, ex) -> new TopicPartition(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES_DLT, rec.partition()));
        Counter deadLettered = registry.counter("telemetry.bridge.dead_lettered");
        ConsumerRecordRecoverer counting = (rec, ex) -> {
            deadLettered.increment();
            dlt.accept(rec, ex);
        };

        ExponentialBackOffWithMaxRetries backOff = new ExponentialBackOffWithMaxRetries(arena.maxRetries());
        backOff.setInitialInterval(arena.initialBackoff().toMillis());
        backOff.setMultiplier(arena.backoffMultiplier());

        DefaultErrorHandler handler = new DefaultErrorHandler(counting, backOff);
        handler.addNotRetryableExceptions(NonRetryableArenaException.class);
        return handler;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/NonRetryableArenaException.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

/**
 * A permanent failure (400, 404, 409, 422, a second 401, or a summary that is not valid JSON). Trying again cannot
 * help, so the error handler sends the record straight to {@code match-summaries.DLT}. {@code code} is Arena's
 * problem code (for example {@code PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE}) or one of ours (for example {@code MALFORMED_SUMMARY}).
 */
public class NonRetryableArenaException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String code;

    public NonRetryableArenaException(String code, String detail) {
        super(code + ": " + detail);
        this.code = code;
    }

    public String code() {
        return code;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/RetryableArenaException.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

/** A temporary failure (429, 5xx, no connection, timeout). The error handler retries it with backoff, then dead-letters it. */
public class RetryableArenaException extends RuntimeException {

    public RetryableArenaException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public RetryableArenaException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/AimStats.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;

/** What one aimbot window has seen of a player's SHOT events. */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record AimStats(int shots, int hits, int headshotHits, long sumReactionMs) {

    public static AimStats empty() {
        return new AimStats(0, 0, 0, 0);
    }

    /** A new value with this SHOT event added: every shot counts toward the reaction time, hits and headshots only when set. */
    public AimStats add(MatchEvent shot) {
        return new AimStats(
                shots + 1,
                hits + (Boolean.TRUE.equals(shot.hit()) ? 1 : 0),
                headshotHits + (Boolean.TRUE.equals(shot.headshot()) ? 1 : 0),
                sumReactionMs + (shot.reactionMs() == null ? 0 : shot.reactionMs()));
    }

    /** Mean reaction time over all shots in the window, 0 when there are none. */
    @JsonIgnore
    public double avgReactionMs() {
        return shots == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) sumReactionMs / shots;
    }

    /** Headshot hits per hit, 0 when there are no hits. */
    @JsonIgnore
    public double headshotRate() {
        return hits == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) headshotHits / hits;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/CooldownProcessor.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Processor;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.ProcessorContext;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Record;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.KeyValueStore;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * Removes repeats. A hopping window keeps matching for as long as a cheater cheats, so the same player would be
 * flagged every second. The store remembers when each {@code playerId|rule} was last published, and a flag inside
 * the cooldown is dropped. "Now" is the injected {@link Clock}, not event time.
 */
public final class CooldownProcessor implements Processor<String, FlagCandidate, String, SuspiciousPlayer> {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CooldownProcessor.class);

    private final Duration cooldown;
    private final String detectorVersion;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final TopologyMetrics metrics;
    private ProcessorContext<String, SuspiciousPlayer> context;
    private KeyValueStore<String, Long> store;

    public CooldownProcessor(Duration cooldown, String detectorVersion, Clock clock, TopologyMetrics metrics) {
        this.cooldown = cooldown;
        this.detectorVersion = detectorVersion;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.metrics = metrics;
    }

    @Override
    public void init(ProcessorContext<String, SuspiciousPlayer> context) {
        this.context = context;
        this.store = context.getStateStore(TelemetryTopology.COOLDOWN_STORE);
    }

    @Override
    public void process(Record<String, FlagCandidate> record) {
        FlagCandidate candidate = record.value();
        String storeKey = candidate.playerId() + "|" + candidate.rule();
        long now = clock.millis();
        Long lastEmitted = store.get(storeKey);
        if (lastEmitted != null && now - lastEmitted < cooldown.toMillis()) {
            return;
        }
        store.put(storeKey, now);
        metrics.flagged(candidate.rule());
        log.info("Flag player={} rule={} score={}", candidate.playerId(), candidate.rule(), candidate.score());
        SuspiciousPlayer flag = new SuspiciousPlayer(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                candidate.playerId(),
                candidate.username(),
                candidate.skillTier(),
                candidate.rule(),
                candidate.score(),
                candidate.windowStart(),
                candidate.windowEnd(),
                now,
                candidate.evidence(),
                detectorVersion);
        context.forward(record.withValue(flag));
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/DetectionRules.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.DetectionProperties;

/**
 * Pure threshold checks and score formulas. Boundaries are inclusive, using
 * {@code >=} and {@code <=}. A score of 0.5 means just over the line and 1.0 means far over it.
 */
public final class DetectionRules {

    // The "far over the line" ends of the score ranges. They are part of the formula, not tunables.
    static final double AIMBOT_TOP_HEADSHOT_RATE = 0.95;
    static final double AIMBOT_BEST_REACTION_MS = 30.0;
    static final double SPEEDHACK_TOP_RATIO = 1.0;

    private final DetectionProperties.Aimbot aim;
    private final DetectionProperties.Speedhack speed;

    public DetectionRules(DetectionProperties props) {
        this.aim = props.aimbot();
        this.speed = props.speedhack();
    }

    public boolean aimbotTriggered(AimStats s) {
        return s.hits() >= aim.minHits()
                && s.avgReactionMs() <= aim.maxAvgReactionMs()
                && s.headshotRate() >= aim.minHeadshotRate();
    }

    /** {@code 0.5 + 0.25 x c((rate - min) / (0.95 - min)) + 0.25 x c((maxReaction - avg) / (maxReaction - 30))}. */
    public double aimbotScore(AimStats s) {
        double rateTerm = clamp((s.headshotRate() - aim.minHeadshotRate())
                / (AIMBOT_TOP_HEADSHOT_RATE - aim.minHeadshotRate()));
        double reactionTerm = clamp((aim.maxAvgReactionMs() - s.avgReactionMs())
                / (aim.maxAvgReactionMs() - AIMBOT_BEST_REACTION_MS));
        return round(0.5 + 0.25 * rateTerm + 0.25 * reactionTerm, 3);
    }

    public Evidence aimbotEvidence(AimStats s) {
        return Evidence.aimbot(s.shots(), s.hits(), s.headshotHits(), round(s.headshotRate(), 4),
                round(s.avgReactionMs(), 4));
    }

    public boolean speedhackTriggered(SpeedStats s) {
        return s.movingSamples() >= speed.minMovingSamples() && s.overspeedRatio() >= speed.minOverspeedRatio();
    }

    /** {@code 0.5 + 0.5 x c((ratio - min) / (1 - min))}. */
    public double speedhackScore(SpeedStats s) {
        double ratioTerm = clamp((s.overspeedRatio() - speed.minOverspeedRatio())
                / (SPEEDHACK_TOP_RATIO - speed.minOverspeedRatio()));
        return round(0.5 + 0.5 * ratioTerm, 3);
    }

    public Evidence speedhackEvidence(SpeedStats s) {
        return Evidence.speedhack(s.movingSamples(), s.overspeedSamples(), round(s.overspeedRatio(), 4),
                round(s.maxSpeed(), 4));
    }

    /** The detector's own movement-speed settings, for the window aggregator. */
    public double movingSpeedFloor() {
        return speed.movingSpeedFloor();
    }

    public double speedLimit() {
        return speed.speedLimit();
    }

    static double clamp(double x) {
        return Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, x));
    }

    /** HALF_UP, as the {@code suspicious-players} contract requires. */
    static double round(double value, int decimals) {
        return BigDecimal.valueOf(value).setScale(decimals, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/EventParser.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;

/**
 * The single definition of "a good record": parseable JSON, a valid event, and a record key equal to the event's
 * player id. {@link ParseProcessor} uses it to decide what goes to the dead-letter topic, and the window branches
 * of the topology use it to decide what to count, so the two can never disagree.
 */
final class EventParser {

    /** Exactly one of {@code event} and {@code problem} is non-null. */
    record Outcome(MatchEvent event, String problem) {
    }

    private EventParser() {
    }

    static Outcome parse(String key, byte[] bytes) {
        if (bytes == null) {
            return new Outcome(null, "null payload");
        }
        try {
            MatchEvent event = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(bytes, MatchEvent.class);
            if (event == null) {
                return new Outcome(null, "payload is JSON null");
            }
            if (!event.isValid()) {
                return new Outcome(null, "invalid event: a required field is missing or malformed");
            }
            if (!event.playerId().equals(key)) {
                return new Outcome(null, "key mismatch: record key '" + key + "' is not playerId '"
                        + event.playerId() + "'");
            }
            return new Outcome(event, null);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return new Outcome(null, e.getMessage() != null ? e.getMessage() : e.getClass().getSimpleName());
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/FlagCandidate.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Window;

/**
 * A window that crossed a threshold, before it is enriched with the player's profile and de-duplicated. It travels
 * through the internal {@code flags} repartition topic, so it must stay serializable.
 */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record FlagCandidate(
        String playerId,
        Rule rule,
        double score,
        long windowStart,
        long windowEnd,
        Evidence evidence,
        String username,
        SkillTier skillTier) {

    public static FlagCandidate aimbot(String playerId, Window window, AimStats stats, DetectionRules rules) {
        return new FlagCandidate(playerId, Rule.AIMBOT, rules.aimbotScore(stats), window.start(), window.end(),
                rules.aimbotEvidence(stats), null, null);
    }

    public static FlagCandidate speedhack(String playerId, Window window, SpeedStats stats, DetectionRules rules) {
        return new FlagCandidate(playerId, Rule.SPEEDHACK, rules.speedhackScore(stats), window.start(), window.end(),
                rules.speedhackEvidence(stats), null, null);
    }

    /** The value joiner for the left join with player-profiles. {@code profile} is null when none was found. */
    public FlagCandidate withProfile(PlayerProfile profile) {
        if (profile == null) {
            return this;
        }
        return new FlagCandidate(playerId, rule, score, windowStart, windowEnd, evidence, profile.username(),
                profile.skillTier());
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/IntegrityProcessor.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KeyValue;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.PunctuationType;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Processor;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.ProcessorContext;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Record;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.KeyValueIterator;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.KeyValueStore;

/**
 * Proves exactly-once. Each player's events carry a gapless {@code seq}, so comparing it with the last seen value
 * counts what arrived in order, what arrived twice and what never arrived. The counts live in a state store and
 * are re-emitted for <b>every</b> player on a wall-clock interval, so the verifier only ever needs the latest value
 * per key and a task restored after a failover needs no memory of what changed.
 */
public final class IntegrityProcessor implements Processor<String, MatchEvent, String, IntegrityStats> {

    private final Duration emitInterval;
    private final Clock clock;
    private ProcessorContext<String, IntegrityStats> context;
    private KeyValueStore<String, IntegrityStats> store;

    public IntegrityProcessor(Duration emitInterval, Clock clock) {
        this.emitInterval = emitInterval;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public void init(ProcessorContext<String, IntegrityStats> context) {
        this.context = context;
        this.store = context.getStateStore(TelemetryTopology.SEQ_STORE);
        context.schedule(emitInterval, PunctuationType.WALL_CLOCK_TIME, timestamp -> emitAll());
    }

    @Override
    public void process(Record<String, MatchEvent> record) {
        MatchEvent event = record.value();
        IntegrityStats current = store.get(event.playerId());
        if (current == null) {
            current = IntegrityStats.empty(event.playerId());
        }
        // Nothing is forwarded here: stats go out from the punctuator.
        store.put(event.playerId(), apply(current, event.seq()));
    }

    /** The three cases: next in order, seen before, or something missing. */
    static IntegrityStats apply(IntegrityStats s, long seq) {
        if (seq == s.lastSeq() + 1) {
            return new IntegrityStats(s.playerId(), s.count() + 1, seq, s.duplicates(), s.gaps(), s.updatedAt());
        }
        if (seq <= s.lastSeq()) {
            return new IntegrityStats(s.playerId(), s.count(), s.lastSeq(), s.duplicates() + 1, s.gaps(),
                    s.updatedAt());
        }
        return new IntegrityStats(s.playerId(), s.count() + 1, seq, s.duplicates(),
                s.gaps() + (seq - s.lastSeq() - 1), s.updatedAt());
    }

    private void emitAll() {
        long now = clock.millis();
        try (KeyValueIterator<String, IntegrityStats> all = store.all()) {
            while (all.hasNext()) {
                KeyValue<String, IntegrityStats> entry = all.next();
                IntegrityStats s = entry.value;
                IntegrityStats stamped = new IntegrityStats(s.playerId(), s.count(), s.lastSeq(), s.duplicates(),
                        s.gaps(), now);
                context.forward(new Record<>(entry.key, stamped, now));
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/ParseProcessor.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Optional;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Processor;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.ProcessorContext;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.Record;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.processor.api.RecordMetadata;

/**
 * Parses the raw bytes of {@code match-events} and keeps the key. Bad records are never dropped silently: null bytes,
 * unparseable JSON, an invalid event or a key that differs from the event's player id all become
 * {@link ParseResult.Failed}, which the topology routes to {@code match-events.DLT}.
 */
public final class ParseProcessor implements Processor<String, byte[], String, ParseResult> {

    private final Clock clock;
    private final TopologyMetrics metrics;
    private ProcessorContext<String, ParseResult> context;

    public ParseProcessor(Clock clock, TopologyMetrics metrics) {
        this.clock = clock;
        this.metrics = metrics;
    }

    @Override
    public void init(ProcessorContext<String, ParseResult> context) {
        this.context = context;
    }

    @Override
    public void process(Record<String, byte[]> record) {
        EventParser.Outcome outcome = EventParser.parse(record.key(), record.value());
        if (outcome.problem() == null) {
            metrics.parsed();
            context.forward(record.withValue(new ParseResult.Parsed(outcome.event())));
        } else {
            metrics.deadLettered();
            context.forward(record.withValue(new ParseResult.Failed(deadLetter(record, outcome.problem()))));
        }
    }

    private DeadLetter deadLetter(Record<String, byte[]> record, String problem) {
        Optional<RecordMetadata> metadata = context.recordMetadata();
        String payload = record.value() == null ? null : Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(record.value());
        return new DeadLetter(
                metadata.map(RecordMetadata::topic).orElse("unknown"),
                metadata.map(RecordMetadata::partition).orElse(-1),
                metadata.map(RecordMetadata::offset).orElse(-1L),
                record.key(),
                payload,
                problem,
                clock.millis());
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/ParseResult.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;

/** The outcome of parsing one raw record: a valid event, or the dead letter to publish for it. */
public sealed interface ParseResult permits ParseResult.Parsed, ParseResult.Failed {

    record Parsed(MatchEvent event) implements ParseResult {
    }

    record Failed(DeadLetter deadLetter) implements ParseResult {
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/SpeedStats.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnore;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;

/** What one speedhack window has seen of a player's POSITION events. */
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record SpeedStats(int movingSamples, int overspeedSamples, double maxSpeed) {

    public static SpeedStats empty() {
        return new SpeedStats(0, 0, 0.0);
    }

    /**
     * A new value with this POSITION event added. {@code speed = sqrt(dx^2 + dy^2) / (dtMs / 1000)}. A sample is
     * moving when {@code speed >= movingSpeedFloor}, and overspeed when it is moving and {@code speed > speedLimit}.
     * Idle samples count toward nothing.
     */
    public SpeedStats add(MatchEvent position, double movingSpeedFloor, double speedLimit) {
        double speed = speedOf(position);
        boolean moving = speed >= movingSpeedFloor;
        return new SpeedStats(
                movingSamples + (moving ? 1 : 0),
                overspeedSamples + (moving && speed > speedLimit ? 1 : 0),
                Math.max(maxSpeed, speed));
    }

    static double speedOf(MatchEvent position) {
        return Math.hypot(position.dx(), position.dy()) / (position.dtMs() / 1000.0);
    }

    /** Overspeed samples per moving sample, 0 when nothing moved. */
    @JsonIgnore
    public double overspeedRatio() {
        return movingSamples == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) overspeedSamples / movingSamples;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/TelemetryTopology.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerdes;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.DetectionProperties;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.Serdes;
import org.apache.kafka.common.utils.Bytes;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KeyValue;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.StreamsBuilder;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Branched;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Consumed;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Grouped;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Joined;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.KStream;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.KTable;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Materialized;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Named;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Produced;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.Repartitioned;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.kstream.TimeWindows;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.KeyValueStore;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.Stores;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.state.WindowStore;

/**
 * The whole detector as plain Kafka Streams code. It takes plain objects and adds itself to a {@link StreamsBuilder},
 * so it can be built and tested with {@code TopologyTestDriver} and no Spring. Spring only wires it.
 */
public final class TelemetryTopology {

    public static final String SEQ_STORE = "seq-tracker";
    public static final String COOLDOWN_STORE = "flag-cooldown";
    public static final String AIMBOT_WINDOWS = "aimbot-windows";
    public static final String SPEEDHACK_WINDOWS = "speedhack-windows";
    public static final String PROFILES_STORE = "player-profiles-store";
    /** Name of the explicit repartition step, which keeps the internal topic name stable across code changes. */
    public static final String FLAGS_REPARTITION = "flags";

    private final DetectionProperties props;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final TopologyMetrics metrics;
    private final DetectionRules rules;

    public TelemetryTopology(DetectionProperties props, Clock clock, TopologyMetrics metrics) {
        this.props = props;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.metrics = metrics;
        this.rules = new DetectionRules(props);
    }

    public void addTo(StreamsBuilder builder) {
        KTable<String, PlayerProfile> profiles = builder.table(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES,
                Consumed.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.profile()),
                Materialized.<String, PlayerProfile, KeyValueStore<Bytes, byte[]>>as(PROFILES_STORE));

        builder.addStateStore(Stores.keyValueStoreBuilder(
                Stores.persistentKeyValueStore(SEQ_STORE), Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.integrity()));
        builder.addStateStore(Stores.keyValueStoreBuilder(
                Stores.persistentKeyValueStore(COOLDOWN_STORE), Serdes.String(), Serdes.Long()));

        // 1. Parse, and route failures to the DLT. Events are read as raw bytes so that a bad record is handled
        //    by normal topology code instead of a deserialization exception handler.
        KStream<String, byte[]> raw = builder.stream(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS,
                Consumed.with(Serdes.String(), Serdes.ByteArray()));
        KStream<String, ParseResult> parsed = raw.process(() -> new ParseProcessor(clock, metrics));
        Map<String, KStream<String, ParseResult>> outcome = parsed.split(Named.as("parse-"))
                .branch((key, result) -> result instanceof ParseResult.Failed, Branched.as("failed"))
                .defaultBranch(Branched.as("ok"));

        outcome.get("parse-failed")
                .mapValues(result -> ((ParseResult.Failed) result).deadLetter())
                .to(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, Produced.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.deadLetter()));

        KStream<String, MatchEvent> events = outcome.get("parse-ok")
                .mapValues(result -> ((ParseResult.Parsed) result).event());

        // 2. Integrity tracking: per-player seq counts, re-emitted for every player on a wall-clock interval.
        events.process(() -> new IntegrityProcessor(props.integrityEmitInterval(), clock), SEQ_STORE)
                .to(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, Produced.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.integrity()));

        // 3. Detection windows read their own key-preserving parse of the raw stream. They must not use the
        //    `events` stream above. KStream#process marks its output as possibly key-changing, so grouping it would
        //    make Kafka Streams add an internal repartition topic per window and rewrite every event (about 24,000
        //    per second) through the broker. mapValues keeps the key, so no repartition topic appears. EventParser is
        //    the same predicate ParseProcessor uses, so both paths accept exactly the same records.
        KStream<String, MatchEvent> windowEvents = raw
                .mapValues((key, bytes) -> EventParser.parse(key, bytes).event())
                .filter((key, event) -> event != null);

        // 3a. Aimbot: a hopping window over SHOT events.
        var aim = props.aimbot();
        KStream<String, FlagCandidate> aimFlags = windowEvents
                .filter((key, event) -> event.type() == EventType.SHOT)
                .groupByKey(Grouped.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.matchEvent()))
                .windowedBy(TimeWindows.ofSizeAndGrace(aim.windowSize(), aim.grace()).advanceBy(aim.advance()))
                .aggregate(AimStats::empty, (key, event, stats) -> stats.add(event),
                        Materialized.<String, AimStats, WindowStore<Bytes, byte[]>>as(AIMBOT_WINDOWS)
                                .withKeySerde(Serdes.String())
                                .withValueSerde(JsonSerdes.of(AimStats.class)))
                .toStream()
                .filter((windowedKey, stats) -> stats != null && rules.aimbotTriggered(stats))
                .map((windowedKey, stats) -> KeyValue.pair(windowedKey.key(),
                        FlagCandidate.aimbot(windowedKey.key(), windowedKey.window(), stats, rules)));

        // 3b. Speedhack: the same shape over POSITION events.
        var speed = props.speedhack();
        KStream<String, FlagCandidate> speedFlags = windowEvents
                .filter((key, event) -> event.type() == EventType.POSITION)
                .groupByKey(Grouped.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.matchEvent()))
                .windowedBy(TimeWindows.ofSizeAndGrace(speed.windowSize(), speed.grace()).advanceBy(speed.advance()))
                .aggregate(SpeedStats::empty,
                        (key, event, stats) -> stats.add(event, rules.movingSpeedFloor(), rules.speedLimit()),
                        Materialized.<String, SpeedStats, WindowStore<Bytes, byte[]>>as(SPEEDHACK_WINDOWS)
                                .withKeySerde(Serdes.String())
                                .withValueSerde(JsonSerdes.of(SpeedStats.class)))
                .toStream()
                .filter((windowedKey, stats) -> stats != null && rules.speedhackTriggered(stats))
                .map((windowedKey, stats) -> KeyValue.pair(windowedKey.key(),
                        FlagCandidate.speedhack(windowedKey.key(), windowedKey.window(), stats, rules)));

        // 4. Merge, repartition explicitly, enrich, de-duplicate, publish. Mapping a Windowed<String> key to a plain
        //    String counts as a key change, so Kafka Streams would add a repartition topic before the join anyway.
        //    Naming it keeps the internal topic name stable. Flags are rare, so the cost is negligible.
        aimFlags.merge(speedFlags)
                .repartition(Repartitioned.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.of(FlagCandidate.class))
                        .withName(FLAGS_REPARTITION))
                .leftJoin(profiles, FlagCandidate::withProfile,
                        Joined.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.of(FlagCandidate.class), JsonSerdes.profile()))
                .process(() -> new CooldownProcessor(props.cooldown(), props.detectorVersion(), clock, metrics),
                        COOLDOWN_STORE)
                .to(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, Produced.with(Serdes.String(), JsonSerdes.suspicious()));
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/TopologyMetrics.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;

/**
 * What the topology reports. A plain interface so the topology stays free of Spring and Micrometer. These counters
 * can count an event twice when a transaction is retried after a crash, so they are for dashboards only.
 */
public interface TopologyMetrics {

    TopologyMetrics NOOP = new TopologyMetrics() {
        @Override
        public void parsed() {
        }

        @Override
        public void deadLettered() {
        }

        @Override
        public void flagged(Rule rule) {
        }
    };

    void parsed();

    void deadLettered();

    void flagged(Rule rule);
}

```

```yml
    format:
      console: ecs

telemetry:
  topics:
    replication-factor: ${TOPIC_RF:1}
  detection:
    detector-version: "1.0.0"
    cooldown: 10m
    integrity-emit-interval: 5s
    aimbot:
      window-size: 5s
      advance: 1s
      grace: 2s
      min-hits: 12
      max-avg-reaction-ms: 140
      min-headshot-rate: 0.62
    speedhack:
      window-size: 5s
      advance: 1s
      grace: 2s
      min-moving-samples: 40
      moving-speed-floor: 0.5
      speed-limit: 7.5
      min-overspeed-ratio: 0.15
  arena:
    enabled: ${ARENA_ENABLED:false}
    base-url: ${ARENA_BASE_URL:http://localhost:8080}
    service-username: telemetry-sim
    service-password: ${TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD:}
    max-retries: 3
    initial-backoff: 1s
    backoff-multiplier: 2.0
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/ArenaBridgeIT.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ParticipantSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.KafkaTestContainer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.StubArenaServer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.StubArenaServer.Response;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertyRegistry;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertySource;

/**
 * The bridge against a real broker and a stub Arena: the retry and dead-letter behavior of spec section 10. The
 * streams client is not started (auto-startup=false), so only the Spring Kafka listener runs.
 * {@code max-retries=2} means 3 attempts in total.
 */
@SpringBootTest(classes = DetectorApplication.class, webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT,
        properties = {
                "spring.kafka.streams.auto-startup=false",
                "spring.kafka.streams.application-id=bridge-it-${random.uuid}",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.state.dir=${java.io.tmpdir}/ks-${random.uuid}",
                "telemetry.arena.enabled=true",
                "telemetry.arena.service-password=test-password",
                "telemetry.arena.max-retries=2",
                "telemetry.arena.initial-backoff=100ms"
        })
@DirtiesContext(classMode = DirtiesContext.ClassMode.AFTER_CLASS)
class ArenaBridgeIT {

    private static final String MATCHES = "/api/v1/matches";
    private static final String LOGIN = "/api/v1/auth/login";
    private static final StubArenaServer ARENA = new StubArenaServer();

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void properties(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        registry.add("spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers", KafkaTestContainer.KAFKA::getBootstrapServers);
        registry.add("telemetry.arena.base-url", ARENA::baseUrl);
    }

    @AfterAll
    static void stopArena() {
        ARENA.close();
    }

    @Autowired
    MeterRegistry registry;

    @BeforeEach
    void resetArena() {
        ARENA.reset();
    }

    private static String summaryJson(String externalId) {
        return JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsString(new MatchSummary(externalId, "FFA", "Harbor",
                "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z", "2026-11-17T10:02:00Z",
                List.of(new ParticipantSummary("p1", 1, 3, 1, 1, 10, 5, 200),
                        new ParticipantSummary("p2", 2, 1, 3, 0, 8, 2, 240))));
    }

    private static String send(String json) {
        String externalId = "sim-it-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = KafkaIt.producer()) {
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, externalId, json.replace("@ID@", externalId)
                    .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
            producer.flush();
        }
        return externalId;
    }

    private static String sendSummary() {
        return send(summaryJson("@ID@"));
    }

    private static long callsFor(String externalId) {
        return ARENA.requests("POST", MATCHES).stream().filter(r -> r.body().contains(externalId)).count();
    }

    private static List<ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]>> deadLetters(String externalId) {
        return KafkaIt.readTopic(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES_DLT).stream().filter(r -> externalId.equals(r.key())).toList();
    }

    private double deadLetteredCount() {
        return registry.counter("telemetry.bridge.dead_lettered").count();
    }

    private static void waitForCalls(String externalId, long calls) {
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(callsFor(externalId)).isEqualTo(calls));
    }

    @Test
    void a201IsOneCallAndNoDeadLetter() throws Exception {
        ARENA.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, Response.json(201, "{}"));

        String id = sendSummary();

        waitForCalls(id, 1);
        Thread.sleep(1500);
        assertThat(callsFor(id)).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(deadLetters(id)).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void twoTemporaryFailuresThenSuccessIsThreeCallsAndNoDeadLetter() throws Exception {
        ARENA.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, Response.json(503, ""), Response.json(503, ""), Response.json(201, "{}"));

        String id = sendSummary();

        waitForCalls(id, 3);
        Thread.sleep(1500);
        assertThat(callsFor(id)).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(deadLetters(id)).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void aTemporaryFailureThatNeverEndsIsThreeCallsThenTheDeadLetterTopic() {
        ARENA.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, Response.json(503, ""), Response.json(503, ""), Response.json(503, ""),
                Response.json(503, ""));
        double before = deadLetteredCount();

        String id = sendSummary();

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(deadLetters(id)).hasSize(1));
        assertThat(callsFor(id)).isEqualTo(3);
        ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> letter = deadLetters(id).get(0);
        // The original JSON is kept as it was, and Spring adds its exception headers.
        assertThat(new String(letter.value(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).contains("\"externalId\":\"" + id + "\"");
        assertThat(letter.headers().lastHeader("kafka_dlt-exception-message")).isNotNull();
        assertThat(letter.headers().lastHeader("kafka_dlt-original-offset")).isNotNull();
        assertThat(letter.partition()).isBetween(0, 2);
        assertThat(deadLetteredCount()).isEqualTo(before + 1);
    }

    @Test
    void aPermanentRejectionIsOneCallThenTheDeadLetterTopic() {
        ARENA.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, Response.problem(422, "PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE", "Player is banned"));

        String id = sendSummary();

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(deadLetters(id)).hasSize(1));
        assertThat(callsFor(id)).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(new String(deadLetters(id).get(0).headers().lastHeader("kafka_dlt-exception-message").value(),
                StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).contains("PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE");
    }

    @Test
    void garbageJsonNeverReachesArenaAndIsDeadLettered() {
        String id = send("{\"broken\":");

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).untilAsserted(() -> assertThat(deadLetters(id)).hasSize(1));
        assertThat(ARENA.requests("POST", MATCHES)).isEmpty();
        assertThat(new String(deadLetters(id).get(0).value(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).isEqualTo("{\"broken\":");
    }

    @Test
    void anExpiredTokenIsRefreshedAndTheMatchStillArrives() throws Exception {
        ARENA.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, Response.problem(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "expired"), Response.json(201, "{}"));

        String id = sendSummary();

        waitForCalls(id, 2);
        Thread.sleep(1500);
        List<StubArenaServer.Request> calls = ARENA.requests("POST", MATCHES);
        assertThat(calls).hasSize(2);
        // The retry carries a freshly issued token, so a login happened in between.
        assertThat(calls.get(1).authorization()).isNotEqualTo(calls.get(0).authorization());
        assertThat(ARENA.requests("POST", LOGIN)).isNotEmpty();
        assertThat(deadLetters(id)).isEmpty();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/DetectionPropertiesTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.EnableConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.ConfigDataApplicationContextInitializer;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.runner.ApplicationContextRunner;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

class DetectionPropertiesTest {

    @Configuration
    @EnableConfigurationProperties(DetectionProperties.class)
    static class PropsConfig {
    }

    private static final Duration S1 = Duration.ofSeconds(1);
    private static final Duration S2 = Duration.ofSeconds(2);
    private static final Duration S5 = Duration.ofSeconds(5);

    private final ApplicationContextRunner runner = new ApplicationContextRunner()
            .withInitializer(new ConfigDataApplicationContextInitializer())
            .withUserConfiguration(PropsConfig.class);

    private static void assertRejected(Supplier<?> create, String messagePart) {
        assertThatThrownBy(create::get).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class).hasMessageContaining(messagePart);
    }

    @Test
    void applicationYmlDefaultsBindToTheDocumentedValues() {
        runner.run(ctx -> assertThat(ctx.getBean(DetectionProperties.class)).isEqualTo(TestDetection.defaults()));
    }

    @Test
    void environmentOverridesAreBound() {
        runner.withPropertyValues("telemetry.detection.aimbot.min-hits=20", "telemetry.detection.cooldown=1m")
                .run(ctx -> {
                    DetectionProperties props = ctx.getBean(DetectionProperties.class);
                    assertThat(props.aimbot().minHits()).isEqualTo(20);
                    assertThat(props.cooldown()).isEqualTo(Duration.ofMinutes(1));
                });
    }

    @Test
    void aBrokenRuleFailsStartup() {
        runner.withPropertyValues("telemetry.detection.speedhack.min-overspeed-ratio=1.5")
                .run(ctx -> assertThat(ctx).hasFailed());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsBadTopLevelSettings() {
        DetectionProperties ok = TestDetection.defaults();

        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties(" ", ok.cooldown(), ok.integrityEmitInterval(), ok.aimbot(),
                ok.speedhack()), "detector-version");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties(null, ok.cooldown(), ok.integrityEmitInterval(), ok.aimbot(),
                ok.speedhack()), "detector-version");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties("1", Duration.ZERO, ok.integrityEmitInterval(), ok.aimbot(),
                ok.speedhack()), "cooldown must be positive");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties("1", ok.cooldown(), null, ok.aimbot(), ok.speedhack()),
                "integrity-emit-interval must be positive");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties("1", ok.cooldown(), ok.integrityEmitInterval(), null,
                ok.speedhack()), "required");
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsBadAimbotSettings() {
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(Duration.ZERO, S1, S2, 12, 140, 0.62), "window-size");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S5, S1, Duration.ofSeconds(-1), 12, 140, 0.62), "grace");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S1, S5, S2, 12, 140, 0.62), "must not be greater");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S5, S1, S2, 0, 140, 0.62), "min-hits");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S5, S1, S2, 12, 0, 0.62), "max-avg-reaction-ms");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S5, S1, S2, 12, 140, -0.1), "min-headshot-rate");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S5, S1, S2, 12, 140, 1.1), "min-headshot-rate");
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsBadSpeedhackSettings() {
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S5, Duration.ZERO, S2, 40, 0.5, 7.5, 0.15), "advance");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S1, S5, S2, 40, 0.5, 7.5, 0.15), "must not be greater");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S5, S1, S2, 0, 0.5, 7.5, 0.15), "min-moving-samples");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S5, S1, S2, 40, 0, 7.5, 0.15), "moving-speed-floor");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S5, S1, S2, 40, 0.5, 0, 0.15), "speed-limit");
        assertRejected(() -> new DetectionProperties.Speedhack(S5, S1, S2, 40, 0.5, 7.5, 2), "min-overspeed-ratio");
    }

    @Test
    void acceptsTheBoundaryValues() {
        DetectionProperties.Aimbot edge = new DetectionProperties.Aimbot(S1, S1, S1, 1, 1, 0.0);

        assertThat(edge.minHeadshotRate()).isZero();
    }
}
```
