`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/DetectorEndToEndIT.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.KafkaTestContainer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KafkaStreams;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.StreamsBuilderFactoryBean;
import org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertyRegistry;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertySource;

/**
 * The real detector against a real Kafka 4 broker: one obvious cheater and one honest player, and one garbage record.
 * Production settings apply (64 MB cache, exactly_once_v2), so windows emit at commit time and the first flag may come
 * from a different window than in the TopologyTestDriver tests. The assertions are therefore about who is flagged and
 * how often, not about exact evidence.
 */
@SpringBootTest(classes = DetectorApplication.class, webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT,
        properties = {
                "spring.kafka.streams.application-id=detector-e2e-${random.uuid}",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.commit.interval.ms=100",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.num.standby.replicas=0",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.state.dir=${java.io.tmpdir}/ks-${random.uuid}"
        })
@DirtiesContext(classMode = DirtiesContext.ClassMode.AFTER_CLASS)
class DetectorEndToEndIT {

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void kafka(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        registry.add("spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers", KafkaTestContainer.KAFKA::getBootstrapServers);
    }

    @Autowired
    StreamsBuilderFactoryBean streams;

    @Test
    void flagsTheCheaterOnceLeavesTheHonestPlayerAloneAndDeadLettersGarbage() throws Exception {
        String cheater = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        String honest = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        long now = Instant.now().toEpochMilli();

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).until(() ->
                streams.getKafkaStreams() != null && streams.getKafkaStreams().state() == KafkaStreams.State.RUNNING);

        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = KafkaIt.producer()) {
            KafkaIt.sendJson(producer, Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, cheater,
                    new PlayerProfile(cheater, "it_cheater", "IN", SkillTier.GOLD, now), now - 60_000);
            KafkaIt.sendJson(producer, Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, honest,
                    new PlayerProfile(honest, "it_honest", "US", SkillTier.SILVER, now), now - 60_000);
            producer.flush();
            Thread.sleep(2000); // let the profile table load before the flags arrive

            // The cheater: 15 hits in 2.8 s, 13 of them headshots, 60 ms reaction.
            for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
                KafkaIt.send(producer, TestEvents.shot(cheater, i + 1, now - 10_000 + i * 200L, true, i >= 2, 60));
            }
            // The honest player: 60 s of shots, 35% hit rate, 240 ms reaction.
            for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
                boolean hit = i % 20 < 7;
                KafkaIt.send(producer, TestEvents.shot(honest, i + 1, now - 10_000 + i * 200L, hit, false, 240));
            }
            // One record that is not JSON at all, keyed by the cheater.
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, null, now - 5_000, cheater,
                    "{\"broken\":".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
            producer.flush();
        }

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(1)).untilAsserted(() ->
                assertThat(flagsFor(cheater)).isNotEmpty());
        Thread.sleep(3000); // a duplicate flag would have arrived by now

        List<SuspiciousPlayer> cheaterFlags = flagsFor(cheater);
        assertThat(cheaterFlags).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(cheaterFlags.get(0).rule()).isEqualTo(Rule.AIMBOT);
        assertThat(cheaterFlags.get(0).username()).isEqualTo("it_cheater");
        assertThat(cheaterFlags.get(0).skillTier()).isEqualTo(SkillTier.GOLD);
        assertThat(flagsFor(honest)).isEmpty();

        List<DeadLetter> letters = KafkaIt.readValues(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, DeadLetter.class).stream()
                .filter(l -> cheater.equals(l.key())).toList();
        assertThat(letters).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(new String(Base64.getDecoder().decode(letters.get(0).payloadBase64()), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                .isEqualTo("{\"broken\":");
        assertThat(letters.get(0).sourceTopic()).isEqualTo(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS);
        assertThat(letters.get(0).partition()).isBetween(0, 5);
    }

    @Test
    void topicsAndInternalTopicsHaveTheExpectedShapeOnARealBroker() throws Exception {
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).until(() ->
                streams.getKafkaStreams() != null && streams.getKafkaStreams().state() == KafkaStreams.State.RUNNING);

        Map<String, Integer> partitions = KafkaIt.partitionCounts();

        assertThat(partitions).containsEntry(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, 6)
                .containsEntry(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, 6)
                .containsEntry(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, 6)
                .containsEntry(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, 6)
                .containsEntry(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, 6)
                .containsEntry(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, 3)
                .containsEntry(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH, 1);
        // The window branches add no repartition topic. The single named one is co-partitioned with player-profiles.
        assertThat(partitions.keySet()).noneMatch(name -> name.contains("-windows-repartition"));
        assertThat(partitions).anySatisfy((name, count) -> {
            assertThat(name).endsWith("-flags-repartition");
            assertThat(count).isEqualTo(partitions.get(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES));
        });
    }

    private static List<SuspiciousPlayer> flagsFor(String playerId) {
        return KafkaIt.readValues(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, SuspiciousPlayer.class).stream()
                .filter(f -> playerId.equals(f.playerId())).toList();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/ExactlyOnceIT.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.KafkaTestContainer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KafkaStreams;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.StreamsBuilderFactoryBean;
import org.springframework.test.annotation.DirtiesContext;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertyRegistry;
import org.springframework.test.context.DynamicPropertySource;

/**
 * 20 players x 500 gapless events: every player must end at count 500 with no duplicates and no gaps. A negative
 * control then re-sends one sequence number, to prove the tracker would notice a duplicate if one ever happened.
 */
@SpringBootTest(classes = DetectorApplication.class, webEnvironment = SpringBootTest.WebEnvironment.RANDOM_PORT,
        properties = {
                "spring.kafka.streams.application-id=exactly-once-${random.uuid}",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.commit.interval.ms=100",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.num.standby.replicas=0",
                "spring.kafka.streams.properties.state.dir=${java.io.tmpdir}/ks-${random.uuid}",
                "telemetry.detection.integrity-emit-interval=1s"
        })
@DirtiesContext(classMode = DirtiesContext.ClassMode.AFTER_CLASS)
class ExactlyOnceIT {

    private static final int PLAYERS = 20;
    private static final int EVENTS = 500;

    @DynamicPropertySource
    static void kafka(DynamicPropertyRegistry registry) {
        registry.add("spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers", KafkaTestContainer.KAFKA::getBootstrapServers);
    }

    @Autowired
    StreamsBuilderFactoryBean streams;

    /** The latest committed stats of each of our players. */
    private static Map<String, IntegrityStats> latest(List<String> players) {
        Map<String, IntegrityStats> latest = new HashMap<>();
        for (IntegrityStats s : KafkaIt.readValues(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, IntegrityStats.class)) {
            if (players.contains(s.playerId())) {
                latest.put(s.playerId(), s);
            }
        }
        return latest;
    }

    @Test
    void everyPlayerCountsExactlyItsEventsAndADuplicateIsDetected() {
        List<String> players = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < PLAYERS; i++) {
            players.add(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
        }
        long base = Instant.now().toEpochMilli() - 60_000;

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).until(() ->
                streams.getKafkaStreams() != null && streams.getKafkaStreams().state() == KafkaStreams.State.RUNNING);

        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = KafkaIt.producer()) {
            // Idle POSITION events, so the speedhack rule stays quiet. Interleave players like real traffic.
            for (int seq = 1; seq <= EVENTS; seq++) {
                for (String player : players) {
                    KafkaIt.send(producer, TestEvents.position(player, seq, base + seq * 50L, 0, 0));
                }
            }
            producer.flush();
        }

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(90)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(2)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            Map<String, IntegrityStats> stats = latest(players);
            assertThat(stats).hasSize(PLAYERS);
            assertThat(stats.values()).allSatisfy(s -> assertThat(s.count()).isEqualTo(EVENTS));
        });

        Map<String, IntegrityStats> stats = latest(players);
        assertThat(stats.values()).allSatisfy(s -> {
            assertThat(s.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(EVENTS);
            assertThat(s.duplicates()).isZero();
            assertThat(s.gaps()).isZero();
        });

        // Negative control: send seq 250 of the first player again.
        String victim = players.get(0);
        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = KafkaIt.producer()) {
            KafkaIt.send(producer, TestEvents.position(victim, 250, base + 250 * 50L, 0, 0));
            producer.flush();
        }

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(60)).pollInterval(Duration.ofSeconds(2)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            IntegrityStats s = latest(players).get(victim);
            assertThat(s.duplicates()).isEqualTo(1);
            assertThat(s.count()).isEqualTo(EVENTS);
            assertThat(s.gaps()).isZero();
        });
        assertThat(latest(players).get(players.get(1)).duplicates()).isZero();
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/KafkaIt.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.KafkaTestContainer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.admin.AdminClient;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.KafkaConsumer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.common.TopicPartition;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.ByteArrayDeserializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.ByteArraySerializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer;

/** Plain-client helpers for the integration tests: produce events and read whole topics with read_committed. */
final class KafkaIt {

    private KafkaIt() {
    }

    static String bootstrap() {
        return KafkaTestContainer.KAFKA.getBootstrapServers();
    }

    static KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer() {
        Properties p = new Properties();
        p.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrap());
        p.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
        p.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, ByteArraySerializer.class.getName());
        p.put(ProducerConfig.ENABLE_IDEMPOTENCE_CONFIG, true);
        p.put(ProducerConfig.LINGER_MS_CONFIG, 5);
        return new KafkaProducer<>(p);
    }

    /** Sends one event keyed by its player id, with the event time as the record timestamp. */
    static void send(KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer, MatchEvent event) {
        producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>("match-events", null, event.ts(), event.playerId(), TestEvents.bytes(event)));
    }

    static void sendJson(KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer, String topic, String key, Object value, long ts) {
        producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, null, ts, key, JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(value)));
    }

    /** Everything currently committed in {@code topic}, from the beginning. Empty when the topic does not exist yet. */
    static List<ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]>> readTopic(String topic) {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrap());
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, ByteArrayDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ISOLATION_LEVEL_CONFIG, "read_committed");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ENABLE_AUTO_COMMIT_CONFIG, false);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "it-reader-" + UUID.randomUUID());
        try (KafkaConsumer<String, byte[]> consumer = new KafkaConsumer<>(config)) {
            var infos = consumer.partitionsFor(topic, Duration.ofSeconds(5));
            if (infos == null || infos.isEmpty()) {
                return List.of();
            }
            List<TopicPartition> partitions = infos.stream()
                    .map(i -> new TopicPartition(i.topic(), i.partition())).collect(Collectors.toList());
            consumer.assign(partitions);
            consumer.seekToBeginning(partitions);
            Map<TopicPartition, Long> ends = consumer.endOffsets(partitions);

            List<ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]>> records = new ArrayList<>();
            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + Duration.ofSeconds(15).toNanos();
            while (!reachedEnds(consumer, ends) && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
                consumer.poll(Duration.ofMillis(200)).forEach(records::add);
            }
            return records;
        }
    }

    private static boolean reachedEnds(KafkaConsumer<String, byte[]> consumer, Map<TopicPartition, Long> ends) {
        return ends.entrySet().stream().allMatch(e -> consumer.position(e.getKey()) >= e.getValue());
    }

    static <T> List<T> readValues(String topic, Class<T> type) {
        return readTopic(topic).stream()
                .filter(r -> r.value() != null)
                .map(r -> JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(r.value(), type))
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    /** Partition counts of every topic on the broker. */
    static Map<String, Integer> partitionCounts() throws Exception {
        try (AdminClient admin = AdminClient.create(Map.of("bootstrap.servers", bootstrap()))) {
            Map<String, Integer> counts = new HashMap<>();
            admin.describeTopics(admin.listTopics().names().get()).allTopicNames().get()
                    .forEach((name, d) -> counts.put(name, d.partitions().size()));
            return counts;
        }
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/WiringUnitTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.simple.SimpleMeterRegistry;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KafkaStreams;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.errors.StreamsUncaughtExceptionHandler.StreamThreadExceptionResponse;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.boot.health.contributor.Health;
import org.springframework.boot.health.contributor.Status;
import org.springframework.kafka.config.StreamsBuilderFactoryBean;

/** The small Spring-facing classes of the detector, tested without a context or a broker. */
class WiringUnitTest {

    // --- MicrometerTopologyMetrics -------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    void metricsCountParsedDeadLetteredAndFlaggedPerRule() {
        SimpleMeterRegistry registry = new SimpleMeterRegistry();
        MicrometerTopologyMetrics metrics = new MicrometerTopologyMetrics(registry);

        metrics.parsed();
        metrics.parsed();
        metrics.deadLettered();
        metrics.flagged(Rule.AIMBOT);
        metrics.flagged(Rule.AIMBOT);
        metrics.flagged(Rule.SPEEDHACK);

        assertThat(registry.get("telemetry.detect.events.parsed").counter().count()).isEqualTo(2.0);
        assertThat(registry.get("telemetry.detect.events.dead_lettered").counter().count()).isEqualTo(1.0);
        assertThat(registry.get("telemetry.detect.flags").tag("rule", "AIMBOT").counter().count()).isEqualTo(2.0);
        assertThat(registry.get("telemetry.detect.flags").tag("rule", "SPEEDHACK").counter().count()).isEqualTo(1.0);
    }

    // --- KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static Health healthIn(KafkaStreams.State state) {
        StreamsBuilderFactoryBean factory = mock(StreamsBuilderFactoryBean.class);
        KafkaStreams streams = mock(KafkaStreams.class);
        when(factory.getKafkaStreams()).thenReturn(streams);
        when(streams.state()).thenReturn(state);
        return new KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator(factory).health();
    }

    @Test
    void healthIsUpOnlyWhileRunning() {
        Health health = healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.RUNNING);

        assertThat(health.getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.UP);
        assertThat(health.getDetails()).containsEntry("state", "RUNNING");
    }

    @Test
    void healthIsOutOfServiceWhileCreatedOrRebalancing() {
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.CREATED).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.OUT_OF_SERVICE);
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.REBALANCING).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.OUT_OF_SERVICE);
    }

    @Test
    void healthIsDownInEveryOtherState() {
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.ERROR).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.DOWN);
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.NOT_RUNNING).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.DOWN);
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.PENDING_SHUTDOWN).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.DOWN);
        assertThat(healthIn(KafkaStreams.State.PENDING_ERROR).getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.DOWN);
    }

    @Test
    void healthIsOutOfServiceBeforeTheClientExists() {
        StreamsBuilderFactoryBean factory = mock(StreamsBuilderFactoryBean.class);
        when(factory.getKafkaStreams()).thenReturn(null);

        Health health = new KafkaStreamsHealthIndicator(factory).health();

        assertThat(health.getStatus()).isEqualTo(Status.OUT_OF_SERVICE);
    }

    // --- StreamsRuntimeConfig ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    @Test
    void aDeadStreamThreadIsReplaced() {
        StreamsBuilderFactoryBean factoryBean = new StreamsBuilderFactoryBean();

        new StreamsRuntimeConfig().streamsRuntimeConfigurer().configure(factoryBean);

        assertThat(factoryBean.getStreamsUncaughtExceptionHandler().handle(new IllegalStateException("boom")))
                .isEqualTo(StreamThreadExceptionResponse.REPLACE_THREAD);
    }

    @Test
    void theClockIsUtcSystemTime() {
        assertThat(new ClockConfig().clock().getZone().getId()).isEqualTo("Z");
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/ArenaBridgeUnitTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;

import java.time.Duration;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** The listener's parsing and the properties' validation, without Kafka. The error handler is covered by ArenaBridgeIT. */
class ArenaBridgeUnitTest {

    private static ConsumerRecord<String, String> record(String value) {
        return new ConsumerRecord<>(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, 0, 0L, "sim-r-1-000001", value);
    }

    @Test
    void theListenerParsesTheSummaryAndHandsItToTheClient() {
        ArenaMatchClient client = mock(ArenaMatchClient.class);
        MatchSummary summary = new MatchSummary("sim-r-1-000001", "FFA", "Harbor", "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z",
                "2026-11-17T10:02:00Z", java.util.List.of());

        new ArenaBridgeListener(client).onSummary(record(JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsString(summary)));

        verify(client).recordMatch(summary);
    }

    @Test
    void garbageIsNonRetryableAndNeverReachesArena() {
        ArenaMatchClient client = mock(ArenaMatchClient.class);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaBridgeListener(client).onSummary(record("{\"broken\":")))
                .isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((NonRetryableArenaException) e).code()).isEqualTo("MALFORMED_SUMMARY");
        verify(client, never()).recordMatch(any());
    }

    @Test
    void aNullValueIsAlsoMalformed() {
        ArenaMatchClient client = mock(ArenaMatchClient.class);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaBridgeListener(client).onSummary(record(null)))
                .isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void propertiesRejectBrokenSettings() {
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaProperties(true, "http://x", "u", "", 3, Duration.ofSeconds(1), 2.0))
                .hasMessageContaining("TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD");
        assertThat(new ArenaProperties(false, "http://x", "u", "", 3, Duration.ofSeconds(1), 2.0).enabled()).isFalse();
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaProperties(false, " ", "u", "p", 3, Duration.ofSeconds(1), 2.0))
                .hasMessageContaining("base-url");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaProperties(false, "http://x", "u", "p", -1, Duration.ofSeconds(1), 2.0))
                .hasMessageContaining("max-retries");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaProperties(false, "http://x", "u", "p", 3, Duration.ZERO, 2.0))
                .hasMessageContaining("initial-backoff");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> new ArenaProperties(false, "http://x", "u", "p", 3, Duration.ofSeconds(1), 0.5))
                .hasMessageContaining("backoff-multiplier");
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/bridge/ArenaMatchClientTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.bridge;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ParticipantSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.StubArenaServer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.StubArenaServer.Response;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.simple.SimpleMeterRegistry;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ArenaMatchClientTest {

    private static final String MATCHES = "/api/v1/matches";
    private static final String LOGIN = "/api/v1/auth/login";

    private static final MatchSummary SUMMARY = new MatchSummary("sim-r-1-000001", "FFA", "Harbor",
            "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z", "2026-11-17T10:02:00Z",
            List.of(new ParticipantSummary("p1", 1, 3, 1, 1, 10, 5, 200),
                    new ParticipantSummary("p2", 2, 1, 3, 0, 8, 2, 240)));

    private StubArenaServer arena;
    private SimpleMeterRegistry registry;
    private ArenaMatchClient client;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        arena = new StubArenaServer();
        registry = new SimpleMeterRegistry();
        client = new ArenaMatchClient(new ArenaProperties(true, arena.baseUrl(), "telemetry-sim", "service-pw", 3,
                Duration.ofMillis(100), 2.0), registry);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        arena.close();
    }

    private double posted(String outcome) {
        return registry.counter("telemetry.bridge.matches.posted", "outcome", outcome).count();
    }

    private void matchesAnswer(Response... responses) {
        arena.enqueue("POST", MATCHES, responses);
    }

    @Test
    void a201MeansCreated() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(201, "{}"));

        client.recordMatch(SUMMARY);

        assertThat(posted("created")).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(posted("replayed")).isZero();
        StubArenaServer.Request request = arena.requests("POST", MATCHES).get(0);
        assertThat(request.authorization()).isEqualTo("Bearer token-1");
        assertThat(request.body()).contains("\"externalId\":\"sim-r-1-000001\"").contains("\"participants\"");
    }

    @Test
    void a200MeansAReplayOfAMatchArenaAlreadyHas() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(200, "{}"));

        client.recordMatch(SUMMARY);

        assertThat(posted("replayed")).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(posted("created")).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void theLoginTokenIsCachedBetweenMatches() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(201, "{}"), Response.json(201, "{}"));

        client.recordMatch(SUMMARY);
        client.recordMatch(SUMMARY);

        assertThat(arena.requests("POST", LOGIN)).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(posted("created")).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void a401LogsInAgainAndRetriesOnceInsideTheSameCall() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.problem(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "expired"), Response.json(201, "{}"));

        client.recordMatch(SUMMARY);

        assertThat(arena.requests("POST", LOGIN)).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(arena.requests("POST", MATCHES)).extracting(StubArenaServer.Request::authorization)
                .containsExactly("Bearer token-1", "Bearer token-2");
        assertThat(posted("created")).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void aSecond401IsNonRetryable() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.problem(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "no"), Response.problem(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "no"));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((NonRetryableArenaException) e).code()).isEqualTo("UNAUTHORIZED");
        assertThat(arena.requests("POST", MATCHES)).hasSize(2);
    }

    @Test
    void tooManyRequestsAndServerErrorsAreRetryable() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(429, ""), Response.json(500, ""), Response.json(503, "{}"));

        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(RetryableArenaException.class);
        }
        assertThat(posted("created")).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void clientErrorsAreNonRetryableAndCarryArenasCodeAndDetail() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.problem(422, "PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE", "Player sim_0042 is banned"),
                Response.problem(409, "MATCH_CONFLICT", "externalId reused"),
                Response.problem(400, "VALIDATION_FAILED", "bad body"),
                Response.problem(404, "PLAYER_NOT_FOUND", "unknown player"));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .hasMessage("PLAYER_NOT_ELIGIBLE: Player sim_0042 is banned");
        for (String code : List.of("MATCH_CONFLICT", "VALIDATION_FAILED", "PLAYER_NOT_FOUND")) {
            assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                    .extracting(e -> ((NonRetryableArenaException) e).code()).isEqualTo(code);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void aClientErrorWithoutProblemJsonReportsTheRawStatusAndBody() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(403, "forbidden"), Response.json(418, ""));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .hasMessage("HTTP_403: forbidden");
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .hasMessage("HTTP_418: ");
    }

    @Test
    void aLongRawBodyIsCut() {
        matchesAnswer(Response.json(400, "x".repeat(1000)));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .satisfies(e -> assertThat(e.getMessage().length()).isLessThan(330));
    }

    @Test
    void aRefusedConnectionIsRetryable() {
        arena.close();

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(RetryableArenaException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("unreachable");
    }

    @Test
    void aRejectedLoginIsNonRetryableButAServerErrorOnLoginIsRetryable() {
        arena.enqueue("POST", LOGIN, Response.problem(401, "BAD_CREDENTIALS", "wrong password"));
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((NonRetryableArenaException) e).code()).isEqualTo("LOGIN_FAILED");

        arena.enqueue("POST", LOGIN, Response.json(503, ""));
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(RetryableArenaException.class);

        arena.enqueue("POST", LOGIN, Response.json(429, ""));
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(RetryableArenaException.class);
    }

    @Test
    void aLoginWithoutATokenIsNonRetryable() {
        arena.enqueue("POST", LOGIN, Response.json(200, "{}"));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> client.recordMatch(SUMMARY)).isInstanceOf(NonRetryableArenaException.class)
                .hasMessageContaining("accessToken");
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/AimbotTopologyTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AimbotTopologyTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final String Q = TestEvents.player(2);
    private static final long T0 = TopologyFixture.START.toEpochMilli();

    private final TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture();

    @AfterEach
    void close() {
        fixture.close();
    }

    /**
     * Sends {@code count} shots, one every {@code spacingMs}, all hits, the last {@code headshots} of them headshots.
     * Returns the next free seq.
     */
    private long burst(String player, long firstSeq, long startTs, int count, long spacingMs, int headshots,
                       int reactionMs) {
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            boolean headshot = i >= count - headshots;
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(player, firstSeq + i, startTs + i * spacingMs, true, headshot, reactionMs));
        }
        return firstSeq + count;
    }

    @Test
    void anObviousAimbotBurstProducesExactlyOneFlagWithCorrectEvidence() {
        // 15 shots over 2.8 s, all hits, 13 headshots (the last 13), 60 ms reaction.
        burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 60);

        List<SuspiciousPlayer> flags = fixture.flags.readValuesToList();

        assertThat(flags).hasSize(1);
        SuspiciousPlayer flag = flags.get(0);
        assertThat(flag.rule()).isEqualTo(Rule.AIMBOT);
        assertThat(flag.playerId()).isEqualTo(P);
        assertThat(flag.detectorVersion()).isEqualTo("1.0.0");
        assertThat(flag.flagId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(flag.detectedAt()).isEqualTo(T0);
        // The 12th shot (t = 2.2 s) is the first moment any window reaches 12 hits. The earliest window holding it
        // is [T0 - 2 s, T0 + 3 s), which then holds shots 1 to 12 of which 10 are headshots.
        assertThat(flag.windowStart()).isEqualTo(T0 - 2000);
        assertThat(flag.windowEnd()).isEqualTo(T0 + 3000);
        assertThat(flag.evidence()).isEqualTo(Evidence.aimbot(12, 12, 10, 0.8333, 60.0));
        // 0.5 + 0.25 x (0.8333 - 0.62) / 0.33 + 0.25 x (140 - 60) / 110
        assertThat(flag.score()).isEqualTo(0.843);
        // No profile was published, so the flag carries no username or tier.
        assertThat(flag.username()).isNull();
        assertThat(flag.skillTier()).isNull();
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.aimbotFlags.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void anHonestStreamForAMinuteNeverFlags() {
        // 5 shots a second for 60 s, 35% hit rate, 240 ms reaction.
        for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
            boolean hit = i % 20 < 7;
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, i + 1, T0 + i * 200L, hit, hit && i % 3 == 0, 240));
        }

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.aimbotFlags.get()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void aSecondBurstInsideTheCooldownDoesNotFlagAgain() {
        long next = burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 60);
        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValuesToList()).hasSize(1);

        fixture.advanceTime(Duration.ofSeconds(30));
        burst(P, next, T0 + 30_000, 15, 200, 13, 60);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void aBurstAfterTheCooldownFlagsAgain() {
        long next = burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 60);
        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValuesToList()).hasSize(1);

        fixture.advanceTime(Duration.ofMinutes(11));
        burst(P, next, T0 + Duration.ofMinutes(11).toMillis(), 15, 200, 13, 60);

        List<SuspiciousPlayer> second = fixture.flags.readValuesToList();
        assertThat(second).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(second.get(0).rule()).isEqualTo(Rule.AIMBOT);
        assertThat(second.get(0).detectedAt()).isEqualTo(T0 + Duration.ofMinutes(11).toMillis());
    }

    @Test
    void theCooldownIsPerPlayer() {
        burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 60);
        burst(Q, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 60);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValuesToList()).extracting(SuspiciousPlayer::playerId)
                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(P, Q);
    }

    @Test
    void slowReactionsOrFewHeadshotsOrFewHitsDoNotFlag() {
        burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 200, 13, 141);        // reaction just above the limit
        burst(Q, 1, T0, 15, 200, 0, 60);          // no headshots
        String third = TestEvents.player(3);
        burst(third, 1, T0, 11, 200, 11, 60);     // only 11 hits

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void shotsSpreadOverMoreThanAWindowDoNotAccumulate() {
        // 15 hits, but one every 500 ms: no 5 s window ever holds 12 of them.
        burst(P, 1, T0, 15, 500, 15, 60);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void invalidEventsAreDeadLetteredAndNeverCountedInTheWindows() {
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            // seq 0 is invalid, so the parse step rejects the event and the window path must ignore it too.
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 0, T0 + i * 200L, true, true, 60));
        }

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
        assertThat(fixture.deadLetters.readValuesToList()).hasSize(15);
    }

    @Test
    void eventsKeyedByAnotherPlayerAreNotCountedEither() {
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            fixture.events.pipeInput(Q, TestEvents.bytes(TestEvents.shot(P, i + 1, T0 + i * 200L, true, true, 60)),
                    T0 + i * 200L);
        }

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
        assertThat(fixture.deadLetters.readValuesToList()).hasSize(15);
    }

    @Test
    void positionAndKillEventsNeverFeedTheAimbotWindows() {
        for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.position(P, i + 1, T0 + i * 50L, 0.01, 0.01));
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.kill(P, 100 + i, T0 + i * 200L, Q, true));
        }

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/DeadLetterTopologyTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.KeyValue;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class DeadLetterTopologyTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final String OTHER = TestEvents.player(2);
    private static final long T0 = TopologyFixture.START.toEpochMilli();

    private final TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture();

    @AfterEach
    void close() {
        fixture.close();
    }

    private static byte[] bytes(String s) {
        return s.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    @Test
    void garbageBytesGoToTheDeadLetterTopicWithReasonOffsetAndBase64Payload() {
        byte[] garbage = bytes("{\"broken\":");

        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, garbage, T0);

        List<KeyValue<String, DeadLetter>> letters = fixture.deadLetters.readKeyValuesToList();
        assertThat(letters).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(letters.get(0).key).isEqualTo(P);
        DeadLetter letter = letters.get(0).value;
        assertThat(letter.sourceTopic()).isEqualTo("match-events");
        assertThat(letter.partition()).isZero();
        assertThat(letter.offset()).isZero();
        assertThat(letter.key()).isEqualTo(P);
        assertThat(Base64.getDecoder().decode(letter.payloadBase64())).isEqualTo(garbage);
        assertThat(letter.error()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(letter.receivedAt()).isEqualTo(T0);
    }

    @Test
    void anInvalidEventGoesToTheDeadLetterTopic() {
        MatchEvent invalid = TestEvents.shot(P, 0, T0, true, false, 60); // seq 0 is never valid

        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, TestEvents.bytes(invalid), T0);

        DeadLetter letter = fixture.deadLetters.readValue();
        assertThat(letter.error()).startsWith("invalid event");
        assertThat(Base64.getDecoder().decode(letter.payloadBase64())).isEqualTo(TestEvents.bytes(invalid));
        assertThat(letter.offset()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void aKeyThatIsNotThePlayerIdGoesToTheDeadLetterTopic() {
        MatchEvent event = TestEvents.shot(P, 1, T0, true, false, 60);

        fixture.events.pipeInput(OTHER, TestEvents.bytes(event), T0);

        DeadLetter letter = fixture.deadLetters.readValue();
        assertThat(letter.error()).startsWith("key mismatch").contains(OTHER).contains(P);
        assertThat(letter.key()).isEqualTo(OTHER);
    }

    @Test
    void nullAndJsonNullPayloadsAreDeadLettered() {
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, (byte[]) null, T0);
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, bytes("null"), T0 + 1);

        List<DeadLetter> letters = fixture.deadLetters.readValuesToList();

        assertThat(letters).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(letters.get(0).error()).isEqualTo("null payload");
        assertThat(letters.get(0).payloadBase64()).isNull();
        assertThat(letters.get(1).error()).isEqualTo("payload is JSON null");
    }

    @Test
    void randomBytesAndUnknownEventTypesAreDeadLettered() {
        byte[] random = new byte[16];
        new java.util.Random(1).nextBytes(random);
        random[0] = (byte) 0xFF;

        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, random, T0);
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, bytes("{\"playerId\":\"" + P + "\",\"type\":\"TELEPORT\",\"seq\":1,\"ts\":5}"), T0 + 1);

        assertThat(fixture.deadLetters.readValuesToList()).hasSize(2);
    }

    @Test
    void validEventsAfterBadOnesAreStillProcessedAndNeverDeadLettered() {
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, bytes("{\"broken\":"), T0);
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, TestEvents.bytes(TestEvents.shot(P, 0, T0, true, false, 60)), T0 + 1);
        fixture.events.pipeInput(OTHER, TestEvents.bytes(TestEvents.shot(P, 1, T0, true, false, 60)), T0 + 2);
        for (int seq = 1; seq <= 5; seq++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, seq, T0 + 10L * seq, true, false, 60));
        }

        List<DeadLetter> letters = fixture.deadLetters.readValuesToList();

        assertThat(letters).hasSize(3);
        assertThat(letters).extracting(DeadLetter::offset).containsExactly(0L, 1L, 2L);
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.deadLettered.get()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.parsed.get()).isEqualTo(5);
    }

    @Test
    void positionKillAndShotEventsAllParse() {
        fixture.send(TestEvents.position(P, 1, T0, 0.1, 0.1));
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 2, T0 + 1, false, false, 200));
        fixture.send(TestEvents.kill(P, 3, T0 + 2, OTHER, true));

        assertThat(fixture.deadLetters.isEmpty()).isTrue();
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.parsed.get()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(EventType.values()).hasSize(3);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/DetectionRulesTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.TestDetection;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class DetectionRulesTest {

    private final DetectionRules rules = new DetectionRules(TestDetection.defaults());

    /** An aimbot window with the given counts and an exact average reaction time. */
    private static AimStats aim(int hits, int headshotHits, int shots, long avgReactionMs) {
        return new AimStats(shots, hits, headshotHits, avgReactionMs * shots);
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotNeedsTwelveHitsInclusive() {
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(11, 11, 11, 60))).isFalse();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(12, 12, 12, 60))).isTrue();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(13, 13, 13, 60))).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotReactionBoundaryIs140Inclusive() {
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(20, 20, 20, 140))).isTrue();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(20, 20, 20, 141))).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotHeadshotRateBoundaryIs062Inclusive() {
        // 31 of 50 hits is exactly 0.62, 30 of 50 is 0.60.
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(50, 31, 50, 60))).isTrue();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(50, 30, 50, 60))).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotNeedsAllThreeConditionsAtOnce() {
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(11, 11, 11, 141))).isFalse();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(50, 30, 50, 141))).isFalse();
        assertThat(rules.aimbotTriggered(aim(12, 0, 12, 60))).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotScoreIsHalfAtTheBoundary() {
        // Headshot rate 0.62 and reaction 140 are both exactly on the line.
        assertThat(rules.aimbotScore(aim(50, 31, 50, 140))).isEqualTo(0.5);
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotScoreIsOneWhenFarOver() {
        // Rate 0.95 or more and reaction 30 or less are both at the top of the range.
        assertThat(rules.aimbotScore(aim(100, 100, 100, 30))).isEqualTo(1.0);
        assertThat(rules.aimbotScore(aim(100, 100, 100, 10))).isEqualTo(1.0);
    }

    @Test
    void aimbotScoreFollowsTheFormula() {
        // rate 0.8235 -> (0.8235 - 0.62) / 0.33 = 0.6167; reaction 61.4 -> (140 - 61.4) / 110 = 0.7145
        AimStats stats = new AimStats(23, 17, 14, Math.round(61.4 * 23));
        double rate = 14 / 17.0;
        double reaction = Math.round(61.4 * 23) / 23.0;
        double expected = 0.5 + 0.25 * ((rate - 0.62) / 0.33) + 0.25 * ((140 - reaction) / 110);

        assertThat(rules.aimbotScore(stats)).isEqualTo(Math.round(expected * 1000) / 1000.0);
    }

    @Test
    void speedhackNeedsFortyMovingSamplesInclusive() {
        assertThat(rules.speedhackTriggered(new SpeedStats(39, 39, 11))).isFalse();
        assertThat(rules.speedhackTriggered(new SpeedStats(40, 40, 11))).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void speedhackOverspeedRatioBoundaryIs015Inclusive() {
        // 6 of 40 is exactly 0.15, 5 of 40 is 0.125.
        assertThat(rules.speedhackTriggered(new SpeedStats(40, 6, 8))).isTrue();
        assertThat(rules.speedhackTriggered(new SpeedStats(40, 5, 8))).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void speedhackScoreIsHalfAtTheBoundaryAndOneWhenEverySampleIsOverspeed() {
        assertThat(rules.speedhackScore(new SpeedStats(40, 6, 8))).isEqualTo(0.5);
        assertThat(rules.speedhackScore(new SpeedStats(60, 60, 11))).isEqualTo(1.0);
    }

    @Test
    void speedhackScoreFollowsTheFormula() {
        // ratio 0.5 -> 0.5 + 0.5 x (0.5 - 0.15) / 0.85 = 0.70588 -> 0.706
        assertThat(rules.speedhackScore(new SpeedStats(100, 50, 9))).isEqualTo(0.706);
    }

    @Test
    void evidenceRatesAreRoundedToFourDecimalsHalfUp() {
        Evidence aimEvidence = rules.aimbotEvidence(new AimStats(23, 17, 14, 1412)); // 14/17 = 0.82352..., 1412/23 = 61.3913...

        assertThat(aimEvidence.shots()).isEqualTo(23);
        assertThat(aimEvidence.hits()).isEqualTo(17);
        assertThat(aimEvidence.headshotHits()).isEqualTo(14);
        assertThat(aimEvidence.headshotRate()).isEqualTo(0.8235);
        assertThat(aimEvidence.avgReactionMs()).isEqualTo(61.3913);

        Evidence speedEvidence = rules.speedhackEvidence(new SpeedStats(3, 1, 10.123456));

        assertThat(speedEvidence.movingSamples()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(speedEvidence.overspeedSamples()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(speedEvidence.overspeedRatio()).isEqualTo(0.3333);
        assertThat(speedEvidence.maxSpeed()).isEqualTo(10.1235);
    }

    @Test
    void roundingIsHalfUp() {
        assertThat(DetectionRules.round(0.12345, 4)).isEqualTo(0.1235);
        assertThat(DetectionRules.round(0.2225, 3)).isEqualTo(0.223);
        assertThat(DetectionRules.round(2.5, 0)).isEqualTo(3.0);
    }

    @Test
    void clampKeepsValuesInZeroToOne() {
        assertThat(DetectionRules.clamp(-3)).isZero();
        assertThat(DetectionRules.clamp(0.4)).isEqualTo(0.4);
        assertThat(DetectionRules.clamp(7)).isEqualTo(1.0);
    }

    @Test
    void exposesTheSpeedSettingsForTheAggregator() {
        assertThat(rules.movingSpeedFloor()).isEqualTo(0.5);
        assertThat(rules.speedLimit()).isEqualTo(7.5);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/IntegrityTopologyTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class IntegrityTopologyTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final String Q = TestEvents.player(2);
    private static final long T0 = TopologyFixture.START.toEpochMilli();
    private static final Duration EMIT = Duration.ofSeconds(5);

    private final TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture();

    @AfterEach
    void close() {
        fixture.close();
    }

    private void sendSeqs(String player, long from, long to) {
        for (long seq = from; seq <= to; seq++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(player, seq, T0 + seq, true, false, 200));
        }
    }

    /** Emits the stats and returns the latest value per player. */
    private Map<String, IntegrityStats> emit() {
        fixture.advanceTime(EMIT);
        return fixture.integrity.readKeyValuesToMap();
    }

    @Test
    void sequenceOneToOneHundredGivesCountOneHundred() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 100);

        IntegrityStats stats = emit().get(P);

        assertThat(stats.count()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(stats.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(stats.duplicates()).isZero();
        assertThat(stats.gaps()).isZero();
        assertThat(stats.playerId()).isEqualTo(P);
    }

    @Test
    void repeatingASequenceNumberCountsOneDuplicateAndDoesNotCountTheEvent() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 100);
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 50, T0 + 500, true, false, 200));

        IntegrityStats stats = emit().get(P);

        assertThat(stats.duplicates()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(stats.count()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(stats.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(stats.gaps()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void repeatingTheLastSequenceNumberIsAlsoADuplicate() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 10);
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 10, T0 + 99, true, false, 200));

        assertThat(emit().get(P).duplicates()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void jumpingToSequenceOneHundredFiveCountsFourGaps() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 100);
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 105, T0 + 105, true, false, 200));

        IntegrityStats stats = emit().get(P);

        assertThat(stats.gaps()).isEqualTo(4);
        assertThat(stats.count()).isEqualTo(101);
        assertThat(stats.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(105);
        assertThat(stats.duplicates()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void aFirstEventThatIsNotSequenceOneCountsTheMissingOnesAsGaps() {
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 4, T0, true, false, 200));

        IntegrityStats stats = emit().get(P);

        assertThat(stats.gaps()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(stats.count()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(stats.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(4);
    }

    @Test
    void nothingIsEmittedUntilTheIntervalPasses() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 10);

        assertThat(fixture.integrity.isEmpty()).isTrue();

        fixture.advanceTime(Duration.ofSeconds(4));
        assertThat(fixture.integrity.isEmpty()).isTrue();

        fixture.advanceTime(Duration.ofSeconds(1));
        assertThat(fixture.integrity.isEmpty()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void everyPlayerIsReEmittedOnEveryIntervalEvenWhenNothingChanged() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 5);
        sendSeqs(Q, 1, 7);

        Map<String, IntegrityStats> first = emit();
        Map<String, IntegrityStats> second = emit();

        assertThat(first).containsOnlyKeys(P, Q);
        assertThat(second).containsOnlyKeys(P, Q);
        assertThat(second.get(P).count()).isEqualTo(5);
        assertThat(second.get(Q).count()).isEqualTo(7);
    }

    @Test
    void emittedStatsCarryTheCurrentClockAsUpdatedAt() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 3);

        long expected = T0 + EMIT.toMillis();

        assertThat(emit().get(P).updatedAt()).isEqualTo(expected);
    }

    @Test
    void playersAreTrackedIndependently() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 20);
        sendSeqs(Q, 1, 3);
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(Q, 3, T0 + 1000, true, false, 200));

        Map<String, IntegrityStats> stats = emit();

        assertThat(stats.get(P).duplicates()).isZero();
        assertThat(stats.get(Q).duplicates()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void positionAndKillEventsCountToo() {
        fixture.send(TestEvents.position(P, 1, T0, 0.1, 0.1));
        fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 2, T0 + 1, true, true, 60));
        fixture.send(TestEvents.kill(P, 3, T0 + 2, Q, true));

        assertThat(emit().get(P).count()).isEqualTo(3);
    }

    @Test
    void badRecordsNeverReachTheTracker() {
        sendSeqs(P, 1, 3);
        fixture.events.pipeInput(P, new byte[] {1, 2, 3}, T0 + 50);

        IntegrityStats stats = emit().get(P);

        assertThat(stats.count()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(stats.duplicates()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void applyCoversEveryCase() {
        IntegrityStats start = new IntegrityStats(P, 10, 10, 0, 0, 0);

        assertThat(IntegrityProcessor.apply(start, 11)).isEqualTo(new IntegrityStats(P, 11, 11, 0, 0, 0));
        assertThat(IntegrityProcessor.apply(start, 10)).isEqualTo(new IntegrityStats(P, 10, 10, 1, 0, 0));
        assertThat(IntegrityProcessor.apply(start, 3)).isEqualTo(new IntegrityStats(P, 10, 10, 1, 0, 0));
        assertThat(IntegrityProcessor.apply(start, 15)).isEqualTo(new IntegrityStats(P, 11, 15, 0, 4, 0));
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/ProfileJoinTopologyTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ProfileJoinTopologyTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final long T0 = TopologyFixture.START.toEpochMilli();

    private final TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture();

    @AfterEach
    void close() {
        fixture.close();
    }

    private void aimbotBurst() {
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, i + 1, T0 + i * 200L, true, true, 60));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void aFlagForAKnownPlayerCarriesUsernameAndSkillTier() {
        fixture.profiles.pipeInput(P, new PlayerProfile(P, "sim_0042", "IN", SkillTier.GOLD, T0), T0 - 1000);

        aimbotBurst();

        SuspiciousPlayer flag = fixture.flags.readValue();
        assertThat(flag.username()).isEqualTo("sim_0042");
        assertThat(flag.skillTier()).isEqualTo(SkillTier.GOLD);
        assertThat(flag.playerId()).isEqualTo(P);
    }

    @Test
    void aFlagForAnUnknownPlayerIsStillEmittedWithNulls() {
        aimbotBurst();

        SuspiciousPlayer flag = fixture.flags.readValue();
        assertThat(flag.playerId()).isEqualTo(P);
        assertThat(flag.username()).isNull();
        assertThat(flag.skillTier()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void theLatestProfileWins() {
        fixture.profiles.pipeInput(P, new PlayerProfile(P, "old_name", "IN", SkillTier.BRONZE, T0), T0 - 2000);
        fixture.profiles.pipeInput(P, new PlayerProfile(P, "new_name", "IN", SkillTier.PRO, T0), T0 - 1000);

        aimbotBurst();

        SuspiciousPlayer flag = fixture.flags.readValue();
        assertThat(flag.username()).isEqualTo("new_name");
        assertThat(flag.skillTier()).isEqualTo(SkillTier.PRO);
    }

    @Test
    void anotherPlayersProfileIsNotUsed() {
        String other = TestEvents.player(2);
        fixture.profiles.pipeInput(other, new PlayerProfile(other, "someone_else", "US", SkillTier.SILVER, T0),
                T0 - 1000);

        aimbotBurst();

        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValue().username()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    void withProfileLeavesTheFlagAloneWhenTheProfileIsNull() {
        FlagCandidate candidate = new FlagCandidate(P, dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule.AIMBOT, 0.7, 1, 2,
                null, null, null);

        assertThat(candidate.withProfile(null)).isSameAs(candidate);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/SpeedhackTopologyTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class SpeedhackTopologyTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);
    private static final long T0 = TopologyFixture.START.toEpochMilli();

    private final TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture();

    @AfterEach
    void close() {
        fixture.close();
    }

    /** One POSITION every 50 ms moving at {@code speed} units per second along x. Returns the next seq. */
    private long run(long firstSeq, long startTs, int count, double speed) {
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.position(P, firstSeq + i, startTs + i * 50L, speed * 0.05, 0));
        }
        return firstSeq + count;
    }

    @Test
    void sixtyPositionsAtSpeedElevenProduceOneSpeedhackFlag() {
        run(1, T0, 60, 11.0);

        List<SuspiciousPlayer> flags = fixture.flags.readValuesToList();

        assertThat(flags).hasSize(1);
        SuspiciousPlayer flag = flags.get(0);
        assertThat(flag.rule()).isEqualTo(Rule.SPEEDHACK);
        assertThat(flag.playerId()).isEqualTo(P);
        // The 40th moving sample (t = 1.95 s) first satisfies the rule, in the earliest window holding it.
        assertThat(flag.windowStart()).isEqualTo(T0 - 3000);
        assertThat(flag.windowEnd()).isEqualTo(T0 + 2000);
        assertThat(flag.evidence()).isEqualTo(Evidence.speedhack(40, 40, 1.0, 11.0));
        assertThat(flag.score()).isEqualTo(1.0);
        assertThat(fixture.metrics.speedhackFlags.get()).isEqualTo(1);
    }

    @Test
    void speedsBetweenSixPointNineAndSevenPointThreeDoNotFlag() {
        long seq = 1;
        double[] speeds = {6.9, 7.0, 7.1, 7.2, 7.3};
        for (int round = 0; round < 40; round++) {
            for (double speed : speeds) {
                seq = run(seq, T0 + (seq - 1) * 50L, 1, speed);
            }
        }

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void thirtyNineMovingSamplesAreNotEnough() {
        run(1, T0, 39, 11.0);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void idleSamplesDoNotCountAsMovingAndDoNotDiluteTheRatio() {
        // 60 idle samples, then 7 at speed 11 and 33 at a normal speed 4. That is 40 moving samples with 7 over the
        // limit (0.175 >= 0.15). If idle samples counted, the ratio would be 7 / 100 and nothing would fire.
        long seq = run(1, T0, 60, 0.0);
        seq = run(seq, T0 + 3000, 7, 11.0);
        run(seq, T0 + 3000 + 7 * 50L, 33, 4.0);

        List<SuspiciousPlayer> flags = fixture.flags.readValuesToList();

        assertThat(flags).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(flags.get(0).evidence().movingSamples()).isEqualTo(40);
        assertThat(flags.get(0).evidence().overspeedSamples()).isEqualTo(7);
    }

    @Test
    void onlyIdleSamplesNeverFlag() {
        run(1, T0, 400, 0.0);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void aSecondRunInsideTheCooldownDoesNotFlagAgain() {
        long seq = run(1, T0, 60, 11.0);
        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValuesToList()).hasSize(1);

        fixture.advanceTime(java.time.Duration.ofSeconds(20));
        run(seq, T0 + 20_000, 60, 11.0);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.isEmpty()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void anAimbotFlagDoesNotHideASpeedhackFlag() {
        // The cooldown key is playerId|rule, so one rule's flag never suppresses the other's.
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            fixture.send(TestEvents.shot(P, 1000 + i, T0 + i * 200L, true, true, 60));
        }
        run(1, T0, 60, 11.0);

        assertThat(fixture.flags.readValuesToList()).extracting(SuspiciousPlayer::rule)
                .containsExactlyInAnyOrder(Rule.AIMBOT, Rule.SPEEDHACK);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/StatsTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerdes;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Accumulation and rates of {@link AimStats} and {@link SpeedStats}. */
class StatsTest {

    private static final String P = TestEvents.player(1);

    private static MatchEvent shot(boolean hit, boolean headshot, int reactionMs) {
        return TestEvents.shot(P, 1, 1000, hit, headshot, reactionMs);
    }

    /** A POSITION event moving at {@code speed} units per second (dtMs is 50). */
    private static MatchEvent moving(double speed) {
        return TestEvents.position(P, 1, 1000, speed * 0.05, 0);
    }

    @Test
    void aimStatsAccumulateEveryShotButHitsAndHeadshotsOnlyWhenSet() {
        AimStats stats = AimStats.empty()
                .add(shot(true, true, 60))
                .add(shot(true, false, 80))
                .add(shot(false, false, 100));

        assertThat(stats).isEqualTo(new AimStats(3, 2, 1, 240));
        assertThat(stats.avgReactionMs()).isEqualTo(80.0);
        assertThat(stats.headshotRate()).isEqualTo(0.5);
    }

    @Test
    void aimStatsAreImmutable() {
        AimStats empty = AimStats.empty();

        empty.add(shot(true, true, 60));

        assertThat(empty).isEqualTo(new AimStats(0, 0, 0, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void aimRatesAreZeroWithoutDenominators() {
        assertThat(AimStats.empty().avgReactionMs()).isZero();
        assertThat(AimStats.empty().headshotRate()).isZero();
        assertThat(AimStats.empty().add(shot(false, false, 200)).headshotRate()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void aimStatsTreatMissingFieldsAsZero() {
        MatchEvent odd = new MatchEvent("e", "r", P, "m", 1, dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType.SHOT, 1,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null);

        assertThat(AimStats.empty().add(odd)).isEqualTo(new AimStats(1, 0, 0, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void speedStatsClassifyMovingAndOverspeedSamples() {
        SpeedStats stats = SpeedStats.empty()
                .add(moving(0.0), 0.5, 7.5)    // idle
                .add(moving(0.49), 0.5, 7.5)   // still idle
                .add(moving(0.5), 0.5, 7.5)    // moving, boundary inclusive
                .add(moving(7.5), 0.5, 7.5)    // moving, not overspeed (limit exclusive)
                .add(moving(11.0), 0.5, 7.5);  // overspeed

        assertThat(stats.movingSamples()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(stats.overspeedSamples()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(stats.maxSpeed()).isEqualTo(11.0, org.assertj.core.data.Offset.offset(1e-9));
        assertThat(stats.overspeedRatio()).isEqualTo(1 / 3.0);
    }

    @Test
    void speedIsDistanceOverTimeFromBothAxes() {
        MatchEvent diagonal = TestEvents.position(P, 1, 1000, 0.3, 0.4); // 0.5 units in 50 ms = 10 units/s

        assertThat(SpeedStats.speedOf(diagonal)).isEqualTo(10.0, org.assertj.core.data.Offset.offset(1e-9));
    }

    @Test
    void speedRatioIsZeroWhenNothingMoved() {
        assertThat(SpeedStats.empty().overspeedRatio()).isZero();
        assertThat(SpeedStats.empty().add(moving(0), 0.5, 7.5).overspeedRatio()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void statsSurviveTheJsonSerdeUsedByTheWindowStores() {
        AimStats aim = new AimStats(5, 4, 3, 300);
        SpeedStats speed = new SpeedStats(40, 12, 9.25);

        var aimSerde = JsonSerdes.of(AimStats.class);
        var speedSerde = JsonSerdes.of(SpeedStats.class);

        assertThat(aimSerde.deserialize("t", aimSerde.serialize("t", aim))).isEqualTo(aim);
        assertThat(speedSerde.deserialize("t", speedSerde.serialize("t", speed))).isEqualTo(speed);
        assertThat(new String(aimSerde.serialize("t", aim))).doesNotContain("avgReactionMs", "headshotRate");
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/TopologyFixture.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerdes;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.DeadLetter;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.MutableClock;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.TestEvents;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.DetectionProperties;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.TestDetection;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.ByteArraySerializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.StreamsBuilder;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.StreamsConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.TestInputTopic;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.TestOutputTopic;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.Topology;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.TopologyTestDriver;

/**
 * Runs the real {@link TelemetryTopology} in memory with controlled time and no broker. Event time starts at
 * {@link #START}; the injected {@link MutableClock} starts there too.
 */
public final class TopologyFixture implements AutoCloseable {

    public static final Instant START = Instant.parse("2026-11-17T10:00:00Z");

    /** Counts what the topology reports, so tests can assert on it. */
    public static final class RecordingMetrics implements TopologyMetrics {
        public final AtomicInteger parsed = new AtomicInteger();
        public final AtomicInteger deadLettered = new AtomicInteger();
        public final AtomicInteger aimbotFlags = new AtomicInteger();
        public final AtomicInteger speedhackFlags = new AtomicInteger();

        @Override
        public void parsed() {
            parsed.incrementAndGet();
        }

        @Override
        public void deadLettered() {
            deadLettered.incrementAndGet();
        }

        @Override
        public void flagged(Rule rule) {
            (rule == Rule.AIMBOT ? aimbotFlags : speedhackFlags).incrementAndGet();
        }
    }

    public final MutableClock clock = new MutableClock(START);
    public final RecordingMetrics metrics = new RecordingMetrics();
    public final Topology topology;
    public final TopologyTestDriver driver;
    public final TestInputTopic<String, byte[]> events;
    public final TestInputTopic<String, PlayerProfile> profiles;
    public final TestOutputTopic<String, DeadLetter> deadLetters;
    public final TestOutputTopic<String, IntegrityStats> integrity;
    public final TestOutputTopic<String, SuspiciousPlayer> flags;

    public TopologyFixture() {
        this(TestDetection.defaults());
    }

    public TopologyFixture(DetectionProperties props) {
        StreamsBuilder builder = new StreamsBuilder();
        new TelemetryTopology(props, clock, metrics).addTo(builder);
        topology = builder.build();

        Properties p = new Properties();
        p.put(StreamsConfig.APPLICATION_ID_CONFIG, "topology-test");
        p.put(StreamsConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "dummy:9092");
        p.put(StreamsConfig.STATESTORE_CACHE_MAX_BYTES_CONFIG, 0); // emit on every update
        driver = new TopologyTestDriver(topology, p, START);

        events = driver.createInputTopic(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, new StringSerializer(), new ByteArraySerializer());
        profiles = driver.createInputTopic(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, new StringSerializer(),
                JsonSerdes.profile().serializer());
        deadLetters = driver.createOutputTopic(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT, new StringDeserializer(),
                JsonSerdes.deadLetter().deserializer());
        integrity = driver.createOutputTopic(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, new StringDeserializer(),
                JsonSerdes.integrity().deserializer());
        flags = driver.createOutputTopic(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, new StringDeserializer(),
                JsonSerdes.suspicious().deserializer());
    }

    /** Pipes one event, keyed by its player id, with event time {@code ts}. */
    public void send(MatchEvent event) {
        events.pipeInput(event.playerId(), TestEvents.bytes(event), event.ts());
    }

    public void sendAll(List<MatchEvent> batch) {
        batch.forEach(this::send);
    }

    /** Moves both the injected clock and the driver's wall clock, which triggers wall-clock punctuators. */
    public void advanceTime(java.time.Duration duration) {
        clock.advance(duration);
        driver.advanceWallClockTime(duration);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        driver.close();
    }
}
```

`telemetry-detector/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/detector/topology/TopologyShapeTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.detector.topology;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import org.apache.kafka.streams.TopologyDescription;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** Pins the shape of the topology: what it reads, what it writes, and that it never touches the ground truth. */
class TopologyShapeTest {

    private static Set<String> sourceTopics(TopologyDescription description) {
        Set<String> topics = new HashSet<>();
        for (TopologyDescription.Subtopology sub : description.subtopologies()) {
            for (TopologyDescription.Node node : sub.nodes()) {
                if (node instanceof TopologyDescription.Source source && source.topicSet() != null) {
                    topics.addAll(source.topicSet());
                }
            }
        }
        return topics;
    }

    private static Set<String> sinkTopics(TopologyDescription description) {
        Set<String> topics = new HashSet<>();
        for (TopologyDescription.Subtopology sub : description.subtopologies()) {
            for (TopologyDescription.Node node : sub.nodes()) {
                if (node instanceof TopologyDescription.Sink sink && sink.topic() != null) {
                    topics.add(sink.topic());
                }
            }
        }
        return topics;
    }

    @Test
    void readsOnlyEventsProfilesAndTheInternalFlagsRepartitionTopic() {
        try (TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture()) {
            assertThat(sourceTopics(fixture.topology.describe())).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
                    Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, TelemetryTopology.FLAGS_REPARTITION + "-repartition");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void addsNoRepartitionTopicInFrontOfTheWindows() {
        // KStream#process marks its output as key-changing; grouping it would rewrite every event through the broker.
        try (TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture()) {
            String description = fixture.topology.describe().toString();

            assertThat(description).doesNotContain("aimbot-windows-repartition")
                    .doesNotContain("speedhack-windows-repartition");
        }
    }

    @Test
    void publishesFlagsIntegrityStatsAndDeadLettersOnly() {
        try (TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture()) {
            assertThat(sinkTopics(fixture.topology.describe())).contains(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS,
                    Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void neverMentionsTheGroundTruthTopic() {
        try (TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture()) {
            assertThat(fixture.topology.describe().toString()).doesNotContain(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH);
            assertThat(sourceTopics(fixture.topology.describe())).doesNotContain(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH);
            assertThat(sinkTopics(fixture.topology.describe())).doesNotContain(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void writesDeadLettersToTheDeadLetterTopic() {
        try (TopologyFixture fixture = new TopologyFixture()) {
            assertThat(sinkTopics(fixture.topology.describe())).contains(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS_DLT);
        }
    }
}
```
