`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/SimulatorProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator;

import java.time.Duration;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;

/**
 * Every simulator tunable, bound from {@code telemetry.sim.*}. Startup fails when a validation rule is broken.
 *
 * <p>All rules are checked in the compact constructor instead of with Bean Validation annotations.
 * Hibernate Validator has no {@code @Positive} for {@link Duration}, and the cross-field rules need a constructor
 * anyway, so one mechanism keeps the rules in one place.
 */
@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.sim")
public record SimulatorProperties(
        long seed,
        int players,
        Duration duration,
        int matchSize,
        Duration matchDuration,
        int tickHz,
        double shotsPerSecond,
        double killChancePerHit,
        int shardThreads,
        double cheaterRatio,
        double proRatio,
        Duration cheatStartMin,
        Duration cheatStartMax,
        double malformedRatio) {

    public SimulatorProperties {
        positive(players, "players");
        positive(duration, "duration");
        // A match needs two players, because every kill needs a victim other than the killer.
        if (matchSize < 2) {
            throw invalid("match-size must be at least 2");
        }
        positive(matchDuration, "match-duration");
        positive(tickHz, "tick-hz");
        // dtMs = 1000 / tick-hz must be a whole number of milliseconds.
        if (1000 % tickHz != 0) {
            throw invalid("tick-hz must divide 1000 so a tick is a whole number of milliseconds");
        }
        positive(shotsPerSecond, "shots-per-second");
        // A probability that must be above zero, or no kill would ever happen.
        positive(killChancePerHit, "kill-chance-per-hit");
        ratio(killChancePerHit, "kill-chance-per-hit");
        positive(shardThreads, "shard-threads");
        ratio(cheaterRatio, "cheater-ratio");
        ratio(proRatio, "pro-ratio");
        ratio(malformedRatio, "malformed-ratio");
        if (cheaterRatio + proRatio > 1.0) {
            throw invalid("cheater-ratio + pro-ratio must not exceed 1.0");
        }
        positive(cheatStartMin, "cheat-start-min");
        positive(cheatStartMax, "cheat-start-max");
        if (cheatStartMax.compareTo(cheatStartMin) <= 0) {
            throw invalid("cheat-start-max must be greater than cheat-start-min");
        }
        if (cheatStartMax.compareTo(duration) >= 0) {
            throw invalid("cheat-start-max must be less than duration");
        }
        int roomsPerRound = matchSize * shardThreads;
        if (players % roomsPerRound != 0) {
            throw invalid("players (" + players + ") must be a multiple of match-size x shard-threads ("
                    + roomsPerRound + ")");
        }
    }

    /** Milliseconds per tick; also the {@code dtMs} of every POSITION event (1000 / tick-hz). */
    public int tickMs() {
        return 1000 / tickHz;
    }

    private static void positive(Duration value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isZero() || value.isNegative()) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be positive");
        }
    }

    private static void positive(double value, String name) {
        if (!(value > 0)) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be positive");
        }
    }

    private static void ratio(double value, String name) {
        if (!(value >= 0.0 && value <= 1.0)) {
            throw invalid(name + " must be between 0.0 and 1.0");
        }
    }

    private static IllegalArgumentException invalid(String message) {
        return new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.sim." + message);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/arena/ArenaClient.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.arena;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.client.SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.client.HttpClientErrorException;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;

/**
 * Registers simulated players with Arena. Logs in as the service account lazily, caches the access token, and logs
 * in again once on any 401. A 409 {@code USERNAME_TAKEN} means the player already exists, so its id is looked up.
 */
@Component
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = "telemetry.arena.enabled", havingValue = "true")
public class ArenaClient {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ArenaClient.class);

    private final ArenaProperties props;
    private final RestClient http;
    private volatile String token;

    public ArenaClient(ArenaProperties props) {
        this.props = props;
        SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory factory = new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
        factory.setConnectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2));
        factory.setReadTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10));
        this.http = RestClient.builder().baseUrl(props.baseUrl()).requestFactory(factory).build();
    }

    /** A service token, from the cache unless there is none yet. */
    String serviceToken() {
        String cached = token;
        return cached != null ? cached : login();
    }

    private synchronized String login() {
        String body = http.post().uri("/api/v1/auth/login").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .body(json(Map.of("username", props.serviceUsername(), "password", props.servicePassword())))
                .retrieve().body(String.class);
        token = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readTree(body).path("accessToken").asString();
        if (token == null || token.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Arena login returned no accessToken");
        }
        return token;
    }

    /** Registers the player and returns Arena's id for it. Safe to repeat: an existing username is looked up. */
    public String register(PlayerState player) {
        Map<String, Object> request = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        request.put("username", player.username());
        request.put("email", player.username() + "@sim.fairplay.local");
        request.put("password", props.playerPassword());
        request.put("country", player.country());
        try {
            String body = http.post().uri("/api/v1/auth/register").contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                    .body(json(request)).retrieve().body(String.class);
            return JsonSerde.MAPPER.readTree(body).path("id").asString();
        } catch (HttpClientErrorException.Conflict e) {
            log.debug("{} already exists in Arena, looking it up", player.username());
            return lookup(player.username());
        }
    }

    /** {@code GET /api/v1/players?q=<username>&size=5}, taking the exact (case-insensitive) username match. */
    private String lookup(String username) {
        String body = authorized(() -> http.get()
                .uri(uri -> uri.path("/api/v1/players").queryParam("q", username).queryParam("size", 5).build())
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + serviceToken())
                .retrieve().body(String.class));
        for (JsonNode player : JsonSerde.MAPPER.readTree(body).path("content")) {
            if (username.equalsIgnoreCase(player.path("username").asString())) {
                return player.path("id").asString();
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("Arena says " + username + " is taken but the lookup found no such player");
    }

    /** Runs {@code call}; on a 401 it logs in again and tries once more. */
    private <T> T authorized(java.util.function.Supplier<T> call) {
        try {
            return call.get();
        } catch (HttpClientErrorException.Unauthorized e) {
            token = null;
            return call.get();
        }
    }

    private static String json(Object value) {
        return JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsString(value);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/arena/ArenaPlayerRegistrar.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.arena;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.Future;
import java.util.concurrent.Semaphore;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import tools.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;

/**
 * Makes sure every simulated player exists in Arena and returns Arena's id for each username. Registration runs on
 * virtual threads limited by a semaphore, because Arena hashes passwords with bcrypt and is slow per request.
 *
 * <p>The mapping is saved to {@code mapping-file} (a JSON object {@code {"sim_0001": "<arena uuid>"}}). A later run
 * loads it and skips players already mapped. If any player cannot be mapped the run must not start, so this throws.
 */
@Component
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = "telemetry.arena.enabled", havingValue = "true")
public class ArenaPlayerRegistrar {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ArenaPlayerRegistrar.class);
    private static final TypeReference<TreeMap<String, String>> MAPPING = new TypeReference<>() {
    };

    private final ArenaClient client;
    private final Path mappingFile;
    private final int concurrency;

    public ArenaPlayerRegistrar(ArenaClient client, ArenaProperties props) {
        this.client = client;
        this.mappingFile = Path.of(props.mappingFile());
        this.concurrency = props.registerConcurrency();
    }

    /** Username to Arena id for every player. Throws when any player could not be registered. */
    public Map<String, String> register(List<PlayerState> players) throws InterruptedException {
        Map<String, String> mapping = new ConcurrentHashMap<>(load());
        List<PlayerState> todo = players.stream().filter(p -> !mapping.containsKey(p.username())).toList();
        log.info("Arena registration: {} players already mapped, {} to register", players.size() - todo.size(), todo.size());

        Semaphore permits = new Semaphore(concurrency);
        List<Throwable> failures = new ArrayList<>();
        try (ExecutorService executor = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
            List<Future<?>> futures = new ArrayList<>(todo.size());
            for (PlayerState player : todo) {
                futures.add(executor.submit(() -> {
                    permits.acquire();
                    try {
                        mapping.put(player.username(), client.register(player));
                    } finally {
                        permits.release();
                    }
                    return null;
                }));
            }
            for (Future<?> future : futures) {
                try {
                    future.get();
                } catch (ExecutionException e) {
                    failures.add(e.getCause());
                }
            }
        }
        // Save what worked even when something failed, so a rerun only retries the rest.
        save(mapping);
        if (!failures.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(failures.size() + " of " + todo.size()
                    + " players could not be registered with Arena; first error: " + failures.get(0), failures.get(0));
        }
        return Map.copyOf(mapping);
    }

    private Map<String, String> load() {
        if (!Files.exists(mappingFile)) {
            return Map.of();
        }
        try {
            return JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(Files.readString(mappingFile, StandardCharsets.UTF_8), MAPPING);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot read " + mappingFile, e);
        }
    }

    private void save(Map<String, String> mapping) {
        try {
            Path parent = mappingFile.toAbsolutePath().getParent();
            if (parent != null) {
                Files.createDirectories(parent);
            }
            Files.writeString(mappingFile, JsonSerde.MAPPER.writerWithDefaultPrettyPrinter()
                    .writeValueAsString(new TreeMap<>(mapping)), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot write " + mappingFile, e);
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/arena/ArenaProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.arena;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

/**
 * Arena (Project 1) connection settings, bound from {@code telemetry.arena.*}. The service password comes from
 * {@code TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD} and is never hard-coded; when Arena is enabled a blank one fails startup.
 *
 * <p>DECISION: the spec uses {@code ArenaProperties} without defining it. The fields are exactly the keys of the
 * simulator YAML in spec section 6. The detector has its own record of the same name with the keys it needs
 * (retry settings instead of registration settings), because the two apps share no module for it.
 */
@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.arena")
public record ArenaProperties(
        @DefaultValue("false") boolean enabled,
        @DefaultValue("http://localhost:8080") String baseUrl,
        @DefaultValue("telemetry-sim") String serviceUsername,
        @DefaultValue("") String servicePassword,
        @DefaultValue("sim-player-pass") String playerPassword,
        @DefaultValue("16") int registerConcurrency,
        @DefaultValue("./sim-players.json") String mappingFile) {

    public ArenaProperties {
        if (enabled && (servicePassword == null || servicePassword.isBlank())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "telemetry.arena.service-password must not be blank when Arena is enabled: set TELEMETRY_SERVICE_PASSWORD");
        }
        if (baseUrl == null || baseUrl.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.base-url must not be blank");
        }
        if (registerConcurrency < 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.register-concurrency must be positive");
        }
        if (mappingFile == null || mappingFile.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.mapping-file must not be blank");
        }
        // Arena requires 8 to 72 characters for a password.
        if (playerPassword == null || playerPassword.length() < 8 || playerPassword.length() > 72) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.arena.player-password must be 8 to 72 characters");
        }
    }
}
```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/engine/EventFactory.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.engine;

import static java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.random.RandomGenerator;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.SimulatorProperties;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.match.MatchRoom;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.BehaviorProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;

/**
 * Produces the events of one tick for one player, in this order: exactly one POSITION, then zero or more SHOTs,
 * each followed by at most one KILL. All events in a tick share {@code ts = clock.millis()}, and each takes the
 * player's next sequence number.
 */
public final class EventFactory {

    private static final double MEASUREMENT_NOISE_SD = 0.15;

    private final SimulatorProperties props;
    private final String runId;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final double meanShotsPerTick;
    private final int dtMs;

    public EventFactory(SimulatorProperties props, String runId, Clock clock) {
        this.props = props;
        this.runId = runId;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.dtMs = props.tickMs();
        this.meanShotsPerTick = props.shotsPerSecond() / props.tickHz();
    }

    /** Plays one tick for {@code player}, recording shots and kills in {@code room} and passing events to {@code sink}. */
    public void tick(PlayerState player, MatchRoom room, RandomGenerator rng, Consumer<MatchEvent> sink) {
        long now = clock.millis();
        BehaviorProfile profile = player.profileAt(now);

        sink.accept(position(player, room, profile.move(), now, rng));

        BehaviorProfile.Aim aim = profile.aim();
        int shots = poisson(meanShotsPerTick, rng);
        for (int i = 0; i < shots; i++) {
            boolean hit = rng.nextDouble() < aim.hitChance();
            boolean headshot = hit && rng.nextDouble() < aim.headshotChance();
            int reactionMs = (int) Math.max(aim.reactionFloor(),
                    Math.round(aim.reactionMean() + aim.reactionSd() * rng.nextGaussian()));
            room.recordShot(player, hit, reactionMs);
            long shotSeq = player.nextSeq();
            sink.accept(new MatchEvent(eventId(player.playerId(), shotSeq), runId, player.playerId(),
                    room.externalId(), shotSeq, EventType.SHOT, now,
                    hit, headshot, reactionMs, null, null, null, null));

            if (hit && rng.nextDouble() < props.killChancePerHit()) {
                PlayerState victim = room.randomOpponent(player, rng);
                room.recordKill(player, victim, headshot);
                long killSeq = player.nextSeq();
                sink.accept(new MatchEvent(eventId(player.playerId(), killSeq), runId, player.playerId(),
                        room.externalId(), killSeq, EventType.KILL, now,
                        null, headshot, null, null, null, null, victim.playerId()));
            }
        }
    }

    private MatchEvent position(PlayerState player, MatchRoom room, BehaviorProfile.Move move, long now,
                                RandomGenerator rng) {
        double dx = 0;
        double dy = 0;
        if (rng.nextDouble() < move.moveChance()) {
            double base = move.speedMin() + rng.nextDouble() * (move.speedMax() - move.speedMin());
            double speed = base * move.speedMultiplier();
            double measured = Math.max(0, speed + MEASUREMENT_NOISE_SD * rng.nextGaussian());
            double angle = rng.nextDouble() * 2 * Math.PI;
            dx = measured * Math.cos(angle) * dtMs / 1000.0;
            dy = measured * Math.sin(angle) * dtMs / 1000.0;
        }
        long seq = player.nextSeq();
        return new MatchEvent(eventId(player.playerId(), seq), runId, player.playerId(),
                room.externalId(), seq, EventType.POSITION, now,
                null, null, null, dx, dy, dtMs, null);
    }

    /** Knuth's algorithm. Fine for the small means used here (0.225). */
    static int poisson(double mean, RandomGenerator rng) {
        double limit = Math.exp(-mean);
        int k = 0;
        double p = 1.0;
        do {
            k++;
            p *= rng.nextDouble();
        } while (p > limit);
        return k - 1;
    }

    /**
     * The event id is a name-based UUID of run, player and seq, not {@code UUID.randomUUID()}, so every
     * value the simulator emits is a function of the seed and the run id.
     */
    private String eventId(String playerId, long seq) {
        return UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes((runId + ":" + playerId + ":" + seq).getBytes(UTF_8)).toString();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/engine/MalformedInjector.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.engine;

import static java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8;

import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.random.RandomGenerator;

/**
 * Decides when to send a deliberately bad record next to a real event, and what it looks like. Three kinds rotate:
 * truncated JSON, valid JSON that is missing required fields, and 16 random bytes. Bad records carry no seq and are
 * not counted in the manifest.
 */
public final class MalformedInjector {

    static final byte[] TRUNCATED_JSON = "{\"broken\":".getBytes(UTF_8);
    static final byte[] MISSING_FIELDS = "{\"playerId\":\"not-a-uuid\",\"type\":\"SHOT\"}".getBytes(UTF_8);
    static final int KINDS = 3;
    static final int RANDOM_BYTES = 16;

    private final double ratio;
    private int nextKind;

    public MalformedInjector(double ratio) {
        this.ratio = ratio;
    }

    /** With probability {@code malformed-ratio}, the next bad payload in the rotation, otherwise empty. */
    public Optional<byte[]> maybeNext(RandomGenerator rng) {
        if (rng.nextDouble() >= ratio) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return Optional.of(payload(nextKind++ % KINDS, rng));
    }

    static byte[] payload(int kind, RandomGenerator rng) {
        return switch (kind) {
            case 0 -> TRUNCATED_JSON.clone();
            case 1 -> MISSING_FIELDS.clone();
            default -> {
                byte[] bytes = new byte[RANDOM_BYTES];
                rng.nextBytes(bytes);
                // 0xFF can never start valid UTF-8 or JSON, so random bytes are guaranteed to be garbage.
                bytes[0] = (byte) 0xFF;
                yield bytes;
            }
        };
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/engine/ShardWorker.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.engine;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.SplittableRandom;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.SimulatorProperties;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.match.MatchRoom;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.match.MatchSummaryBuilder;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.output.EventPublisher;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

/**
 * One worker thread's share of the simulation: it owns a fixed set of rooms and only their players, with its own
 * {@code SplittableRandom(seed + shardIndex)} and its own {@link MalformedInjector}, so no state is shared between
 * threads except the global match counter.
 *
 * <p>DECISION: every room of a shard starts and ends together. When the first room is over, the whole shard's
 * matches finish, their summaries are published, and the shard's players are reshuffled into new rooms. The spec
 * says a worker "reshuffles its own players into new rooms" and does not say how to do that while rooms end at
 * different times; ending them together keeps the reshuffle trivial.
 */
public final class ShardWorker implements Runnable {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ShardWorker.class);

    private final int shardIndex;
    private final SimulatorProperties props;
    private final String runId;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final AtomicInteger matchCounter;
    private final EventPublisher publisher;
    private final EventFactory eventFactory;
    private final SplittableRandom rng;
    private final MalformedInjector injector;
    private final Consumer<MatchEvent> sink;
    private final long matchDurationMs;
    private final List<PlayerState> players;
    private List<MatchRoom> rooms;

    public ShardWorker(int shardIndex, List<PlayerState> players, SimulatorProperties props, String runId,
                       Clock clock, AtomicInteger matchCounter, EventPublisher publisher) {
        this.shardIndex = shardIndex;
        this.players = new ArrayList<>(players);
        this.props = props;
        this.runId = runId;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.matchCounter = matchCounter;
        this.publisher = publisher;
        this.eventFactory = new EventFactory(props, runId, clock);
        this.rng = new SplittableRandom(props.seed() + shardIndex);
        this.injector = new MalformedInjector(props.malformedRatio());
        this.matchDurationMs = props.matchDuration().toMillis();
        // After each real event, with probability malformed-ratio, also send one bad record for the same player.
        this.sink = event -> {
            publisher.publish(event);
            injector.maybeNext(rng).ifPresent(bytes -> publisher.publishMalformed(event.playerId(), bytes));
        };
        this.rooms = newRooms(clock.millis());
    }

    /**
     * Splits the whole population into rooms of {@code match-size} in index order and deals room {@code r} to
     * shard {@code r % shards}, so worker {@code k} owns rooms k, k+shards, k+2*shards and so on.
     */
    public static List<List<PlayerState>> partition(List<PlayerState> population, SimulatorProperties props) {
        List<List<PlayerState>> shards = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int k = 0; k < props.shardThreads(); k++) {
            shards.add(new ArrayList<>());
        }
        int rooms = population.size() / props.matchSize();
        for (int r = 0; r < rooms; r++) {
            shards.get(r % props.shardThreads())
                    .addAll(population.subList(r * props.matchSize(), (r + 1) * props.matchSize()));
        }
        return shards;
    }

    /**
     * What the scheduler calls. An exception thrown from a task given to {@code scheduleAtFixedRate} silently
     * cancels every later run, which would freeze this shard's players without any visible error, so each tick is
     * guarded and the next one still happens.
     */
    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            tick();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            log.error("Shard {} tick failed", shardIndex, t);
        }
    }

    /** One tick for every player in the shard. Public so tests can drive it with a mutable clock. */
    public void tick() {
        long now = clock.millis();
        if (rooms.get(0).isOver(now, matchDurationMs)) {
            endRound(now);
        }
        for (MatchRoom room : rooms) {
            for (PlayerState player : room.members()) {
                eventFactory.tick(player, room, rng, sink);
            }
        }
    }

    public List<MatchRoom> rooms() {
        return rooms;
    }

    private void endRound(long now) {
        for (MatchRoom room : rooms) {
            publisher.publishSummary(MatchSummaryBuilder.build(room, now));
        }
        rooms = newRooms(now);
    }

    private List<MatchRoom> newRooms(long startedAtMs) {
        List<PlayerState> shuffled = new ArrayList<>(players);
        Collections.shuffle(shuffled, rng);
        List<MatchRoom> next = new ArrayList<>(shuffled.size() / props.matchSize());
        for (int i = 0; i + props.matchSize() <= shuffled.size(); i += props.matchSize()) {
            next.add(new MatchRoom(matchCounter.incrementAndGet(), runId, startedAtMs,
                    shuffled.subList(i, i + props.matchSize())));
        }
        return next;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/engine/SimulationEngine.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.engine;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.SimulatorProperties;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.arena.ArenaPlayerRegistrar;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.output.EventPublisher;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.output.ManifestTracker;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PopulationFactory;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Gauge;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent;
import org.springframework.context.event.EventListener;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Runs one simulation: builds the population, publishes profiles and ground truth, plays the matches on the shard
 * workers for {@code duration}, then publishes the manifest. The run happens on a platform thread named
 * {@code simulation-main}, so the app stays responsive and the actuator stays up. When the run ends the app keeps
 * running, so Prometheus can still scrape it.
 *
 * <p>DECISION: {@code telemetry.sim.autostart} (default true) switches the automatic start off. It is a plain
 * property read by {@code @ConditionalOnProperty}, not a field of {@link SimulatorProperties}, so no constructor
 * call changes. Tests that want a Spring context without a run set it to false.
 */
@Component
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = "telemetry.sim.autostart", havingValue = "true", matchIfMissing = true)
public class SimulationEngine {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(SimulationEngine.class);
    private static final long RATE_LOG_INTERVAL_MS = 10_000;
    private static final int RUN_ID_BYTES = 3;

    private final SimulatorProperties props;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final EventPublisher publisher;
    private final ManifestTracker manifest;
    private final MeterRegistry registry;
    private final Optional<ArenaPlayerRegistrar> registrar;
    private final CompletableFuture<RunInfo> completion = new CompletableFuture<>();
    private final List<ExecutorService> executors = new ArrayList<>();
    private volatile Thread mainThread;

    public SimulationEngine(SimulatorProperties props, Clock clock, EventPublisher publisher,
                            ManifestTracker manifest, MeterRegistry registry,
                            Optional<ArenaPlayerRegistrar> registrar) {
        this.props = props;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.publisher = publisher;
        this.manifest = manifest;
        this.registry = registry;
        this.registrar = registrar;
    }

    @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
    public void start() {
        Thread thread = new Thread(this::runAndReport, "simulation-main");
        mainThread = thread;
        thread.start();
    }

    /** Completes with the {@link RunInfo} when the run is finished, or exceptionally when it failed. */
    public CompletableFuture<RunInfo> completion() {
        return completion;
    }

    @PreDestroy
    void stop() {
        Thread thread = mainThread;
        if (thread != null) {
            thread.interrupt();
        }
        shutdownExecutors();
    }

    private void runAndReport() {
        try {
            completion.complete(run());
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            log.warn("Simulation interrupted before it finished");
            completion.completeExceptionally(e);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Simulation failed", e);
            completion.completeExceptionally(e);
        }
    }

    /** The whole run, synchronously. Steps follow spec section 8, "Run lifecycle". */
    RunInfo run() throws Exception {
        String runId = "r-" + randomHex();
        PopulationFactory factory = new PopulationFactory(props);

        // DECISION: with Arena enabled, players are registered BEFORE the run clock starts. Registration can take a
        // minute (bcrypt), and cheat start times are offsets from runStartedAt, so starting the clock first would
        // let cheats begin before anyone sends an event. Usernames do not depend on the start time, so a first
        // population is only used to know whom to register, and the real one is built after registration.
        Map<String, String> arenaIds = registrar.isPresent()
                ? registrar.get().register(factory.create(clock.millis()))
                : Map.of();

        long runStartedAt = clock.millis();
        log.info("SIMULATION START runId={} players={} duration={} seed={} arena={}", runId, props.players(),
                props.duration(), props.seed(), registrar.isPresent());

        List<PlayerState> population = factory.create(runStartedAt);
        if (registrar.isPresent()) {
            // Arena's id replaces the made-up one, so profiles, ground truth, events and match summaries all use it.
            population = population.stream().map(p -> p.withPlayerId(arenaIds.get(p.username()))).toList();
        }
        List<PlayerState> players = population;
        // Cheaters whose cheat has started: the dashboard's "Cheaters active" panel.
        Gauge.builder("telemetry.sim.players.cheating",
                () -> players.stream().filter(p -> p.isCheatingAt(clock.millis())).count()).register(registry);

        List<CompletableFuture<?>> setup = new ArrayList<>(population.size() * 2);
        for (PlayerState player : population) {
            setup.add(publisher.publishProfile(PopulationFactory.profileOf(player, runStartedAt), runStartedAt));
            setup.add(publisher.publishGroundTruth(PopulationFactory.truthOf(runId, player), runStartedAt));
        }
        CompletableFuture.allOf(setup.toArray(CompletableFuture[]::new)).join();
        log.info("Published {} profiles and {} ground-truth records", population.size(), population.size());

        playMatches(runId, population);
        publisher.flush();

        long endedAt = clock.millis();
        List<ManifestEntry> entries = manifest.entries(runId, population);
        RunInfo info = new RunInfo(runId, runStartedAt, endedAt, population.size(), manifest.totalAcked(),
                manifest.totalFailed(), publisher.malformedSent());
        List<CompletableFuture<?>> finish = new ArrayList<>(entries.size() + 1);
        for (ManifestEntry entry : entries) {
            finish.add(publisher.publishManifestEntry(entry, endedAt));
        }
        finish.add(publisher.publishRunInfo(info, endedAt));
        CompletableFuture.allOf(finish.toArray(CompletableFuture[]::new)).join();

        log.info("SIMULATION COMPLETE runId={} acked={} failed={} malformed={}", runId, info.totalAcked(),
                info.totalFailed(), info.malformedSent());
        return info;
    }

    private void playMatches(String runId, List<PlayerState> population) throws InterruptedException {
        AtomicInteger matchCounter = new AtomicInteger();
        List<List<PlayerState>> shards = ShardWorker.partition(population, props);
        List<ScheduledExecutorService> schedulers = new ArrayList<>(shards.size());
        long startedAt = clock.millis();
        for (int k = 0; k < shards.size(); k++) {
            ShardWorker worker = new ShardWorker(k, shards.get(k), props, runId, clock, matchCounter, publisher);
            int shard = k;
            // One platform thread per shard. A shared pool could move a shard between threads between ticks.
            ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(
                    r -> new Thread(r, "shard-" + shard));
            synchronized (executors) {
                executors.add(scheduler);
            }
            schedulers.add(scheduler);
            scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(worker, 0, props.tickMs(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        }

        long deadline = startedAt + props.duration().toMillis();
        long lastLogAt = startedAt;
        long lastAcked = 0;
        long remaining;
        while ((remaining = deadline - clock.millis()) > 0) {
            Thread.sleep(Math.min(remaining, RATE_LOG_INTERVAL_MS));
            long now = clock.millis();
            long acked = manifest.totalAcked();
            long elapsedMs = Math.max(1, now - lastLogAt);
            log.info("Sending {} events/s (acked so far {})", (acked - lastAcked) * 1000 / elapsedMs, acked);
            lastLogAt = now;
            lastAcked = acked;
        }

        for (ScheduledExecutorService scheduler : schedulers) {
            scheduler.shutdown();
        }
        for (ScheduledExecutorService scheduler : schedulers) {
            if (!scheduler.awaitTermination(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                scheduler.shutdownNow();
            }
        }
    }

    private void shutdownExecutors() {
        synchronized (executors) {
            executors.forEach(ExecutorService::shutdownNow);
        }
    }

    private static String randomHex() {
        byte[] bytes = new byte[RUN_ID_BYTES];
        new SecureRandom().nextBytes(bytes);
        return HexFormat.of().formatHex(bytes);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/match/MatchRoom.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.match;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.random.RandomGenerator;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;

/**
 * One match of {@code match-size} players. Only the shard thread that owns the room touches it, so nothing here is
 * synchronized. Keeps the per-player totals the match summary is built from.
 */
public final class MatchRoom {

    /** The six maps rotate by match number. */
    static final List<String> MAPS = List.of("Rust Yard", "Dockside", "Old Town", "Skyline", "Quarry", "Harbor");

    /**
     * Running totals of one player in one match.
     * A small mutable class, not a record. It is a thread-confined accumulator updated thousands of
     * times per second, not an event, DTO or stored state value.
     */
    public static final class PlayerTotals {
        int kills;
        int deaths;
        int headshotKills;
        int shotsFired;
        int shotsHit;
        long sumReactionMs;

        public int kills() {
            return kills;
        }

        public int deaths() {
            return deaths;
        }

        public int headshotKills() {
            return headshotKills;
        }

        public int shotsFired() {
            return shotsFired;
        }

        public int shotsHit() {
            return shotsHit;
        }

        public long sumReactionMs() {
            return sumReactionMs;
        }
    }

    private final int matchNumber;
    private final String externalId;
    private final long startedAtMs;
    private final List<PlayerState> members;
    private final Map<String, PlayerTotals> totals = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    public MatchRoom(int matchNumber, String runId, long startedAtMs, List<PlayerState> members) {
        if (members.size() < 2) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("a match needs at least two players");
        }
        this.matchNumber = matchNumber;
        this.externalId = "sim-%s-%06d".formatted(runId, matchNumber);
        this.startedAtMs = startedAtMs;
        this.members = List.copyOf(members);
        for (PlayerState member : this.members) {
            totals.put(member.playerId(), new PlayerTotals());
        }
    }

    public String externalId() {
        return externalId;
    }

    public String mapName() {
        return MAPS.get((matchNumber - 1) % MAPS.size());
    }

    public long startedAtMs() {
        return startedAtMs;
    }

    public List<PlayerState> members() {
        return members;
    }

    public PlayerTotals totalsOf(String playerId) {
        return totals.get(playerId);
    }

    public boolean isOver(long nowMs, long matchDurationMs) {
        return nowMs >= startedAtMs + matchDurationMs;
    }

    public void recordShot(PlayerState shooter, boolean hit, int reactionMs) {
        PlayerTotals t = totals.get(shooter.playerId());
        t.shotsFired++;
        t.sumReactionMs += reactionMs;
        if (hit) {
            t.shotsHit++;
        }
    }

    public void recordKill(PlayerState killer, PlayerState victim, boolean headshot) {
        PlayerTotals k = totals.get(killer.playerId());
        k.kills++;
        if (headshot) {
            k.headshotKills++;
        }
        totals.get(victim.playerId()).deaths++;
    }

    /** A uniformly random room member other than {@code player}. */
    public PlayerState randomOpponent(PlayerState player, RandomGenerator rng) {
        int own = members.indexOf(player);
        int pick = rng.nextInt(members.size() - 1);
        return members.get(pick >= own ? pick + 1 : pick);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/match/MatchSummaryBuilder.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.match;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ParticipantSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;

/** Turns a finished {@link MatchRoom} into a body Arena's {@code POST /api/v1/matches} accepts. */
public final class MatchSummaryBuilder {

    private static final String GAME_MODE = "FFA";

    private MatchSummaryBuilder() {
    }

    public static MatchSummary build(MatchRoom room, long endedAtMs) {
        List<PlayerState> ranked = new ArrayList<>(room.members());
        // Placement: kills descending, then deaths ascending, then player id ascending.
        ranked.sort(Comparator
                .comparingInt((PlayerState p) -> room.totalsOf(p.playerId()).kills()).reversed()
                .thenComparingInt(p -> room.totalsOf(p.playerId()).deaths())
                .thenComparing(PlayerState::playerId));

        List<ParticipantSummary> participants = new ArrayList<>(ranked.size());
        for (int i = 0; i < ranked.size(); i++) {
            PlayerState player = ranked.get(i);
            MatchRoom.PlayerTotals t = room.totalsOf(player.playerId());
            int avgReaction = t.shotsFired() == 0
                    ? 1
                    : (int) Math.max(1, Math.round((double) t.sumReactionMs() / t.shotsFired()));
            participants.add(new ParticipantSummary(player.playerId(), i + 1, t.kills(), t.deaths(),
                    t.headshotKills(), t.shotsFired(), t.shotsHit(), avgReaction));
        }
        return new MatchSummary(room.externalId(), GAME_MODE, room.mapName(),
                Instant.ofEpochMilli(room.startedAtMs()).toString(),
                Instant.ofEpochMilli(endedAtMs).toString(),
                participants);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/output/EventPublisher.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.output;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.EventType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchEvent;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.MatchSummary;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.Counter;
import io.micrometer.core.instrument.MeterRegistry;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.kafka.core.KafkaTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * The only class that sends to Kafka. Events are serialized to {@code byte[]} here, so {@link #publishMalformed}
 * can send raw garbage through the same producer. Sends are never retried by hand: the idempotent producer
 * already retries safely, and a send that still fails is counted in the manifest.
 */
@Component
public class EventPublisher {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(EventPublisher.class);

    private final KafkaTemplate<String, byte[]> template;
    private final ManifestTracker manifest;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final Map<EventType, Counter> sentByType = new EnumMap<>(EventType.class);
    private final Counter sendFailures;
    private final Counter malformedCounter;
    private final Counter matchesCompleted;
    private final LongAdder malformedSent = new LongAdder();

    public EventPublisher(KafkaTemplate<String, byte[]> template, ManifestTracker manifest, MeterRegistry registry,
                          Clock clock) {
        this.template = template;
        this.manifest = manifest;
        this.clock = clock;
        // Counters are looked up once here: registering one per event would cost real time at 25,000 events/s.
        for (EventType type : EventType.values()) {
            sentByType.put(type, Counter.builder("telemetry.sim.events.sent").tag("type", type.name()).register(registry));
        }
        this.sendFailures = Counter.builder("telemetry.sim.send.failures").register(registry);
        this.malformedCounter = Counter.builder("telemetry.sim.malformed.sent").register(registry);
        this.matchesCompleted = Counter.builder("telemetry.sim.matches.completed").register(registry);
    }

    /** Sends one event keyed by its player id, with the event time as the Kafka record timestamp. */
    public void publish(MatchEvent event) {
        byte[] bytes = JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(event);
        ProducerRecord<String, byte[]> record =
                new ProducerRecord<>(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, null, event.ts(), event.playerId(), bytes);
        CompletableFuture<?> future;
        try {
            future = template.send(record);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            onFailure(event, e);
            return;
        }
        future.whenComplete((result, error) -> {
            if (error == null) {
                manifest.acked(event.playerId(), event.seq());
                sentByType.get(event.type()).increment();
            } else {
                onFailure(event, error);
            }
        });
    }

    /** Sends a deliberately bad record keyed like a real one. It has no seq and is not in the manifest. */
    public void publishMalformed(String playerId, byte[] bytes) {
        template.send(new ProducerRecord<>(Topics.MATCH_EVENTS, null, clock.millis(), playerId, bytes));
        malformedSent.increment();
        malformedCounter.increment();
    }

    public long malformedSent() {
        return malformedSent.sum();
    }

    public CompletableFuture<?> publishProfile(PlayerProfile profile, long ts) {
        return sendJson(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, profile.playerId(), profile, ts);
    }

    public CompletableFuture<?> publishGroundTruth(GroundTruth truth, long ts) {
        return sendJson(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH, truth.playerId(), truth, ts);
    }

    /** Keyed by the match's external id, as the Arena bridge expects. */
    public void publishSummary(MatchSummary summary) {
        sendJson(Topics.MATCH_SUMMARIES, summary.externalId(), summary, clock.millis());
        matchesCompleted.increment();
    }

    public CompletableFuture<?> publishManifestEntry(ManifestEntry entry, long ts) {
        return sendJson(Topics.SIMULATION_MANIFEST, entry.playerId(), entry, ts);
    }

    public CompletableFuture<?> publishRunInfo(RunInfo info, long ts) {
        return sendJson(Topics.SIMULATION_MANIFEST, Topics.RUN_KEY, info, ts);
    }

    /** Blocks until every earlier send has completed and its callback has run. */
    public void flush() {
        template.flush();
    }

    private CompletableFuture<?> sendJson(String topic, String key, Object value, long ts) {
        byte[] bytes = JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(value);
        return template.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, null, ts, key, bytes));
    }

    private void onFailure(MatchEvent event, Throwable error) {
        manifest.failed(event.playerId());
        sendFailures.increment();
        log.warn("Send failed for player {} seq {}: {}", event.playerId(), event.seq(), error.toString());
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/output/ManifestTracker.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.output;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.LongAdder;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population.PlayerState;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Counts, per player, how many events the broker acknowledged and how many sends failed. At the end of a run
 * this becomes one {@link ManifestEntry} per player, which the verifier compares with the detector's
 * {@code integrity-stats}: that comparison is the exactly-once proof.
 *
 * <p>Producer callbacks run on the Kafka I/O thread while shard threads keep sending, so every counter is a
 * {@link LongAdder} or an {@link AtomicLong}.
 */
@Component
public class ManifestTracker {

    private static final class PlayerCounters {
        private final LongAdder acked = new LongAdder();
        private final LongAdder failed = new LongAdder();
        private final AtomicLong highestAckedSeq = new AtomicLong();
    }

    private final Map<String, PlayerCounters> counters = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public void acked(String playerId, long seq) {
        PlayerCounters c = counters.computeIfAbsent(playerId, id -> new PlayerCounters());
        c.acked.increment();
        c.highestAckedSeq.accumulateAndGet(seq, Math::max);
    }

    public void failed(String playerId) {
        counters.computeIfAbsent(playerId, id -> new PlayerCounters()).failed.increment();
    }

    public long ackedOf(String playerId) {
        PlayerCounters c = counters.get(playerId);
        return c == null ? 0 : c.acked.sum();
    }

    public long failedOf(String playerId) {
        PlayerCounters c = counters.get(playerId);
        return c == null ? 0 : c.failed.sum();
    }

    /** The highest sequence number the broker acknowledged for the player, 0 when none. */
    public long highestAckedSeq(String playerId) {
        PlayerCounters c = counters.get(playerId);
        return c == null ? 0 : c.highestAckedSeq.get();
    }

    public long totalAcked() {
        return counters.values().stream().mapToLong(c -> c.acked.sum()).sum();
    }

    public long totalFailed() {
        return counters.values().stream().mapToLong(c -> c.failed.sum()).sum();
    }

    /**
     * One entry per player. DECISION: {@code finalSeq} is the last sequence number the simulator generated
     * ({@link PlayerState#lastSeq()}), not the highest acknowledged one, so a failed send shows up as
     * {@code finalSeq > acked} instead of hiding itself.
     */
    public List<ManifestEntry> entries(String runId, List<PlayerState> players) {
        List<ManifestEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>(players.size());
        for (PlayerState player : players) {
            entries.add(new ManifestEntry(runId, player.playerId(), player.lastSeq(),
                    ackedOf(player.playerId()), failedOf(player.playerId())));
        }
        return entries;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/BehaviorProfile.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import java.util.random.RandomGenerator;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;

/**
 * How a player aims and moves. Drawn once per player. Cheaters get a second, cheat profile
 * derived from their base profile: aimbots change only the aim values, speedhackers only the movement values.
 */
public record BehaviorProfile(Aim aim, Move move) {

    /** Aiming parameters. {@code headshotChance} applies given a hit. */
    public record Aim(double hitChance, double headshotChance, double reactionMean, double reactionSd,
                      int reactionFloor) {
    }

    /** Movement parameters. {@code speedMin..speedMax} (units/s) is drawn again on every tick. */
    public record Move(double moveChance, double speedMin, double speedMax, double speedMultiplier) {
    }

    private static final double HONEST_MOVE_CHANCE = 0.70;
    private static final double HONEST_SPEED_MIN = 2.0;
    private static final double HONEST_SPEED_MAX = 7.0;
    private static final double HACK_MOVE_CHANCE = 0.85;
    private static final double HACK_SPEED_MIN = 5.0;
    private static final double HACK_SPEED_MAX = 7.0;

    public static BehaviorProfile honest(RandomGenerator rng) {
        double reactionMean = clamp(240 + 30 * rng.nextGaussian(), 170, 400);
        Aim aim = new Aim(uniform(rng, 0.20, 0.45), uniform(rng, 0.25, 0.55), reactionMean, 35, 80);
        return new BehaviorProfile(aim, honestMove());
    }

    public static BehaviorProfile pro(RandomGenerator rng) {
        Aim aim = new Aim(uniform(rng, 0.40, 0.55), uniform(rng, 0.55, 0.68), uniform(rng, 155, 185), 30, 80);
        return new BehaviorProfile(aim, honestMove());
    }

    /** The profile this player switches to when the cheat starts. Only the cheat's own family changes. */
    public BehaviorProfile asCheater(CheatType type, RandomGenerator rng) {
        return switch (type) {
            case AIMBOT -> new BehaviorProfile(
                    new Aim(uniform(rng, 0.60, 0.90), uniform(rng, 0.75, 0.95), uniform(rng, 40, 80), 10, 20), move);
            case AIMBOT_SUBTLE -> new BehaviorProfile(
                    new Aim(uniform(rng, 0.45, 0.60), uniform(rng, 0.60, 0.70), uniform(rng, 110, 135), 20, 50), move);
            case SPEEDHACK -> new BehaviorProfile(aim, hackMove(uniform(rng, 1.5, 2.0)));
            case SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE -> new BehaviorProfile(aim, hackMove(uniform(rng, 1.12, 1.20)));
            case NONE -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("NONE is not a cheat");
        };
    }

    public SkillTier skillTier() {
        return tierOf(aim.headshotChance());
    }

    /** Below 0.33 BRONZE, below 0.41 SILVER, below 0.48 GOLD, below 0.55 PLATINUM, otherwise PRO. */
    public static SkillTier tierOf(double headshotChance) {
        if (headshotChance < 0.33) {
            return SkillTier.BRONZE;
        }
        if (headshotChance < 0.41) {
            return SkillTier.SILVER;
        }
        if (headshotChance < 0.48) {
            return SkillTier.GOLD;
        }
        if (headshotChance < 0.55) {
            return SkillTier.PLATINUM;
        }
        return SkillTier.PRO;
    }

    private static Move honestMove() {
        return new Move(HONEST_MOVE_CHANCE, HONEST_SPEED_MIN, HONEST_SPEED_MAX, 1.0);
    }

    private static Move hackMove(double multiplier) {
        return new Move(HACK_MOVE_CHANCE, HACK_SPEED_MIN, HACK_SPEED_MAX, multiplier);
    }

    private static double uniform(RandomGenerator rng, double low, double high) {
        return low + rng.nextDouble() * (high - low);
    }

    private static double clamp(double value, double low, double high) {
        return Math.max(low, Math.min(high, value));
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/CheaterAssignment.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;

/** Decides, per player index, who is a cheater, a pro or honest. Same seed, same answer. */
public final class CheaterAssignment {

    /** The role of one player. {@code cheatType} is {@link CheatType#NONE} for honest players and pros. */
    public record Role(Segment segment, CheatType cheatType) {
    }

    private static final Role HONEST = new Role(Segment.HONEST, CheatType.NONE);
    private static final Role PRO = new Role(Segment.PRO, CheatType.NONE);

    private CheaterAssignment() {
    }

    /**
     * Shuffles the indices with {@code new Random(seed)}. The first {@code round(players x cheaterRatio)} become
     * cheaters (40% AIMBOT, 20% AIMBOT_SUBTLE, 24% SPEEDHACK, 16% SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE, rounded down, any remainder to
     * AIMBOT). The next {@code round(players x proRatio)} become PRO, and everyone else is HONEST.
     *
     * @return the role of player {@code i} at index {@code i}
     */
    public static List<Role> assign(int players, double cheaterRatio, double proRatio, long seed) {
        List<Integer> order = new ArrayList<>(players);
        for (int i = 0; i < players; i++) {
            order.add(i);
        }
        Collections.shuffle(order, new Random(seed));

        int cheaters = (int) Math.round(players * cheaterRatio);
        int pros = (int) Math.round(players * proRatio);
        int subtleAim = cheaters * 20 / 100;
        int speed = cheaters * 24 / 100;
        int subtleSpeed = cheaters * 16 / 100;
        int aim = cheaters - subtleAim - speed - subtleSpeed; // 40%, plus the rounding remainder

        Role[] roles = new Role[players];
        int next = 0;
        next = fill(roles, order, next, aim, new Role(Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.AIMBOT));
        next = fill(roles, order, next, subtleAim, new Role(Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE));
        next = fill(roles, order, next, speed, new Role(Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.SPEEDHACK));
        next = fill(roles, order, next, subtleSpeed, new Role(Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE));
        next = fill(roles, order, next, pros, PRO);
        for (int i = 0; i < players; i++) {
            if (roles[i] == null) {
                roles[i] = HONEST;
            }
        }
        return List.of(roles);
    }

    private static int fill(Role[] roles, List<Integer> order, int from, int count, Role role) {
        for (int i = from; i < from + count; i++) {
            roles[order.get(i)] = role;
        }
        return from + count;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/PlayerState.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;

/**
 * One simulated player. Everything is fixed at creation except the per-player sequence counter, which is only
 * advanced by the shard thread that owns the player. {@code cheat} and {@code cheatStartsAt} are null for
 * non-cheaters.
 */
public record PlayerState(
        int index,
        String playerId,
        String username,
        String country,
        Segment segment,
        CheatType cheatType,
        SkillTier skillTier,
        BehaviorProfile base,
        BehaviorProfile cheat,
        Long cheatStartsAt,
        AtomicLong seq) {

    /**
     * The same player under another id, with a fresh sequence counter. Used when Arena assigns the real ids: the
     * simulator makes up ids first (so usernames and behavior stay seeded) and swaps them before anything is sent.
     */
    public PlayerState withPlayerId(String newPlayerId) {
        return new PlayerState(index, newPlayerId, username, country, segment, cheatType, skillTier, base, cheat,
                cheatStartsAt, new AtomicLong());
    }

    /** The profile in force at {@code nowMs}: the cheat profile from {@code cheatStartsAt} on, else the base one. */
    public BehaviorProfile profileAt(long nowMs) {
        return isCheatingAt(nowMs) ? cheat : base;
    }

    public boolean isCheatingAt(long nowMs) {
        return cheatStartsAt != null && nowMs >= cheatStartsAt;
    }

    /** Next per-player sequence number: 1, 2, 3 and so on, with no gaps. */
    public long nextSeq() {
        return seq.incrementAndGet();
    }

    /** The highest sequence number handed out so far, 0 when none. */
    public long lastSeq() {
        return seq.get();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-simulator/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/PopulationFactory.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import static java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;
import java.util.SplittableRandom;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.SimulatorProperties;
import net.datafaker.Faker;

/** Builds the seeded population: ids, usernames, countries, roles, behavior profiles and cheat start times. */
public final class PopulationFactory {

    /**
     * XORed into the seed so the population draws never replay a shard's stream: shard k uses
     * {@code SplittableRandom(seed + k)}, and shard 0 would otherwise share this one.
     */
    private static final long POPULATION_STREAM = 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15L;

    private final SimulatorProperties props;

    public PopulationFactory(SimulatorProperties props) {
        this.props = props;
    }

    /**
     * Player {@code i} gets id {@code nameUUIDFromBytes(seed + ":" + i)} and username {@code sim_0001} and up.
     * Behavior profiles, cheat profiles and cheat start offsets are drawn in player-index order from one
     * {@code SplittableRandom(seed ^ POPULATION_STREAM)}, separate from the shuffle in {@link CheaterAssignment}, so the whole population
     * depends only on the seed. Countries come from a Datafaker seeded the same way.
     */
    public List<PlayerState> create(long runStartedAtMs) {
        List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles = CheaterAssignment.assign(
                props.players(), props.cheaterRatio(), props.proRatio(), props.seed());
        SplittableRandom rng = new SplittableRandom(props.seed() ^ POPULATION_STREAM);
        Faker faker = new Faker(new Random(props.seed()));
        long minMs = props.cheatStartMin().toMillis();
        long maxMs = props.cheatStartMax().toMillis();

        List<PlayerState> players = new ArrayList<>(props.players());
        for (int i = 0; i < props.players(); i++) {
            CheaterAssignment.Role role = roles.get(i);
            BehaviorProfile base = role.segment() == Segment.PRO ? BehaviorProfile.pro(rng) : BehaviorProfile.honest(rng);
            BehaviorProfile cheat = null;
            Long cheatStartsAt = null;
            if (role.segment() == Segment.CHEATER) {
                cheat = base.asCheater(role.cheatType(), rng);
                cheatStartsAt = runStartedAtMs + minMs + Math.round(rng.nextDouble() * (maxMs - minMs));
            }
            // Cheaters keep their base profile's tier, so the tier does not give them away.
            SkillTier tier = role.segment() == Segment.PRO ? SkillTier.PRO : base.skillTier();
            players.add(new PlayerState(
                    i,
                    UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes((props.seed() + ":" + i).getBytes(UTF_8)).toString(),
                    "sim_%04d".formatted(i + 1),
                    faker.country().countryCode2().toUpperCase(),
                    role.segment(),
                    role.cheatType(),
                    tier,
                    base,
                    cheat,
                    cheatStartsAt,
                    new AtomicLong()));
        }
        return players;
    }

    public static PlayerProfile profileOf(PlayerState player, long registeredAtMs) {
        return new PlayerProfile(player.playerId(), player.username(), player.country(), player.skillTier(),
                registeredAtMs);
    }

    public static GroundTruth truthOf(String runId, PlayerState player) {
        CheatType type = player.cheatType();
        return new GroundTruth(runId, player.playerId(), player.segment(), type, player.cheatStartsAt());
    }
}
```
