`telemetry-simulator/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/CheaterAssignmentTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CheaterAssignmentTest {

    private static Map<CheatType, Long> cheatCounts(List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles) {
        return roles.stream().collect(Collectors.groupingBy(CheaterAssignment.Role::cheatType, Collectors.counting()));
    }

    private static long count(List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles, Segment segment) {
        return roles.stream().filter(r -> r.segment() == segment).count();
    }

    @Test
    void thousandPlayersGiveTheExpectedCounts() {
        List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles = CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 42);

        assertThat(roles).hasSize(1000);
        assertThat(cheatCounts(roles)).containsEntry(CheatType.AIMBOT, 20L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, 10L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.SPEEDHACK, 12L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE, 8L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.NONE, 950L);
        assertThat(count(roles, Segment.CHEATER)).isEqualTo(50);
        assertThat(count(roles, Segment.PRO)).isEqualTo(20);
        assertThat(count(roles, Segment.HONEST)).isEqualTo(930);
    }

    @Test
    void sameSeedGivesTheSameAssignment() {
        assertThat(CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 42))
                .isEqualTo(CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 42));
    }

    @Test
    void differentSeedGivesADifferentAssignment() {
        assertThat(CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 42))
                .isNotEqualTo(CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 43));
    }

    @Test
    void roundingRemainderGoesToAimbot() {
        // round(130 x 0.05) = 7 cheaters: 1 subtle aimbot, 1 speedhack, 1 subtle speedhack, and 4 aimbots.
        List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles = CheaterAssignment.assign(130, 0.05, 0.0, 1);

        assertThat(cheatCounts(roles)).containsEntry(CheatType.AIMBOT, 4L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, 1L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.SPEEDHACK, 1L)
                .containsEntry(CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE, 1L);
        assertThat(count(roles, Segment.CHEATER)).isEqualTo(7);
    }

    @Test
    void zeroRatiosGiveOnlyHonestPlayers() {
        List<CheaterAssignment.Role> roles = CheaterAssignment.assign(40, 0.0, 0.0, 5);

        assertThat(count(roles, Segment.HONEST)).isEqualTo(40);
    }

    @Test
    void honestPlayersAndProsHaveNoCheatType() {
        assertThat(CheaterAssignment.assign(1000, 0.05, 0.02, 42))
                .filteredOn(r -> r.segment() != Segment.CHEATER)
                .allMatch(r -> r.cheatType() == CheatType.NONE);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-simulator/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/PlayerStateTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.TestSimProps;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class PlayerStateTest {

    @Test
    void withPlayerIdKeepsEverythingButTheIdAndStartsANewSequence() {
        List<PlayerState> population = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.of(42, 40)).create(1000);
        PlayerState original = population.stream().filter(p -> p.cheat() != null).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        original.nextSeq();
        original.nextSeq();

        PlayerState moved = original.withPlayerId("arena-id");

        assertThat(moved.playerId()).isEqualTo("arena-id");
        assertThat(moved.username()).isEqualTo(original.username());
        assertThat(moved.country()).isEqualTo(original.country());
        assertThat(moved.segment()).isEqualTo(original.segment());
        assertThat(moved.cheatType()).isEqualTo(original.cheatType());
        assertThat(moved.cheatStartsAt()).isEqualTo(original.cheatStartsAt());
        assertThat(moved.base()).isSameAs(original.base());
        assertThat(moved.cheat()).isSameAs(original.cheat());
        assertThat(moved.lastSeq()).isZero();
        assertThat(original.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(2);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-simulator/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/simulator/population/PopulationFactoryTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.population;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SkillTier;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.simulator.TestSimProps;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class PopulationFactoryTest {

    private static final long RUN_START = 1_763_372_100_000L;

    /** PlayerState holds an AtomicLong, so compare everything else through this projection. */
    private record Fingerprint(String id, String username, String country, Segment segment, CheatType cheat,
                               SkillTier tier, BehaviorProfile base, BehaviorProfile cheatProfile, Long start) {
        static Fingerprint of(PlayerState p) {
            return new Fingerprint(p.playerId(), p.username(), p.country(), p.segment(), p.cheatType(),
                    p.skillTier(), p.base(), p.cheat(), p.cheatStartsAt());
        }
    }

    private static List<Fingerprint> population(long seed) {
        return new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.of(seed, 1000)).create(RUN_START).stream()
                .map(Fingerprint::of).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Test
    void idsAndUsernamesFollowTheNamingFormulas() {
        List<PlayerState> players = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START);

        assertThat(players).hasSize(1000);
        assertThat(players.get(0).username()).isEqualTo("sim_0001");
        assertThat(players.get(999).username()).isEqualTo("sim_1000");
        assertThat(players.get(7).playerId())
                .isEqualTo(UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("42:7".getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).toString());
        assertThat(players.stream().map(PlayerState::playerId).distinct()).hasSize(1000);
        assertThat(players).allSatisfy(p -> assertThat(p.country()).matches("[A-Z]{2}"));
    }

    @Test
    void sameSeedGivesTheSamePopulationIncludingProfilesAndStartTimes() {
        assertThat(population(42)).isEqualTo(population(42));
    }

    @Test
    void differentSeedGivesADifferentPopulation() {
        assertThat(population(42)).isNotEqualTo(population(43));
    }

    @Test
    void onlyCheatersHaveACheatProfileAndAStartInsideTheWindow() {
        List<PlayerState> players = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START);

        for (PlayerState p : players) {
            if (p.segment() == Segment.CHEATER) {
                assertThat(p.cheat()).isNotNull();
                assertThat(p.cheatStartsAt()).isBetween(RUN_START + 10_000, RUN_START + 60_000);
            } else {
                assertThat(p.cheat()).isNull();
                assertThat(p.cheatStartsAt()).isNull();
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void prosAreTierProAndCheatersKeepTheirBaseTier() {
        List<PlayerState> players = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START);

        assertThat(players).filteredOn(p -> p.segment() == Segment.PRO)
                .isNotEmpty().allMatch(p -> p.skillTier() == SkillTier.PRO);
        assertThat(players).filteredOn(p -> p.segment() != Segment.PRO)
                .allMatch(p -> p.skillTier() == p.base().skillTier());
    }

    @Test
    void profileSwitchesExactlyAtCheatStart() {
        PlayerState cheater = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START).stream()
                .filter(p -> p.cheatType() == CheatType.AIMBOT).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        long start = cheater.cheatStartsAt();

        assertThat(cheater.profileAt(start - 1)).isSameAs(cheater.base());
        assertThat(cheater.profileAt(start)).isSameAs(cheater.cheat());
        assertThat(cheater.isCheatingAt(start - 1)).isFalse();
        assertThat(cheater.isCheatingAt(start)).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    void honestPlayersNeverSwitch() {
        PlayerState honest = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START).stream()
                .filter(p -> p.segment() == Segment.HONEST).findFirst().orElseThrow();

        assertThat(honest.profileAt(Long.MAX_VALUE)).isSameAs(honest.base());
        assertThat(honest.isCheatingAt(Long.MAX_VALUE)).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    void sequenceNumbersCountUpFromOneWithoutGaps() {
        PlayerState p = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START).get(0);

        assertThat(p.lastSeq()).isZero();
        assertThat(p.nextSeq()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(p.nextSeq()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(p.lastSeq()).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void profileAndGroundTruthRecordsMirrorThePlayer() {
        List<PlayerState> players = new PopulationFactory(TestSimProps.defaults()).create(RUN_START);
        PlayerState cheater = players.stream().filter(p -> p.segment() == Segment.CHEATER).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        PlayerState honest = players.stream().filter(p -> p.segment() == Segment.HONEST).findFirst().orElseThrow();

        PlayerProfile profile = PopulationFactory.profileOf(cheater, RUN_START);
        GroundTruth truth = PopulationFactory.truthOf("r-abc123", cheater);
        GroundTruth honestTruth = PopulationFactory.truthOf("r-abc123", honest);

        assertThat(profile.playerId()).isEqualTo(cheater.playerId());
        assertThat(profile.username()).isEqualTo(cheater.username());
        assertThat(profile.skillTier()).isEqualTo(cheater.skillTier());
        assertThat(profile.registeredAt()).isEqualTo(RUN_START);
        assertThat(truth).isEqualTo(new GroundTruth("r-abc123", cheater.playerId(), Segment.CHEATER,
                cheater.cheatType(), cheater.cheatStartsAt()));
        assertThat(honestTruth.cheatType()).isEqualTo(CheatType.NONE);
        assertThat(honestTruth.cheatStartsAt()).isNull();
        assertThat(Objects.requireNonNull(truth.cheatStartsAt())).isGreaterThan(RUN_START);
    }
}
```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/DetectionEvaluator.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;

/**
 * Scores detection against ground truth. Pure math: no Kafka, no clock.
 *
 * <p>Flags before the run's {@code startedAt}, and flags for players that are not in this run's ground truth, are
 * ignored. A cheater is detected when at least one flag has {@code detectedAt >= cheatStartsAt}; the latency is the
 * earliest such flag minus {@code cheatStartsAt}.
 */
public final class DetectionEvaluator {

    private static final List<CheatType> CHEAT_TYPES = List.of(
            CheatType.AIMBOT, CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, CheatType.SPEEDHACK, CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE);

    private DetectionEvaluator() {
    }

    public static DetectionReport evaluate(RunInfo run, Collection<GroundTruth> truth,
                                           Collection<SuspiciousPlayer> flags, Map<String, String> usernames) {
        Map<String, GroundTruth> byPlayer = new HashMap<>();
        for (GroundTruth t : truth) {
            if (run.runId().equals(t.runId())) {
                byPlayer.put(t.playerId(), t);
            }
        }

        Map<String, List<SuspiciousPlayer>> flagsByPlayer = new HashMap<>();
        Map<Rule, Long> flagsByRule = new EnumMap<>(Rule.class);
        for (SuspiciousPlayer flag : flags) {
            if (flag.detectedAt() < run.startedAt() || !byPlayer.containsKey(flag.playerId())) {
                continue;
            }
            flagsByPlayer.computeIfAbsent(flag.playerId(), k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(flag);
            flagsByRule.merge(flag.rule(), 1L, Long::sum);
        }
        flagsByPlayer.values().forEach(list -> list.sort(Comparator.comparingLong(SuspiciousPlayer::detectedAt)));

        int cheaters = 0;
        int honest = 0;
        int pro = 0;
        int detected = 0;
        int correctRule = 0;
        int early = 0;
        int fpHonest = 0;
        int fpPro = 0;
        List<DetectionReport.Missed> missed = new ArrayList<>();
        List<DetectionReport.FalsePositive> falsePositives = new ArrayList<>();
        Map<CheatType, List<Double>> latencies = new EnumMap<>(CheatType.class);
        Map<CheatType, Integer> counts = new EnumMap<>(CheatType.class);

        List<GroundTruth> ordered = new ArrayList<>(byPlayer.values());
        ordered.sort(Comparator.comparing(GroundTruth::playerId));
        for (GroundTruth t : ordered) {
            List<SuspiciousPlayer> own = flagsByPlayer.getOrDefault(t.playerId(), List.of());
            String name = usernames.getOrDefault(t.playerId(), t.playerId());
            if (t.segment() == Segment.CHEATER) {
                cheaters++;
                counts.merge(t.cheatType(), 1, Integer::sum);
                long start = t.cheatStartsAt();
                if (own.stream().anyMatch(f -> f.detectedAt() < start)) {
                    early++;
                }
                SuspiciousPlayer first = own.stream().filter(f -> f.detectedAt() >= start).findFirst().orElse(null);
                if (first == null) {
                    missed.add(new DetectionReport.Missed(name, t.cheatType(), start));
                    continue;
                }
                detected++;
                if (first.rule() == familyOf(t.cheatType())) {
                    correctRule++;
                }
                latencies.computeIfAbsent(t.cheatType(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
                        .add((first.detectedAt() - start) / 1000.0);
            } else {
                if (t.segment() == Segment.PRO) {
                    pro++;
                } else {
                    honest++;
                }
                if (!own.isEmpty()) {
                    if (t.segment() == Segment.PRO) {
                        fpPro++;
                    } else {
                        fpHonest++;
                    }
                    SuspiciousPlayer first = own.get(0);
                    falsePositives.add(new DetectionReport.FalsePositive(name, t.segment(), first.rule(),
                            first.score(), first.evidence()));
                }
            }
        }

        List<DetectionReport.LatencyRow> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        List<Double> all = new ArrayList<>();
        for (CheatType type : CHEAT_TYPES) {
            List<Double> own = latencies.getOrDefault(type, List.of());
            all.addAll(own);
            rows.add(row(type, counts.getOrDefault(type, 0), own));
        }
        rows.add(row(null, cheaters, all));

        int fp = fpHonest + fpPro;
        return new DetectionReport(run.runId(), run.startedAt(), run.endedAt(), cheaters, honest, pro, detected,
                correctRule, early, fpHonest, fpPro,
                ratio(detected, cheaters), ratio(fp, honest + pro), ratio(fpHonest, honest), ratio(fpPro, pro),
                ratio(detected, detected + fp), flagsByRule, rows, missed, falsePositives);
    }

    /** The rule that counts as correct for a cheat type: {@code AIMBOT*} needs AIMBOT, {@code SPEEDHACK*} needs SPEEDHACK. */
    static Rule familyOf(CheatType type) {
        return switch (type) {
            case AIMBOT, AIMBOT_SUBTLE -> Rule.AIMBOT;
            case SPEEDHACK, SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE -> Rule.SPEEDHACK;
            case NONE -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("NONE has no cheat family");
        };
    }

    /** Nearest rank: sort ascending, take index {@code ceil(p / 100 * n) - 1}. Null for an empty list. */
    static Double percentile(List<Double> values, double p) {
        if (values.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        List<Double> sorted = values.stream().sorted().toList();
        int index = (int) Math.ceil(p * sorted.size() / 100.0) - 1;
        return sorted.get(Math.max(0, index));
    }

    private static DetectionReport.LatencyRow row(CheatType type, int count, List<Double> latencies) {
        return new DetectionReport.LatencyRow(type, count, latencies.size(), percentile(latencies, 50),
                percentile(latencies, 95), latencies.isEmpty() ? null : percentile(latencies, 100));
    }

    private static double ratio(int numerator, int denominator) {
        return denominator == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) numerator / denominator;
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/DetectionReport.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;

/**
 * The result of scoring the flags against ground truth. All ratios are 0.0 when their denominator is 0
 * (DECISION: one rule for every undefined ratio, so a report never contains NaN).
 */
public record DetectionReport(
        String runId,
        long startedAt,
        long endedAt,
        int cheaters,
        int honest,
        int pro,
        int detected,
        /** Detected cheaters whose earliest qualifying flag has the rule matching their cheat family. */
        int correctRule,
        /** Cheaters flagged before their cheat started. Reported only; it does not change recall. */
        int earlyFlags,
        int falsePositivesHonest,
        int falsePositivesPro,
        double recall,
        double fpr,
        double fprHonest,
        double fprPro,
        double precision,
        Map<Rule, Long> flagsByRule,
        List<LatencyRow> latency,
        List<Missed> missed,
        List<FalsePositive> falsePositives) {

    public int falsePositiveCount() {
        return falsePositivesHonest + falsePositivesPro;
    }

    /** One row of the latency table; {@code cheatType} is null for the overall row. Latencies are in seconds. */
    public record LatencyRow(CheatType cheatType, int count, int detected, Double p50, Double p95, Double max) {
    }

    public record Missed(String username, CheatType cheatType, long cheatStartsAt) {
    }

    /** A honest or pro player with a flag, described by their first flag. */
    public record FalsePositive(String username, Segment segment, Rule rule, double score, Evidence evidence) {
    }
}
```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/IntegrityChecker.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;

/**
 * Proves that every acknowledged event was counted exactly once. Pure comparison: no Kafka, no clock.
 *
 * <p>A player passes when stats exist, the simulator reported no failed sends, the detector saw no duplicates and no
 * gaps, and both {@code lastSeq} and {@code count} equal the manifest's {@code finalSeq}.
 */
public final class IntegrityChecker {

    static final int MAX_LISTED_FAILURES = 20;

    private IntegrityChecker() {
    }

    public static IntegrityReport check(RunInfo run, Collection<ManifestEntry> manifest,
                                        Map<String, IntegrityStats> stats) {
        int passed = 0;
        int withoutStats = 0;
        long expected = 0;
        long counted = 0;
        long duplicates = 0;
        long gaps = 0;
        List<IntegrityReport.Failure> failures = new ArrayList<>();

        List<ManifestEntry> ordered = new ArrayList<>(manifest);
        ordered.sort(Comparator.comparing(ManifestEntry::playerId));
        for (ManifestEntry m : ordered) {
            IntegrityStats s = stats.get(m.playerId());
            expected += m.finalSeq();
            List<String> reasons = new ArrayList<>();
            if (s == null) {
                withoutStats++;
                reasons.add("no integrity stats");
            } else {
                counted += s.count();
                duplicates += s.duplicates();
                gaps += s.gaps();
                if (s.duplicates() != 0) {
                    reasons.add("duplicates=" + s.duplicates());
                }
                if (s.gaps() != 0) {
                    reasons.add("gaps=" + s.gaps());
                }
                if (s.lastSeq() != m.finalSeq()) {
                    reasons.add("lastSeq=" + s.lastSeq() + " expected " + m.finalSeq());
                }
                if (s.count() != m.finalSeq()) {
                    reasons.add("count=" + s.count() + " expected " + m.finalSeq());
                }
            }
            if (m.failed() != 0) {
                reasons.add("simulator reported failed sends=" + m.failed());
            }
            if (reasons.isEmpty()) {
                passed++;
            } else if (failures.size() < MAX_LISTED_FAILURES) {
                failures.add(new IntegrityReport.Failure(m, s, List.copyOf(reasons)));
            }
        }
        return new IntegrityReport(run.runId(), run.startedAt(), run.endedAt(), ordered.size(), passed, expected,
                counted, duplicates, gaps, withoutStats, failures);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/IntegrityReport.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.util.List;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;

/** The outcome of comparing the simulator's manifest with the detector's integrity stats. */
public record IntegrityReport(
        String runId,
        long startedAt,
        long endedAt,
        int playersChecked,
        int playersPassed,
        long eventsExpected,
        long eventsCounted,
        long duplicates,
        long gaps,
        int playersWithoutStats,
        /** At most {@link IntegrityChecker#MAX_LISTED_FAILURES}; {@code playersChecked - playersPassed} in total. */
        List<Failure> failures) {

    public boolean passed() {
        return playersChecked > 0 && playersPassed == playersChecked;
    }

    /** One failing player with both records side by side. {@code stats} is null when none was found. */
    public record Failure(ManifestEntry manifest, IntegrityStats stats, List<String> reasons) {
    }
}
```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/ReportWriter.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Locale;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Renders a report as Markdown and writes it to {@code reports/detection-<runId>.md} or
 * {@code reports/integrity-<runId>.md}. The first lines are the run id, the start and end times (ISO-8601) and the
 * verifier mode.
 */
@Component
public class ReportWriter {

    private final Path reportsDir;

    public ReportWriter(VerifierProperties props) {
        this.reportsDir = Path.of(props.reportsDir());
    }

    public String renderDetection(DetectionReport r) {
        StringBuilder md = header("detection", r.runId(), r.startedAt(), r.endedAt());
        md.append("## Summary\n\n| Measure | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n");
        row(md, "Cheaters", r.cheaters());
        row(md, "Detected cheaters", r.detected());
        row(md, "Detected with the correct rule", r.correctRule());
        row(md, "Recall", percent(r.recall()));
        row(md, "False-positive rate (honest + pro)", percent(r.fpr()));
        row(md, "False-positive rate (honest only)", percent(r.fprHonest()) + " of " + r.honest());
        row(md, "False-positive rate (pro only)", percent(r.fprPro()) + " of " + r.pro());
        row(md, "Precision", percent(r.precision()));
        row(md, "Cheaters flagged before their cheat started", r.earlyFlags());
        for (Rule rule : Rule.values()) {
            row(md, "Flags, rule " + rule, r.flagsByRule().getOrDefault(rule, 0L));
        }

        md.append("\n## Detection latency (seconds after the cheat started)\n\n")
                .append("| Cheat type | Cheaters | Detected | p50 | p95 | max |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n");
        for (DetectionReport.LatencyRow l : r.latency()) {
            md.append("| ").append(l.cheatType() == null ? "**ALL**" : l.cheatType().name())
                    .append(" | ").append(l.count()).append(" | ").append(l.detected())
                    .append(" | ").append(seconds(l.p50())).append(" | ").append(seconds(l.p95()))
                    .append(" | ").append(seconds(l.max())).append(" |\n");
        }

        md.append("\n## Missed cheaters\n\n");
        if (r.missed().isEmpty()) {
            md.append("None.\n");
        } else {
            md.append("| Player | Cheat type | Cheat started |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n");
            for (DetectionReport.Missed m : r.missed()) {
                md.append("| ").append(m.username()).append(" | ").append(m.cheatType()).append(" | ")
                        .append(Instant.ofEpochMilli(m.cheatStartsAt())).append(" |\n");
            }
        }

        md.append("\n## False positives\n\n");
        if (r.falsePositives().isEmpty()) {
            md.append("None.\n");
        } else {
            md.append("| Player | Segment | Rule | Score | Evidence of the first flag |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n");
            for (DetectionReport.FalsePositive f : r.falsePositives()) {
                md.append("| ").append(f.username()).append(" | ").append(f.segment()).append(" | ")
                        .append(f.rule()).append(" | ").append(String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", f.score()))
                        .append(" | ").append(evidence(f.evidence())).append(" |\n");
            }
        }
        return md.toString();
    }

    public String renderIntegrity(IntegrityReport r) {
        StringBuilder md = header("integrity", r.runId(), r.startedAt(), r.endedAt());
        md.append("## Result: ").append(r.passed() ? "PASS" : "FAIL").append("\n\n");
        md.append("| Measure | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n");
        row(md, "Players checked", r.playersChecked());
        row(md, "Players passed", r.playersPassed());
        row(md, "Events expected (sum of finalSeq)", r.eventsExpected());
        row(md, "Events counted (sum of count)", r.eventsCounted());
        row(md, "Duplicates", r.duplicates());
        row(md, "Gaps", r.gaps());
        row(md, "Players with no stats", r.playersWithoutStats());

        md.append("\n## Failing players");
        int failing = r.playersChecked() - r.playersPassed();
        if (r.failures().isEmpty()) {
            md.append("\n\nNone.\n");
        } else {
            md.append(" (").append(r.failures().size()).append(" of ").append(failing).append(" shown)\n\n")
                    .append("| Player | Manifest | Integrity stats | Reasons |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n");
            for (IntegrityReport.Failure f : r.failures()) {
                md.append("| ").append(f.manifest().playerId()).append(" | finalSeq=").append(f.manifest().finalSeq())
                        .append(" acked=").append(f.manifest().acked()).append(" failed=").append(f.manifest().failed())
                        .append(" | ");
                if (f.stats() == null) {
                    md.append("none");
                } else {
                    md.append("count=").append(f.stats().count()).append(" lastSeq=").append(f.stats().lastSeq())
                            .append(" duplicates=").append(f.stats().duplicates())
                            .append(" gaps=").append(f.stats().gaps());
                }
                md.append(" | ").append(String.join("; ", f.reasons())).append(" |\n");
            }
        }
        return md.toString();
    }

    /** Writes {@code content} to {@code <reports-dir>/<mode>-<runId>.md}, creating the directory, and returns the path. */
    public Path write(String mode, String runId, String content) {
        Path file = reportsDir.resolve(mode + "-" + runId + ".md");
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(reportsDir);
            Files.writeString(file, content, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException("Cannot write " + file, e);
        }
        return file;
    }

    private static StringBuilder header(String mode, String runId, long startedAt, long endedAt) {
        return new StringBuilder("# Telemetry verifier: ").append(mode).append("\n\n")
                .append("- Run id: ").append(runId).append('\n')
                .append("- Started: ").append(Instant.ofEpochMilli(startedAt)).append('\n')
                .append("- Ended: ").append(Instant.ofEpochMilli(endedAt)).append('\n')
                .append("- Mode: ").append(mode).append("\n\n");
    }

    private static void row(StringBuilder md, String label, Object value) {
        md.append("| ").append(label).append(" | ").append(value).append(" |\n");
    }

    private static String percent(double ratio) {
        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.2f%%", ratio * 100);
    }

    private static String seconds(Double value) {
        return value == null ? "-" : String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1f", value);
    }

    private static String evidence(Evidence e) {
        if (e == null) {
            return "-";
        }
        if (e.shots() != null) {
            return "shots=" + e.shots() + " hits=" + e.hits() + " headshotHits=" + e.headshotHits()
                    + " headshotRate=" + e.headshotRate() + " avgReactionMs=" + e.avgReactionMs();
        }
        return "movingSamples=" + e.movingSamples() + " overspeedSamples=" + e.overspeedSamples()
                + " overspeedRatio=" + e.overspeedRatio() + " maxSpeed=" + e.maxSpeed();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/TopicSnapshotReader.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.ConsumerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.consumer.KafkaConsumer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.PartitionInfo;
import org.apache.kafka.common.TopicPartition;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.ByteArrayDeserializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringDeserializer;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Reads a whole topic as it stands now. A plain consumer with no group id, {@code read_committed} (so records of an
 * aborted transaction are never seen), no auto-commit, assigned to every partition and sought to the beginning. It
 * records the end offsets once and polls until each partition's position reaches its end, or until
 * {@code read-timeout} passes.
 */
@Component
public class TopicSnapshotReader {

    private static final Duration POLL = Duration.ofMillis(200);
    private static final Duration METADATA_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(10);

    private final String bootstrapServers;
    private final Duration readTimeout;

    public TopicSnapshotReader(@Value("${spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers}") String bootstrapServers,
                               VerifierProperties props) {
        this.bootstrapServers = bootstrapServers;
        this.readTimeout = props.readTimeout();
    }

    /** Every record, partition by partition in offset order. Empty when the topic does not exist. */
    public List<ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]>> readAll(String topic) {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, ByteArrayDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ISOLATION_LEVEL_CONFIG, "read_committed");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.ENABLE_AUTO_COMMIT_CONFIG, false);
        try (KafkaConsumer<String, byte[]> consumer = new KafkaConsumer<>(config)) {
            List<PartitionInfo> infos = consumer.partitionsFor(topic, METADATA_TIMEOUT);
            if (infos == null || infos.isEmpty()) {
                return List.of();
            }
            List<TopicPartition> partitions = infos.stream()
                    .map(info -> new TopicPartition(info.topic(), info.partition())).toList();
            consumer.assign(partitions);
            consumer.seekToBeginning(partitions);
            Map<TopicPartition, Long> ends = consumer.endOffsets(partitions);

            List<ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]>> records = new ArrayList<>();
            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + readTimeout.toNanos();
            while (!reachedEnds(consumer, ends) && System.nanoTime() < deadline) {
                consumer.poll(POLL).forEach(records::add);
            }
            return records;
        }
    }

    /** The latest value per key. A null value (a tombstone) removes the key. */
    public Map<String, byte[]> readLatest(String topic) {
        Map<String, byte[]> latest = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record : readAll(topic)) {
            if (record.value() == null) {
                latest.remove(record.key());
            } else {
                latest.put(record.key(), record.value());
            }
        }
        return latest;
    }

    public <T> Map<String, T> readLatest(String topic, Class<T> type) {
        Map<String, T> typed = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        readLatest(topic).forEach((key, bytes) -> typed.put(key, JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(bytes, type)));
        return typed;
    }

    /** Every non-null value, in offset order within each partition. */
    public <T> List<T> readAll(String topic, Class<T> type) {
        return readAll(topic).stream()
                .filter(record -> record.value() != null)
                .map(record -> JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(record.value(), type))
                .toList();
    }

    private static boolean reachedEnds(KafkaConsumer<String, byte[]> consumer, Map<TopicPartition, Long> ends) {
        return ends.entrySet().stream().allMatch(e -> consumer.position(e.getKey()) >= e.getValue());
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/VerifierApplication.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationPropertiesScan;
import org.springframework.context.ConfigurableApplicationContext;

/** A one-shot command-line app: it runs {@link VerifierRunner}, then exits with the runner's status code. */
@SpringBootApplication
@ConfigurationPropertiesScan
public class VerifierApplication {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        ConfigurableApplicationContext context = SpringApplication.run(VerifierApplication.class, args);
        // SpringApplication.exit asks every ExitCodeGenerator bean, and VerifierRunner is one.
        System.exit(SpringApplication.exit(context));
    }
}
```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/VerifierProperties.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.time.Duration;

import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.ConfigurationProperties;
import org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.DefaultValue;

/**
 * Verifier tunables, bound from {@code telemetry.verifier.*}. Validated in the constructor like the other
 * property records, because Hibernate Validator has no {@code @Positive} for {@link Duration}.
 *
 * <p>DECISION: {@code settlePollInterval} (default 5 s, the detector's integrity emit interval) is not in the spec's
 * YAML. The spec says "re-reads every 5 s"; making it a property lets tests poll fast.
 */
@ConfigurationProperties("telemetry.verifier")
public record VerifierProperties(
        @DefaultValue("./reports") String reportsDir,
        @DefaultValue("30s") Duration readTimeout,
        @DefaultValue("5s") Duration settlePollInterval) {

    public VerifierProperties {
        if (reportsDir == null || reportsDir.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.verifier.reports-dir must not be blank");
        }
        positive(readTimeout, "read-timeout");
        positive(settlePollInterval, "settle-poll-interval");
    }

    private static void positive(Duration value, String name) {
        if (value == null || value.isZero() || value.isNegative()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("telemetry.verifier." + name + " must be positive");
        }
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/main/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/VerifierRunner.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.Topics;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.PlayerProfile;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments;
import org.springframework.boot.DefaultApplicationArguments;
import org.springframework.boot.CommandLineRunner;
import org.springframework.boot.ExitCodeGenerator;
import org.springframework.boot.convert.DurationStyle;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Runs one verifier mode and remembers the exit code, which {@link VerifierApplication} hands to the JVM.
 *
 * <pre>
 * --mode=detection [--min-recall=0.98] [--max-fpr=0.01]
 * --mode=integrity [--settle=60s]
 * </pre>
 *
 * <p>Exit codes: 0 success; 1 integrity failed; 2 detection missed a threshold given on the command line;
 * 3 the simulation has not finished (no {@code RunInfo}); 4 bad arguments.
 * DECISION: 4 is not in the spec, which only lists 0 to 3. Bad arguments need a code that does not look like a result.
 */
@Component
public class VerifierRunner implements CommandLineRunner, ExitCodeGenerator {

    static final int EXIT_OK = 0;
    static final int EXIT_INTEGRITY_FAILED = 1;
    static final int EXIT_THRESHOLD_MISSED = 2;
    static final int EXIT_NOT_FINISHED = 3;
    static final int EXIT_USAGE = 4;

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(VerifierRunner.class);

    private final TopicSnapshotReader reader;
    private final ReportWriter writer;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final Duration settlePollInterval;
    private volatile int exitCode = EXIT_OK;

    // @Autowired: with two constructors Spring cannot choose, and the package-private one exists for tests.
    @Autowired
    public VerifierRunner(TopicSnapshotReader reader, ReportWriter writer, VerifierProperties props) {
        this(reader, writer, props, Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    VerifierRunner(TopicSnapshotReader reader, ReportWriter writer, VerifierProperties props, Clock clock) {
        this.reader = reader;
        this.writer = writer;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.settlePollInterval = props.settlePollInterval();
    }

    @Override
    public int getExitCode() {
        return exitCode;
    }

    @Override
    public void run(String... args) {
        exitCode = execute(new DefaultApplicationArguments(args));
    }

    int execute(ApplicationArguments args) {
        String mode = single(args, "mode").orElse("");
        try {
            return switch (mode) {
                case "detection" -> detection(optionalDouble(args, "min-recall"), optionalDouble(args, "max-fpr"));
                case "integrity" -> integrity(single(args, "settle").map(VerifierRunner::duration).orElse(Duration.ZERO));
                default -> usage("--mode must be detection or integrity");
            };
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return usage(e.getMessage());
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            return usage("interrupted");
        }
    }

    private int detection(Optional<Double> minRecall, Optional<Double> maxFpr) {
        Optional<Manifest> manifest = readManifest();
        if (manifest.isEmpty()) {
            return notFinished();
        }
        RunInfo info = manifest.get().run();
        List<GroundTruth> truth = reader.readLatest(Topics.CHEATER_GROUND_TRUTH, GroundTruth.class).values().stream().toList();
        List<SuspiciousPlayer> flags = reader.readAll(Topics.SUSPICIOUS_PLAYERS, SuspiciousPlayer.class);
        Map<String, String> usernames = new HashMap<>();
        reader.readLatest(Topics.PLAYER_PROFILES, PlayerProfile.class)
                .forEach((id, profile) -> usernames.put(id, profile.username()));

        DetectionReport report = DetectionEvaluator.evaluate(info, truth, flags, usernames);
        publish("detection", info.runId(), writer.renderDetection(report));

        boolean missedRecall = minRecall.isPresent() && report.recall() < minRecall.get();
        boolean missedFpr = maxFpr.isPresent() && report.fpr() > maxFpr.get();
        if (missedRecall || missedFpr) {
            log.warn("Threshold missed: recall={} (min {}), fpr={} (max {})", report.recall(), minRecall.orElse(null),
                    report.fpr(), maxFpr.orElse(null));
            return EXIT_THRESHOLD_MISSED;
        }
        return EXIT_OK;
    }

    /**
     * Integrity stats are emitted every few seconds, so the last ones can arrive after the run ends. With
     * {@code --settle}, re-read until every player passes or the time is up, then report the final attempt.
     */
    private int integrity(Duration settle) throws InterruptedException {
        Optional<Manifest> read = readManifest();
        if (read.isEmpty()) {
            return notFinished();
        }
        RunInfo info = read.get().run();
        List<ManifestEntry> manifest = read.get().entries();

        long deadline = clock.millis() + settle.toMillis();
        IntegrityReport report;
        while (true) {
            Map<String, IntegrityStats> stats = reader.readLatest(Topics.INTEGRITY_STATS, IntegrityStats.class);
            report = IntegrityChecker.check(info, manifest, stats);
            if (report.passed() || clock.millis() >= deadline) {
                break;
            }
            log.info("Integrity not settled yet: {} of {} players pass", report.playersPassed(), report.playersChecked());
            Thread.sleep(settlePollInterval.toMillis());
        }
        publish("integrity", info.runId(), writer.renderIntegrity(report));
        return report.passed() ? EXIT_OK : EXIT_INTEGRITY_FAILED;
    }

    /** The run record and this run's entries, or empty when the simulator has not written {@code __run__} yet. */
    private Optional<Manifest> readManifest() {
        Map<String, byte[]> raw = reader.readLatest(Topics.SIMULATION_MANIFEST);
        byte[] runBytes = raw.get(Topics.RUN_KEY);
        if (runBytes == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        RunInfo run = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(runBytes, RunInfo.class);
        List<ManifestEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        raw.forEach((key, bytes) -> {
            if (!Topics.RUN_KEY.equals(key)) {
                ManifestEntry entry = JsonSerde.MAPPER.readValue(bytes, ManifestEntry.class);
                // Player ids repeat per seed, so an older run's entries can still be in the compacted topic.
                if (run.runId().equals(entry.runId())) {
                    entries.add(entry);
                }
            }
        });
        return Optional.of(new Manifest(run, entries));
    }

    private record Manifest(RunInfo run, List<ManifestEntry> entries) {
    }

    private int notFinished() {
        log.error("simulation has not finished");
        System.out.println("simulation has not finished");
        return EXIT_NOT_FINISHED;
    }

    private int usage(String problem) {
        log.error("{}. Usage: --mode=detection [--min-recall=0.98] [--max-fpr=0.01] | --mode=integrity [--settle=60s]",
                problem);
        return EXIT_USAGE;
    }

    private void publish(String mode, String runId, String markdown) {
        Path file = writer.write(mode, runId, markdown);
        System.out.println(markdown);
        log.info("Report written to {}", file.toAbsolutePath());
    }

    private static Optional<String> single(ApplicationArguments args, String name) {
        List<String> values = args.getOptionValues(name);
        return values == null || values.isEmpty() ? Optional.empty() : Optional.of(values.get(0));
    }

    private static Optional<Double> optionalDouble(ApplicationArguments args, String name) {
        return single(args, name).map(value -> {
            try {
                return Double.parseDouble(value);
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("--" + name + " must be a number, got " + value);
            }
        });
    }

    private static Duration duration(String value) {
        try {
            return DurationStyle.detectAndParse(value);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("--settle must be a duration such as 60s, got " + value);
        }
    }
}
```

`telemetry-verifier/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/DetectionEvaluatorTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.GroundTruth;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.SuspiciousPlayer;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class DetectionEvaluatorTest {

    private static final String RUN = "r-test01";
    private static final long START = 1_000_000L;
    private static final RunInfo RUN_INFO = new RunInfo(RUN, START, START + 300_000, 7, 0, 0, 0);

    private static GroundTruth cheater(String id, CheatType type, long startsAt) {
        return new GroundTruth(RUN, id, Segment.CHEATER, type, startsAt);
    }

    private static GroundTruth player(String id, Segment segment) {
        return new GroundTruth(RUN, id, segment, CheatType.NONE, null);
    }

    private static SuspiciousPlayer flag(String id, Rule rule, long detectedAt) {
        return new SuspiciousPlayer("f-" + id + "-" + detectedAt, id, null, null, rule, 0.9, detectedAt - 5000,
                detectedAt, detectedAt, Evidence.aimbot(15, 14, 13, 0.9286, 60.0), "1.0.0");
    }

    private static final Map<String, String> NAMES = Map.of("a1", "sim_a1", "a2", "sim_a2", "h1", "sim_h1");

    /**
     * Seven players: a1 caught quickly, a2 (subtle) missed, s1 caught but first by the wrong rule, s2 flagged early and
     * then caught, honest h1 wrongly flagged, honest h2 only flagged before the run, pro p1 never flagged.
     */
    private DetectionReport scenario() {
        long a1Start = START + 10_000;
        long s1Start = START + 10_000;
        long s2Start = START + 30_000;
        List<GroundTruth> truth = List.of(
                cheater("a1", CheatType.AIMBOT, a1Start),
                cheater("a2", CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, START + 20_000),
                cheater("s1", CheatType.SPEEDHACK, s1Start),
                cheater("s2", CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE, s2Start),
                player("h1", Segment.HONEST),
                player("h2", Segment.HONEST),
                player("p1", Segment.PRO));
        List<SuspiciousPlayer> flags = List.of(
                flag("a1", Rule.AIMBOT, a1Start + 3_000),
                flag("a1", Rule.AIMBOT, a1Start + 20_000),
                flag("s1", Rule.AIMBOT, s1Start + 7_000),
                flag("s1", Rule.SPEEDHACK, s1Start + 12_000),
                flag("s2", Rule.SPEEDHACK, s2Start - 5_000),
                flag("s2", Rule.SPEEDHACK, s2Start + 9_000),
                flag("h1", Rule.SPEEDHACK, START + 50_000),
                flag("h2", Rule.AIMBOT, START - 1),
                flag("nobody", Rule.AIMBOT, START + 40_000));
        return DetectionEvaluator.evaluate(RUN_INFO, truth, flags, NAMES);
    }

    @Test
    void countsPlayersBySegment() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        assertThat(r.cheaters()).isEqualTo(4);
        assertThat(r.honest()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(r.pro()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(r.runId()).isEqualTo(RUN);
    }

    @Test
    void recallPrecisionAndFalsePositiveRates() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        assertThat(r.detected()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(r.recall()).isEqualTo(0.75);
        assertThat(r.falsePositivesHonest()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(r.falsePositivesPro()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.falsePositiveCount()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(r.fpr()).isEqualTo(1.0 / 3);
        assertThat(r.fprHonest()).isEqualTo(0.5);
        assertThat(r.fprPro()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.precision()).isEqualTo(0.75);
    }

    @Test
    void ignoresFlagsBeforeTheRunAndFlagsForUnknownPlayers() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        // h2's only flag predates the run, and "nobody" is not in the ground truth.
        assertThat(r.falsePositives()).extracting(DetectionReport.FalsePositive::username).containsExactly("sim_h1");
        assertThat(r.flagsByRule()).containsEntry(Rule.AIMBOT, 3L).containsEntry(Rule.SPEEDHACK, 4L);
    }

    @Test
    void correctRuleLooksAtTheEarliestQualifyingFlagOnly() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        // a1 AIMBOT ok; s1's earliest qualifying flag is AIMBOT (wrong); s2's earliest qualifying is SPEEDHACK ok.
        assertThat(r.correctRule()).isEqualTo(2);
    }

    @Test
    void aFlagBeforeTheCheatStartedCountsAsEarlyButNotAsDetection() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        assertThat(r.earlyFlags()).isEqualTo(1);
        // s2 is still detected, by the flag after the cheat started, 9 s in.
        assertThat(r.latency().stream().filter(l -> l.cheatType() == CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE).findFirst().orElseThrow())
                .satisfies(l -> {
                    assertThat(l.detected()).isEqualTo(1);
                    assertThat(l.p50()).isEqualTo(9.0);
                });
    }

    @Test
    void latencyRowsPerCheatTypeAndOverall() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        assertThat(r.latency()).extracting(DetectionReport.LatencyRow::cheatType)
                .containsExactly(CheatType.AIMBOT, CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, CheatType.SPEEDHACK,
                        CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE, null);
        DetectionReport.LatencyRow aimbot = r.latency().get(0);
        assertThat(aimbot.count()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(aimbot.detected()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(aimbot.p50()).isEqualTo(3.0);
        DetectionReport.LatencyRow subtle = r.latency().get(1);
        assertThat(subtle.count()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(subtle.detected()).isZero();
        assertThat(subtle.p50()).isNull();
        assertThat(subtle.max()).isNull();
        DetectionReport.LatencyRow overall = r.latency().get(4);
        assertThat(overall.count()).isEqualTo(4);
        assertThat(overall.detected()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(overall.p50()).isEqualTo(7.0);
        assertThat(overall.p95()).isEqualTo(9.0);
        assertThat(overall.max()).isEqualTo(9.0);
    }

    @Test
    void listsMissedCheatersWithTheirUsername() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        assertThat(r.missed()).containsExactly(
                new DetectionReport.Missed("sim_a2", CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, START + 20_000));
    }

    @Test
    void falsePositivesCarryTheEvidenceOfTheirFirstFlag() {
        DetectionReport r = scenario();

        DetectionReport.FalsePositive fp = r.falsePositives().get(0);
        assertThat(fp.segment()).isEqualTo(Segment.HONEST);
        assertThat(fp.rule()).isEqualTo(Rule.SPEEDHACK);
        assertThat(fp.score()).isEqualTo(0.9);
        assertThat(fp.evidence()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test
    void aProFlaggedIsAProFalsePositive() {
        DetectionReport r = DetectionEvaluator.evaluate(RUN_INFO,
                List.of(player("p1", Segment.PRO), player("h1", Segment.HONEST)),
                List.of(flag("p1", Rule.AIMBOT, START + 1000)), Map.of());

        assertThat(r.falsePositivesPro()).isEqualTo(1);
        assertThat(r.fprPro()).isEqualTo(1.0);
        assertThat(r.fprHonest()).isZero();
        // With no username known, the player id is shown.
        assertThat(r.falsePositives().get(0).username()).isEqualTo("p1");
    }

    @Test
    void groundTruthOfAnotherRunIsIgnored() {
        GroundTruth stale = new GroundTruth("r-old", "a1", Segment.CHEATER, CheatType.AIMBOT, START);

        DetectionReport r = DetectionEvaluator.evaluate(RUN_INFO, List.of(stale), List.of(), Map.of());

        assertThat(r.cheaters()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    void undefinedRatiosAreZeroNotNaN() {
        DetectionReport r = DetectionEvaluator.evaluate(RUN_INFO, List.of(), List.of(), Map.of());

        assertThat(r.recall()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.fpr()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.precision()).isZero();
        assertThat(r.latency()).hasSize(5);
    }

    @Test
    void nearestRankPercentiles() {
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(List.of(), 50)).isNull();
        // n = 1: every percentile is the one value.
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(List.of(4.0), 50)).isEqualTo(4.0);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(List.of(4.0), 95)).isEqualTo(4.0);
        // n = 2: p50 is the first value (ceil(1) - 1 = 0), p95 the second (ceil(1.9) - 1 = 1).
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(List.of(8.0, 2.0), 50)).isEqualTo(2.0);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(List.of(8.0, 2.0), 95)).isEqualTo(8.0);
        // n = 10: p50 is the 5th value, p95 the 10th (ceil(9.5) - 1 = 9).
        List<Double> ten = List.of(10.0, 9.0, 8.0, 7.0, 6.0, 5.0, 4.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(ten, 50)).isEqualTo(5.0);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(ten, 95)).isEqualTo(10.0);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.percentile(ten, 100)).isEqualTo(10.0);
    }

    @Test
    void cheatFamiliesMatchTheRules() {
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.familyOf(CheatType.AIMBOT)).isEqualTo(Rule.AIMBOT);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.familyOf(CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE)).isEqualTo(Rule.AIMBOT);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.familyOf(CheatType.SPEEDHACK)).isEqualTo(Rule.SPEEDHACK);
        assertThat(DetectionEvaluator.familyOf(CheatType.SPEEDHACK_SUBTLE)).isEqualTo(Rule.SPEEDHACK);
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> DetectionEvaluator.familyOf(CheatType.NONE)).isInstanceOf(IllegalArgumentException.class);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/IntegrityCheckerTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.RunInfo;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class IntegrityCheckerTest {

    private static final RunInfo RUN = new RunInfo("r-1", 1000, 2000, 2, 0, 0, 0);

    private static ManifestEntry entry(String id, long finalSeq, long failed) {
        return new ManifestEntry("r-1", id, finalSeq, finalSeq - failed, failed);
    }

    private static IntegrityStats stats(String id, long count, long lastSeq, long duplicates, long gaps) {
        return new IntegrityStats(id, count, lastSeq, duplicates, gaps, 5000);
    }

    private static IntegrityReport.Failure onlyFailure(IntegrityReport report) {
        assertThat(report.failures()).hasSize(1);
        return report.failures().get(0);
    }

    @Test
    void everyPlayerPassesWhenTheCountsMatchExactly() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN,
                List.of(entry("a", 100, 0), entry("b", 50, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 100, 100, 0, 0), "b", stats("b", 50, 50, 0, 0)));

        assertThat(report.passed()).isTrue();
        assertThat(report.playersChecked()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(report.playersPassed()).isEqualTo(2);
        assertThat(report.eventsExpected()).isEqualTo(150);
        assertThat(report.eventsCounted()).isEqualTo(150);
        assertThat(report.duplicates()).isZero();
        assertThat(report.gaps()).isZero();
        assertThat(report.failures()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void duplicatesFailAPlayer() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 100, 100, 3, 0)));

        assertThat(report.passed()).isFalse();
        assertThat(report.duplicates()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly("duplicates=3");
    }

    @Test
    void gapsFailAPlayer() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 100, 100, 0, 4)));

        assertThat(report.gaps()).isEqualTo(4);
        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly("gaps=4");
    }

    @Test
    void aWrongLastSeqFailsAPlayer() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 100, 99, 0, 0)));

        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly("lastSeq=99 expected 100");
    }

    @Test
    void aWrongCountFailsAPlayer() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 98, 100, 0, 0)));

        assertThat(report.eventsCounted()).isEqualTo(98);
        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly("count=98 expected 100");
    }

    @Test
    void failedSendsInTheManifestFailAPlayerEvenWhenTheStatsLookRight() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 2)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 100, 100, 0, 0)));

        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly("simulator reported failed sends=2");
    }

    @Test
    void aPlayerWithoutStatsFailsAndIsCounted() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)), Map.of());

        assertThat(report.playersWithoutStats()).isEqualTo(1);
        IntegrityReport.Failure failure = onlyFailure(report);
        assertThat(failure.stats()).isNull();
        assertThat(failure.reasons()).containsExactly("no integrity stats");
    }

    @Test
    void severalReasonsAreAllListed() {
        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(entry("a", 100, 0)),
                Map.of("a", stats("a", 97, 97, 2, 5)));

        assertThat(onlyFailure(report).reasons()).containsExactly(
                "duplicates=2", "gaps=5", "lastSeq=97 expected 100", "count=97 expected 100");
    }

    @Test
    void listsAtMostTwentyFailuresButCountsThemAll() {
        List<ManifestEntry> manifest = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            manifest.add(entry("p%02d".formatted(i), 10, 0));
        }

        IntegrityReport report = IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, manifest, new HashMap<>());

        assertThat(report.playersChecked()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(report.playersPassed()).isZero();
        assertThat(report.failures()).hasSize(IntegrityChecker.MAX_LISTED_FAILURES);
    }

    @Test
    void anEmptyManifestIsNotAPass() {
        assertThat(IntegrityChecker.check(RUN, List.of(), Map.of()).passed()).isFalse();
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/ReportWriterTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.CheatType;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Evidence;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.ManifestEntry;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Rule;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.Segment;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class ReportWriterTest {

    @TempDir
    Path dir;

    private ReportWriter writer() {
        return new ReportWriter(new VerifierProperties(dir.resolve("reports").toString(), Duration.ofSeconds(1),
                Duration.ofSeconds(1)));
    }

    private DetectionReport detection(boolean withProblems) {
        Map<Rule, Long> byRule = new EnumMap<>(Rule.class);
        byRule.put(Rule.AIMBOT, 3L);
        return new DetectionReport("r-abc123", 1_763_372_100_000L, 1_763_372_400_000L, 50, 930, 20, 49, 48, 2, 0, 1,
                0.98, 0.0105, 0.0, 0.05, 0.98, byRule,
                List.of(new DetectionReport.LatencyRow(CheatType.AIMBOT, 20, 20, 2.0, 4.0, 5.0),
                        new DetectionReport.LatencyRow(CheatType.SPEEDHACK, 12, 0, null, null, null),
                        new DetectionReport.LatencyRow(null, 32, 20, 2.0, 4.0, 5.0)),
                withProblems ? List.of(new DetectionReport.Missed("sim_0007", CheatType.AIMBOT_SUBTLE, 1_763_372_150_000L))
                        : List.of(),
                withProblems ? List.of(
                        new DetectionReport.FalsePositive("sim_0042", Segment.PRO, Rule.AIMBOT, 0.7,
                                Evidence.aimbot(14, 12, 9, 0.75, 130.0)),
                        new DetectionReport.FalsePositive("sim_0043", Segment.HONEST, Rule.SPEEDHACK, 0.6,
                                Evidence.speedhack(45, 8, 0.1778, 7.9)),
                        new DetectionReport.FalsePositive("sim_0044", Segment.HONEST, Rule.SPEEDHACK, 0.6, null))
                        : List.of());
    }

    @Test
    void detectionReportStartsWithRunIdTimesAndMode() {
        String md = writer().renderDetection(detection(false));

        assertThat(md).startsWith("# Telemetry verifier: detection");
        assertThat(md).contains("- Run id: r-abc123", "- Started: 2025-11-17T", "- Mode: detection");
    }

    @Test
    void detectionReportShowsTheSummaryAndLatencyTables() {
        String md = writer().renderDetection(detection(false));

        assertThat(md).contains("| Recall | 98.00% |", "| False-positive rate (honest + pro) | 1.05% |",
                "| False-positive rate (pro only) | 5.00% of 20 |", "| Flags, rule AIMBOT | 3 |",
                "| Flags, rule SPEEDHACK | 0 |");
        assertThat(md).contains("| AIMBOT | 20 | 20 | 2.0 | 4.0 | 5.0 |", "| SPEEDHACK | 12 | 0 | - | - | - |",
                "| **ALL** | 32 | 20 | 2.0 | 4.0 | 5.0 |");
        assertThat(md).contains("## Missed cheaters\n\nNone.", "## False positives\n\nNone.");
    }

    @Test
    void detectionReportListsMissedCheatersAndFalsePositivesWithEvidence() {
        String md = writer().renderDetection(detection(true));

        assertThat(md).contains("| sim_0007 | AIMBOT_SUBTLE | 2025-11-17T");
        assertThat(md).contains("| sim_0042 | PRO | AIMBOT | 0.700 | shots=14 hits=12 headshotHits=9 headshotRate=0.75 avgReactionMs=130.0 |");
        assertThat(md).contains("movingSamples=45 overspeedSamples=8 overspeedRatio=0.1778 maxSpeed=7.9");
        assertThat(md).contains("| sim_0044 | HONEST | SPEEDHACK | 0.600 | - |");
    }

    @Test
    void integrityReportShowsPassAndTheCounters() {
        IntegrityReport report = new IntegrityReport("r-abc123", 1000, 2000, 1000, 1000, 7_500_000, 7_500_000, 0, 0, 0,
                List.of());

        String md = writer().renderIntegrity(report);

        assertThat(md).contains("# Telemetry verifier: integrity", "## Result: PASS", "| Players passed | 1000 |",
                "| Events expected (sum of finalSeq) | 7500000 |", "| Duplicates | 0 |", "## Failing players\n\nNone.");
    }

    @Test
    void integrityReportListsFailuresSideBySide() {
        ManifestEntry manifest = new ManifestEntry("r-abc123", "p1", 100, 100, 0);
        IntegrityReport report = new IntegrityReport("r-abc123", 1000, 2000, 3, 1, 300, 297, 4, 1, 1, List.of(
                new IntegrityReport.Failure(manifest, new IntegrityStats("p1", 97, 100, 4, 1, 5), List.of("duplicates=4")),
                new IntegrityReport.Failure(new ManifestEntry("r-abc123", "p2", 100, 100, 0), null,
                        List.of("no integrity stats"))));

        String md = writer().renderIntegrity(report);

        assertThat(md).contains("## Result: FAIL", "## Failing players (2 of 2 shown)",
                "| p1 | finalSeq=100 acked=100 failed=0 | count=97 lastSeq=100 duplicates=4 gaps=1 | duplicates=4 |",
                "| p2 | finalSeq=100 acked=100 failed=0 | none | no integrity stats |");
    }

    @Test
    void writesTheFileUnderTheReportsDirectoryCreatingIt() throws IOException {
        Path file = writer().write("detection", "r-abc123", "hello");

        assertThat(file).isEqualTo(dir.resolve("reports").resolve("detection-r-abc123.md"));
        assertThat(Files.readString(file)).isEqualTo("hello");
    }

    @Test
    void reportsAnUnwritableDirectoryAsAnError() throws IOException {
        Path blocker = Files.writeString(dir.resolve("file"), "not a directory");
        ReportWriter writer = new ReportWriter(new VerifierProperties(blocker.toString(), Duration.ofSeconds(1),
                Duration.ofSeconds(1)));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> writer.write("detection", "r-1", "x")).isInstanceOf(java.io.UncheckedIOException.class);
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/TopicSnapshotReaderIT.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.UUID;

import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.json.JsonSerde;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.model.IntegrityStats;
import dev.fairplay.telemetry.common.support.KafkaTestContainer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.admin.AdminClient;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.admin.NewTopic;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerConfig;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.ByteArraySerializer;
import org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

/** The reader against a real broker: latest value per key, tombstones, read_committed and the end-offset stop. */
class TopicSnapshotReaderIT {

    private static final String BOOTSTRAP = KafkaTestContainer.KAFKA.getBootstrapServers();

    private final TopicSnapshotReader reader = new TopicSnapshotReader(BOOTSTRAP,
            new VerifierProperties("./reports", Duration.ofSeconds(20), Duration.ofSeconds(1)));

    private static String newTopic(int partitions) throws Exception {
        String topic = "it-" + UUID.randomUUID();
        try (AdminClient admin = AdminClient.create(Map.of("bootstrap.servers", BOOTSTRAP))) {
            admin.createTopics(List.of(new NewTopic(topic, partitions, (short) 1))).all().get();
        }
        return topic;
    }

    private static Properties producerProps() {
        Properties p = new Properties();
        p.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, BOOTSTRAP);
        p.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class.getName());
        p.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, ByteArraySerializer.class.getName());
        return p;
    }

    private static byte[] bytes(String s) {
        return s.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    @Test
    void keepsTheLatestValuePerKeyAndTombstonesRemoveKeys() throws Exception {
        String topic = newTopic(3);
        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = new KafkaProducer<>(producerProps())) {
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "k1", bytes("v1")));
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "k2", bytes("v2")));
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "k1", bytes("v3")));
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "k2", null));
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "k3", bytes("v4")));
            producer.flush();
        }

        Map<String, byte[]> latest = reader.readLatest(topic);

        assertThat(latest).containsOnlyKeys("k1", "k3");
        assertThat(new String(latest.get("k1"), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).isEqualTo("v3");
        assertThat(reader.readAll(topic)).hasSize(5);
    }

    @Test
    void readsTypedValuesAndSkipsNullOnes() throws Exception {
        String topic = newTopic(1);
        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = new KafkaProducer<>(producerProps())) {
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "p1",
                    JsonSerde.MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(new IntegrityStats("p1", 5, 5, 0, 0, 1))));
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "p2", null));
            producer.flush();
        }

        assertThat(reader.readLatest(topic, IntegrityStats.class)).containsOnlyKeys("p1");
        assertThat(reader.readAll(topic, IntegrityStats.class)).extracting(IntegrityStats::count).containsExactly(5L);
    }

    @Test
    void returnsWhenItReachesTheEndOffsetsOfAnEmptyTopic() throws Exception {
        String topic = newTopic(2);
        long before = System.nanoTime();

        assertThat(reader.readAll(topic)).isEmpty();
        assertThat(Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - before)).isLessThan(Duration.ofSeconds(15));
    }

    @Test
    void aTopicThatDoesNotExistGivesNothing() {
        // The broker may auto-create it on the metadata request; either way there is nothing in it.
        assertThat(reader.readAll("it-missing-" + UUID.randomUUID())).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    void neverShowsRecordsOfAnAbortedTransaction() throws Exception {
        String topic = newTopic(1);
        Properties props = producerProps();
        props.put(ProducerConfig.TRANSACTIONAL_ID_CONFIG, "it-tx-" + UUID.randomUUID());
        try (KafkaProducer<String, byte[]> producer = new KafkaProducer<>(props)) {
            producer.initTransactions();
            producer.beginTransaction();
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "aborted", bytes("x")));
            producer.flush();
            producer.abortTransaction();
            producer.beginTransaction();
            producer.send(new ProducerRecord<>(topic, "committed", bytes("y")));
            producer.commitTransaction();
        }

        assertThat(reader.readLatest(topic)).containsOnlyKeys("committed");
    }
}

```

`telemetry-verifier/src/test/java/dev/fairplay/telemetry/verifier/VerifierContextTest.java`:

```java
package dev.fairplay.telemetry.verifier;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;

/**
 * The whole application context, the way the jar starts it. It needs no broker: the reader connects only when a
 * mode reads a topic, and a bad {@code --mode} stops before that. This is the test that catches wiring mistakes
 * (for example a bean with two constructors) which the plain unit tests, building classes by hand, cannot see.
 */
@SpringBootTest(args = "--mode=nonsense", properties = "telemetry.verifier.reports-dir=target/test-reports")
class VerifierContextTest {

    @Autowired
    VerifierRunner runner;

    @Test
    void theContextStartsAndTheRunnerRanWithTheCommandLineArguments() {
        assertThat(runner.getExitCode()).isEqualTo(VerifierRunner.EXIT_USAGE);
    }
}
```
